function coreCreateMaster(type,data){let map={supplier:'suppliers',customer:'customers',product:'products',warehouse:'warehouses',rack:'racks',machine:'machines',bankAccount:'bankAccounts'},k=map[type],a=state[k]||(state[k]=[]);if(!data.id||!data.code||!data.name&&type!=='bankAccount')return coreResult(false,'Kodi dhe emri janë të detyrueshëm');if(!coreUnique(a,'code',data.code))return coreResult(false,'Kodi ekziston');a.push({...data});return coreResult(true,'',data)}
function coreCreateWeighing(data){if(!data.id||!data.supplier||!data.product||!data.weighingWarehouse)return coreResult(false,'Peshimi kërkon furnitor, produkt dhe magazinë');if(state.weighings.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(false,'Peshimi ekziston');let rows=data.bagRows||[];if(!rows.length||rows.some(r=>!(+r.gross>0)||+r.tare<0||+r.gross<+r.tare))return coreResult(false,'Rresht peshimi i pavlefshëm');let gross=rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(+r.gross||0),0),tare=rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(+r.tare||0),0),net=gross-tare,discount=+data.discount||0,x={...data,gross,tare,net,physical:net,billable:net*(1-discount/100),bags:rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(+r.bags||0),0),status:data.status||'Draft'};state.weighings.push(x);return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreConfirmWeighing(id,lotData){let w=by('weighings',id);if(!w?.id)return coreResult(false,'Peshimi nuk u gjet');if(!(w.gross>0)||!((+w.net||0)>0))return coreResult(false,'Peshimi ka peshë të pavlefshme (bruto/tara) dhe nuk konfirmohet');if(w.status==='Anuluar')return coreResult(false,'Peshimi i anuluar nuk mund të konfirmohet');let existing=state.lots.find(l=>l.weighing===id);if(existing){w.status='Konfirmuar';return coreResult(true,'',existing)};let lot={...lotData,weighing:id,gross:w.gross,tare:w.tare,net:w.net,originalNet:w.net,availableNet:w.net,bags:w.bags,date:w.date,status:'Në magazinim'};state.lots.push(lot);w.status='Konfirmuar';coreMove({key:'WEIGH:'+id,type:'IN',source:'weighing',sourceId:id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:w.net,unitCost:+lot.unitCost||0,date:w.date,testRunId:w.testRunId});return coreResult(true,'',lot)}
function coreCreatePurchase(data,confirm=false){
  if(!data.id||!data.supplier||!by('suppliers',data.supplier).id)return coreResult(false,'Fatura kërkon furnitor të vlefshëm');
  if(data.weighing&&!state.weighings.some(w=>w.id===data.weighing&&w.status==='Konfirmuar'))return coreResult(false,'Peshimi duhet të jetë i konfirmuar');
  if(data.supplierInvoiceNo&& !purchaseScopeUnique(data.supplier,data.supplierInvoiceNo,data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i faturës ekziston për këtë furnitor');
  const pre=!!data.isPrepayment,qty=+data.billable,price=+data.price,vat=+data.vat||0,rate=+data.exchangeRate;
  if(!Number.isFinite(rate)||rate<=0)return coreResult(false,'Kursi duhet të jetë pozitiv');
  if(!Number.isFinite(vat)||vat<0||vat>100)return coreResult(false,'TVSH duhet të jetë midis 0 dhe 100%');
  if(pre){if(!Number.isFinite(+data.total)||!(+data.total>0))return coreResult(false,'Shuma e parapagimit duhet pozitive');}
  else if(!Number.isFinite(qty)||!(qty>0)||!Number.isFinite(price)||price<0||(confirm&&price<=0))return coreResult(false,'Plotësoni sasinë dhe çmimin pozitiv për konfirmim');
  let f=purchaseInvoices().find(x=>x.id===data.id);
  if(f?.status==='Anuluar')return coreResult(false,'Fatura e anuluar nuk konfirmohet');
  if(f?.status==='Konfirmuar')return coreResult(false,'Fatura është konfirmuar më parë — përdorni korrigjimin');
  if(!f){f={...data,status:'Draft'};purchaseInvoices().push(f)}else Object.assign(f,data);
  if(pre){f.billable=0;f.physical=0;f.price=0;f.vat=0;f.subtotal=0;f.vatAmount=0;f.total=+data.total;}
  else{f.subtotal=qty*price;f.vatAmount=f.subtotal*vat/100;f.total=f.subtotal+f.vatAmount;}
  f.status=confirm?'Konfirmuar':'Draft';return coreResult(true,'',f);
}
function coreSupplierPayment(data){if(!data.supplier||!(data.amount>0))return coreResult(false,'Furnitori dhe shuma pozitive janë të detyrueshme');if(state.payments.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i mandatit ekziston');state.payments.push({...data,status:data.status||'Konfirmuar'});return coreResult(true,'',data)}
function coreProcess(data){let lot=by('lots',data.sourceLot);if(!lot?.id)return coreResult(false,'Loti burimor mungon');if(state.processes.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(true,'',state.processes.find(x=>x.id===data.id));if(!(data.input>0)||!(data.output>0)||data.input>lotAvail(lot)+.001||data.output>data.input&&!data.authorization)return coreResult(false,'Sasi procesi e pavlefshme ose mbi stok');let inputCost=+lot.unitCost||0,totalCost=data.input*inputCost,unitCost=totalCost/data.output,p={...data,lots:[data.sourceLot],input:data.input,output:data.output,loss:data.input-data.output,yield:data.output/data.input*100,status:'Përfunduar',unitCost,totalCost};state.processes.push(p);lot.availableNet=Math.max(0,lotAvail(lot)-data.input);lot.net=lot.availableNet;let out={id:data.outputLot,code:data.outputLotCode,parentLot:lot.id,sourceLots:[lot.id],process:p.id,supplier:lot.supplier,product:data.product||lot.product,warehouse:data.warehouse||lot.warehouse,rack:data.rack||lot.rack,net:data.output,originalNet:data.output,availableNet:data.output,unpackedNet:data.output,packedNet:0,unitCost,status:'Produkt përfundimtar',date:data.date,testRunId:data.testRunId};state.lots.push(out);coreMove({key:'PROCESS-OUT:'+p.id,type:'OUT',source:'process',sourceId:p.id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:-data.input,unitCost:inputCost,date:data.date,testRunId:data.testRunId});coreMove({key:'PROCESS-IN:'+p.id,type:'IN',source:'process',sourceId:p.id,lot:out.id,product:out.product,warehouse:out.warehouse,qty:data.output,unitCost,date:data.date,testRunId:data.testRunId});return coreResult(true,'',p)}
function corePackage(data){let net=(+data.bags||0)*(+data.bagWeight||0);if(!data.order||!data.product||!data.internalLot)return coreResult(false,'Të dhënat e paketimit mungojnë');if(packs().some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(true,'',packs().find(x=>x.id===data.id));let raw=(data.sources&&data.sources.length)?data.sources:(data.sourceLot?[{lot:data.sourceLot,qty:net}]:[]);if(!raw.length)return coreResult(false,'Zgjidhni të paktën një lot burimor');let sources=[],seen=new Set();for(let z of raw){let lot=by('lots',z.lot??z.lotId??z.id),qty=+z.qty;if(!lot||!lot.id)return coreResult(false,'Loti burimor mungon');if(seen.has(lot.id))return coreResult(false,'Loti '+lot.code+' është shtuar dy herë');seen.add(lot.id);if(!(qty>0))return coreResult(false,'Sasia për lotin '+lot.code+' është e pavlefshme');if(qty>lotUnpacked(lot)+.001)return coreResult(false,'Paketimi tejkalon stokun e papaketuar të lotit '+lot.code+' (të lira '+fmt2(lotUnpacked(lot))+' kg)');if(lot.product!==data.product)return coreResult(false,'Loti '+lot.code+' është i produktit tjetër — përzieni vetëm lotë të së njëjtit produkt');sources.push({lot,qty})}if(!(net>0))return coreResult(false,'Pesha e paketimit është e pavlefshme');let sum=sources.reduce((a,z)=>a+z.qty,0);if(Math.abs(sum-net)>.01)return coreResult(false,'Sasia nga lotet ('+fmt2(sum)+' kg) duhet të jetë '+fmt2(net)+' kg');let ids=sources.map(z=>z.lot.id),qtys={};sources.forEach(z=>qtys[z.lot.id]=Math.round(z.qty*1000)/1000);let x={...data,sourceLots:ids,sourceLotQtys:qtys,net,qty:net,status:data.status||'Paketuar'};delete x.sourceLot;delete x.sources;packs().push(x);sources.forEach(z=>{z.lot.unpackedNet=lotUnpacked(z.lot)-z.qty;z.lot.packedNet=(+z.lot.packedNet||0)+z.qty;coreMove({key:(sources.length===1?'PACK:'+x.id:'PACK:'+x.id+':'+z.lot.id),type:'TRANSFER',source:'packaging',sourceId:x.id,lot:z.lot.id,product:z.lot.product,warehouse:z.lot.warehouse,qty:0,fromState:'unpacked',toState:'packed',stateQty:z.qty,unitCost:z.lot.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId})});return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreCreateSale(data,confirm=false){if(!data.id||!data.invoiceNumber||!data.customer||!(data.lines||[]).length)return coreResult(false,'Fatura e shitjes nuk është e plotë');if(!coreUnique(salesInvoices(),'invoiceNumber',data.invoiceNumber,data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i faturës ekziston');if(data.lines.some(l=>!l.product||!(l.net>0)||l.price<0))return coreResult(false,'Rresht shitjeje i pavlefshëm');let f=salesInvoices().find(x=>x.id===data.id);if(!f){f={...data,status:'Draft'};salesInvoices().push(f)}else Object.assign(f,data);f.subtotal=f.lines.reduce((a,l)=>a+l.net*l.price*(1-(+l.discount||0)/100),0);f.vatAmount=f.subtotal*(+f.vat||0)/100;f.total=f.subtotal+f.vatAmount+(+f.palletCost||0)+(+f.loadingCost||0);f.totalALL=f.total*(f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||1));f.status=confirm?'Konfirmuar':'Draft';return coreResult(true,'',f)}
function coreCustomerReceipt(data){if(!data.customer||!(data.amount>0))return coreResult(false,'Klienti dhe shuma pozitive janë të detyrueshme');if(customerPayments().some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i mandatit ekziston');customerPayments().push({...data,status:data.status||'Konfirmuar'});return coreResult(true,'',data)}
function coreCreateShipment(data){if(state.shipments.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(true,'',by('shipments',data.id));let x={...data,status:'Planifikuar',stockDeducted:false};state.shipments.push(x);return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreAdvanceShipment(id,next){let x=by('shipments',id),cur=normalizedShipmentStatus(x),expected=shipmentStages[shipmentStages.indexOf(cur)+1];if(!x?.id||next!==expected)return coreResult(false,'Kalimi i statusit nuk është në rend');let missing=shipmentRequirements(x,next);if(missing.length)return coreResult(false,'Mungon: '+missing.join(', '));if(next==='Nisur'&&!x.stockDeducted){let allocations=packs().filter(p=>p.order===x.order),left=+x.net||0,lotIds=[...new Set(allocations.flatMap(pk=>pk.sourceLots||[]))],totalPacked=lotIds.reduce((a,lid)=>a+(+by('lots',lid)?.packedNet||0),0);if(totalPacked+.001<left)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');for(let lid of lotIds){let lot=by('lots',lid),take=Math.min(left,+lot?.packedNet||0);if(take){lot.packedNet-=take;lot.availableNet=Math.max(0,lotAvail(lot)-take);lot.net=lot.availableNet;coreMove({key:'SHIP:'+id+':'+lot.id,type:'OUT',source:'shipment',sourceId:id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:-take,unitCost:lot.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId});left-=take}}if(left>.001)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');x.stockDeducted=true;x.stockDeductedAt=new Date().toISOString()}x.status=next;return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreAccountingSync(runId=''){let a=ensureAccounting(),set=a.settings,created=0,tag=x=>!runId||x.testRunId===runId;function post(key,date,journal,desc,ref,lines,testRunId){let bare=String(key).replace(/^core:/,'');if(a.entries.some(e=>e.sourceKey===key||e.sourceKey===bare))return;let d=lines.reduce((z,l)=>z+l.debit,0),c=lines.reduce((z,l)=>z+l.credit,0);a.entries.push({id:uid('JE'),number:'VK-'+new Date().getFullYear()+'-'+String(a.entries.length+1).padStart(5,'0'),sourceKey:key,sourceRef:ref,date,journal,description:desc,lines,status:Math.abs(d-c)<.005?'Postuar':'Gabim',testRunId});created++}purchaseInvoices().filter(f=>tag(f)&&f.status==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let v=f.total*(f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||1));post('core:purchase:'+f.id,f.invoiceDate,'J-PUR','Blerje '+f.id,f.id,[{account:set.purchaseExpense||'311',debit:v,credit:0},{account:set.purchasePayable||'401',debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});state.payments.filter(f=>tag(f)&&(f.status||'Konfirmuar')==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let rate=f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||+f.rate||1);let v=(+f.amount||0)*rate;let cash=/cash|ark/i.test(f.method||'');post('core:payment:'+f.id,f.date,cash?'J-CASH':'J-BANK','Pagesë '+f.id,f.id,[{account:set.purchasePayable||'401',debit:v,credit:0},{account:cash?(set.cash||'530'):(set.bank||'512'),debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});salesInvoices().filter(f=>tag(f)&&f.status==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let rate=f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||1),rev=((+f.subtotal||0)+(+f.palletCost||0)+(+f.loadingCost||0))*rate,vat=(+f.vatAmount||0)*rate,gross=(+f.total||0)*rate,lines=[{account:set.salesReceivable||'411',debit:gross,credit:0},{account:set.salesRevenue||'700',debit:0,credit:rev}];if(vat)lines.push({account:set.salesVat||'445',debit:0,credit:vat});post('core:sale:'+f.id,f.date,'J-SALE','Shitje '+f.id,f.id,lines,f.testRunId)});customerPayments().filter(f=>tag(f)&&(f.status||'Konfirmuar')==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let rate=f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.rate||+f.exchangeRate||1);let v=(+f.amount||0)*rate;let cash=/cash|ark/i.test(f.method||'');post('core:receipt:'+f.id,f.date,cash?'J-CASH':'J-BANK','Arkëtim '+f.id,f.id,[{account:cash?(set.cash||'530'):(set.bank||'512'),debit:v,credit:0},{account:set.salesReceivable||'411',debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});state.shipments.filter(f=>tag(f)&&f.stockDeducted).forEach(f=>{let v=stockMoves().filter(m=>m.source==='shipment'&&m.sourceId===f.id).reduce((z,m)=>z+Math.abs(m.qty)*m.unitCost,0);post('core:cogs:'+f.id,f.date,'J-GEN','KMSH '+f.id,f.id,[{account:set.cogs||'603',debit:v,credit:0},{account:set.inventory||'311',debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});return created}

function coreCreateMaster(type,data){let map={supplier:'suppliers',customer:'customers',product:'products',warehouse:'warehouses',rack:'racks',machine:'machines',bankAccount:'bankAccounts'},k=map[type],a=state[k]||(state[k]=[]);if(!data.id||!data.code||!data.name&&type!=='bankAccount')return coreResult(false,'Kodi dhe emri janë të detyrueshëm');if(!coreUnique(a,'code',data.code))return coreResult(false,'Kodi ekziston');a.push({...data});return coreResult(true,'',data)}
function coreCreateWeighing(data){if(!data.id||!data.supplier||!data.product||!data.weighingWarehouse)return coreResult(false,'Peshimi kërkon furnitor, produkt dhe magazinë');if(state.weighings.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(false,'Peshimi ekziston');let rows=data.bagRows||[];if(!rows.length||rows.some(r=>!(+r.gross>0)||+r.tare<0||+r.gross<+r.tare))return coreResult(false,'Rresht peshimi i pavlefshëm');let gross=rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(+r.gross||0),0),tare=rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(+r.tare||0),0),net=gross-tare,discount=+data.discount||0,x={...data,gross,tare,net,physical:net,billable:net*(1-discount/100),bags:rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(+r.bags||0),0),status:data.status||'Draft'};state.weighings.push(x);return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreConfirmWeighing(id,lotData){let w=by('weighings',id);if(!w?.id)return coreResult(false,'Peshimi nuk u gjet');if(!(w.gross>0)||!((+w.net||0)>0))return coreResult(false,'Peshimi ka peshë të pavlefshme (bruto/tara) dhe nuk konfirmohet');if(w.status==='Anuluar')return coreResult(false,'Peshimi i anuluar nuk mund të konfirmohet');let existing=state.lots.find(l=>l.weighing===id);if(existing){w.status='Konfirmuar';return coreResult(true,'',existing)};let lot={...lotData,weighing:id,gross:w.gross,tare:w.tare,net:w.net,originalNet:w.net,availableNet:w.net,bags:w.bags,date:w.date,status:'Në magazinim'};state.lots.push(lot);w.status='Konfirmuar';coreMove({key:'WEIGH:'+id,type:'IN',source:'weighing',sourceId:id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:w.net,unitCost:+lot.unitCost||0,date:w.date,testRunId:w.testRunId});return coreResult(true,'',lot)}
function coreCreatePurchase(data,confirm=false){
  if(!data.id||!data.supplier||!by('suppliers',data.supplier).id)return coreResult(false,'Fatura kërkon furnitor të vlefshëm');
  if(data.weighing&&!state.weighings.some(w=>w.id===data.weighing&&w.status==='Konfirmuar'))return coreResult(false,'Peshimi duhet të jetë i konfirmuar');
  if(data.supplierInvoiceNo&& !purchaseScopeUnique(data.supplier,data.supplierInvoiceNo,data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i faturës ekziston për këtë furnitor');
  const pre=!!data.isPrepayment,qty=+data.billable,price=+data.price,vat=+data.vat||0,rate=+data.exchangeRate;
  if(!Number.isFinite(rate)||rate<=0)return coreResult(false,'Kursi duhet të jetë pozitiv');
  if(!Number.isFinite(vat)||vat<0||vat>100)return coreResult(false,'TVSH duhet të jetë midis 0 dhe 100%');
  if(pre){if(!Number.isFinite(+data.total)||!(+data.total>0))return coreResult(false,'Shuma e parapagimit duhet pozitive');}
  else if(!Number.isFinite(qty)||!(qty>0)||!Number.isFinite(price)||price<0||(confirm&&price<=0))return coreResult(false,'Plotësoni sasinë dhe çmimin pozitiv për konfirmim');
  let f=purchaseInvoices().find(x=>x.id===data.id);
  if(f?.status==='Anuluar')return coreResult(false,'Fatura e anuluar nuk konfirmohet');
  if(f?.status==='Konfirmuar')return coreResult(false,'Fatura është konfirmuar më parë — përdorni korrigjimin');
  if(!f){f={...data,status:'Draft'};purchaseInvoices().push(f)}else Object.assign(f,data);
  if(pre){f.billable=0;f.physical=0;f.price=0;f.vat=0;f.subtotal=0;f.vatAmount=0;f.total=+data.total;}
  else{f.subtotal=qty*price;f.vatAmount=f.subtotal*vat/100;f.total=f.subtotal+f.vatAmount;}
  f.status=confirm?'Konfirmuar':'Draft';return coreResult(true,'',f);
}
function coreSupplierPayment(data){if(!data.supplier||!(data.amount>0))return coreResult(false,'Furnitori dhe shuma pozitive janë të detyrueshme');if(state.payments.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i mandatit ekziston');state.payments.push({...data,status:data.status||'Konfirmuar'});return coreResult(true,'',data)}
function coreProcess(data){let lot=by('lots',data.sourceLot);if(!lot?.id)return coreResult(false,'Loti burimor mungon');if(state.processes.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(true,'',state.processes.find(x=>x.id===data.id));if(!(data.input>0)||!(data.output>0)||data.input>lotAvail(lot)+.001||data.output>data.input&&!data.authorization)return coreResult(false,'Sasi procesi e pavlefshme ose mbi stok');let inputCost=+lot.unitCost||0,totalCost=data.input*inputCost,unitCost=totalCost/data.output,p={...data,lots:[data.sourceLot],input:data.input,output:data.output,loss:data.input-data.output,yield:data.output/data.input*100,status:'Përfunduar',unitCost,totalCost};state.processes.push(p);lot.availableNet=Math.max(0,lotAvail(lot)-data.input);lot.net=lot.availableNet;let out={id:data.outputLot,code:data.outputLotCode,parentLot:lot.id,sourceLots:[lot.id],process:p.id,supplier:lot.supplier,product:data.product||lot.product,warehouse:data.warehouse||lot.warehouse,rack:data.rack||lot.rack,net:data.output,originalNet:data.output,availableNet:data.output,unpackedNet:data.output,packedNet:0,unitCost,status:'Produkt përfundimtar',date:data.date,testRunId:data.testRunId};state.lots.push(out);coreMove({key:'PROCESS-OUT:'+p.id,type:'OUT',source:'process',sourceId:p.id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:-data.input,unitCost:inputCost,date:data.date,testRunId:data.testRunId});coreMove({key:'PROCESS-IN:'+p.id,type:'IN',source:'process',sourceId:p.id,lot:out.id,product:out.product,warehouse:out.warehouse,qty:data.output,unitCost,date:data.date,testRunId:data.testRunId});return coreResult(true,'',p)}
function corePackage(data){let net=(+data.bags||0)*(+data.bagWeight||0);if(!data.order||!data.product||!data.internalLot)return coreResult(false,'Të dhënat e paketimit sources=[],seen=new Set();for(let z of raw){let lot=by('lots',z.lot??z.lotId??z.id),qty=+z.qty;if(!lot||!lot.id)return coreResult(false,'Loti burimor mungon');if(seen.has(lot.id))return x.sourceLot;delete function coreCreateSale(data,confirm=false){if(!data.id||!data.invoiceNumber||!data.customer||!(data.lines||[]).length)return coreResult(false,'Fatura e shitjes nuk është e plotë');if(!coreUnique(salesInvoices(),'invoiceNumber',data.invoiceNumber,data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i faturës ekziston');if(data.lines.some(l=>!l.product||!(l.net>0)||l.price<0))return coreResult(false,'Rresht shitjeje i pavlefshëm');let f=salesInvoices().find(x=>x.id===data.id);if(!f){f={...data,status:'Draft'};salesInvoices().push(f)}else Object.assign(f,data);f.subtotal=f.lines.reduce((a,l)=>a+l.net*l.price*(1-(+l.discount||0)/100),0);f.vatAmount=f.subtotal*(+f.vat||0)/100;f.total=f.subtotal+f.vatAmount+(+f.palletCost||0)+(+f.loadingCost||0);f.totalALL=f.total*(f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||1));f.status=confirm?'Konfirmuar':'Draft';return coreResult(true,'',f)}
function coreCustomerReceipt(data){if(!data.customer||!(data.amount>0))return coreResult(false,'Klienti dhe shuma pozitive janë të detyrueshme');if(customerPayments().some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i mandatit ekziston');customerPayments().push({...data,status:data.status||'Konfirmuar'});return coreResult(true,'',data)}
function coreCreateShipment(data){if(state.shipments.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(true,'',by('shipments',data.id));let x={...data,status:'Planifikuar',stockDeducted:false};state.shipments.push(x);return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreAdvanceShipment(id,next){let x=by('shipments',id),cur=normalizedShipmentStatus(x),expected=shipmentStages[shipmentStages.indexOf(cur)+1];if(!x?.id||next!==expected)return coreResult(false,'Kalimi i statusit nuk është në rend');let missing=shipmentRequirements(x,next);if(missing.length)return coreResult(false,'Mungon: '+missing.join(', '));if(next==='Nisur'&&!x.stockDeducted){let allocations=packs().filter(p=>p.order===x.order),left=+x.net||0,lotIds=[...new Set(allocations.flatMap(pk=>pk.sourceLots||[]))],totalPacked=lotIds.reduce((a,lid)=>a+(+by('lots',lid)?.packedNet||0),0);if(totalPacked+.001<left)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');for(let lid of lotIds){let lot=by('lots',lid),take=Math.min(left,+lot?.packedNet||0);if(take){lot.packedNet-=take;lot.availableNet=Math.max(0,lotAvail(lot)-take);lot.net=lot.availableNet;coreMove({key:'SHIP:'+id+':'+lot.id,type:'OUT',source:'shipment',sourceId:id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:-take,unitCost:lot.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId});left-=take}}if(left>.001)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');x.stockDeducted=true;x.stockDeductedAt=new Date().toISOString()}x.status=next;return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreAccountingSync(runId=''){let a=ensureAccounting(),set=a.settings,created=0,tag=x=>!runId||x.testRunId===runId;function post(key,date,journal,desc,ref,lines,testRunId){let '+f.id,f.id,[{account:set.purchaseExpense||'311',debit:v,credit:0},{account:set.purchasePayable||'401',debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});state.payments.filter(f=>tag(f)&&(f.status||'Konfirmuar')==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let '+f.id,f.id,[{account:set.purchasePayable||'401',debit:v,credit:0},{account:cash?(set.cash||'530'):(set.bank||'512'),debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});salesInvoices().filter(f=>tag(f)&&f.status==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let rate=f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||1),rev=((+f.subtotal||0)+(+f.palletCost||0)+(+f.loadingCost||0))*rate,vat=(+f.vatAmount||0)*rate,gross=(+f.total||0)*rate,lines=[{account:set.salesReceivable||'411',debit:gross,credit:0},{account:set.salesRevenue||'700',debit:0,credit:rev}];if(vat)lines.push({account:set.salesVat||'445',debit:0,credit:vat});post('core:sale:'+f.id,f.date,'J-SALE','Shitje '+f.id,f.id,lines,f.testRunId)});customerPayments().filter(f=>tag(f)&&(f.status||'Konfirmuar')==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let rate=f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.rate||+f.exchangeRate||1);let       const password=box.querySelector('#pkAdminPass').value;
      const reason=box.querySelector('#pkAdminReason').value.trim();
      const err=box.querySelector('#pkAdminErr');
      if(!username||!password||!reason){ err.textContent='Plotëso administratorin, password-in dhe arsyen.'; return; }
      try{
        const resp=await fetch(base+'/api/auth/login',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({username,password})});
        const data=await resp.json().catch(()=>({}));
        if(!resp.ok || !data.ok){ err.textContent=data.error||'Autorizimi dështoi'; return; }
        if(!data.user || data.user.role!=='ROLE-ADMIN'){
          if(data.token) fetch(base+'/api/auth/logout',{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+data.token}}).catch(()=>{});
          err.textContent='Kredencialet nuk i përkasin një administratori.'; return;
        }
        if(data.token) fetch(base+'/api/auth/logout',{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+data.token}}).catch(()=>{});
        done({admin:data.user.username||username,reason,time:new Date().toISOString(),overruns:overruns.map(x=>({lot:x.lotId,code:x.code,qty:x.qty,stock:x.cap}))});
      }catch(e){ err.textContent='Serveri nuk përgjigjet — override nuk lejohet offline.'; }
    };
  });
};

// Override savePackaging to handle multi-lot with validation and stockMove
window.savePackaging = async function(){
  try{
    const order = $val('pkOrder');
    const product = $val('pkProduct');
    const internal = $val('pkInternal').trim();
    const o = by('orders', order);
    if(!order || !product || !internal) return toast('Plotësoni porosinë, produktin dhe lotin BioBes');
    const bags = $num('pkBags',0);
    const bagWeight = $num('pkBagWeight',0);
    if(!(bags>0) || !(bagWeight>0)) return toast('Thasët dhe kg/thes duhet të jenë më të mëdha se zero');
    const total = Math.round(bags*bagWeight*1000)/1000;
    const rows = packSourceRows('pk');
    if(!rows.length) return toast('Zgjidhni të paktën një lot burimor');
    // collect sources
    const seen=new Set();
    const sources=[];
    const overruns=[];
    let sum=0;
    for(let r of rows){
      const lotId = r.dataset.lot;
      const lot = by('lots', lotId);
      if(!lot || !lot.id) return toast('Loti burimor nuk u gjet');
      if(seen.has(lotId)) return toast('Loti '+lot.code+' është shtuar dy herë');
      seen.add(lotId);
      const qty = parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0;
      if(!(qty>0)) return toast('Sasia për lotin '+lot.code+' duhet të jetë pozitive');
      const cap = lotUnpacked(lot);
      if(qty > cap + 0.001) overruns.push({lotId:lotId,code:lot.code,qty:qty,cap:cap});
      if(lot.product !== product) return toast('Loti '+lot.code+' është i produktit tjetër — përzieni vetëm lotë të së njëjtit produkt');
      const q = Math.round(qty*1000)/1000;
      sources.push({lot: lotId, qty: q});
      sum = Math.round((sum+q)*1000)/1000;
    }
    let adminOverride = null;
    if(overruns.length){
      adminOverride = await packRequireAdminOverride(overruns);
      if(!adminOverride) return;
    }
    if(Math.abs(sum - total) > 0.01) return toast('Sasia nga lotet ('+fmt2(sum)+' kg) duhet të jetë '+fmt2(total)+' kg (diferenca '+fmt2(total-sum)+' kg)');
    const data = {
      id:nextSeqId('PK',packs(),1,new Date().getFullYear()),
      order: order,
      customer: o.customer,
      product: product,
      internalLot: internal,
      customerLot: $val('pkCustomer'),
      template: $val('pkTemplate'),
      bags: bags,
      bagWeight: bagWeight,
      cropYear: $num('pkCrop', new Date().getFullYear()),
      bestBefore: $num('pkBest', new Date().getFullYear()+3),
      cert: $val('pkCert'),
      organic: $val('pkOrganic'),
      originType: $val('pkOriginType'),
      part: $val('pkPart'),
      origin: $val('pkOrigin'),
      date: new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),
      sources: sources,
      adminOverride: adminOverride
    };
    const r = atomicTransaction('Paketim', ()=> corePackage(data));
    if(!r || !r.ok) return toast((r&&r.error)||'Paketimi dështoi');
    if(adminOverride){
      state.events = state.events || [];
      const od = adminOverride.overruns.map(x=>x.code+': '+fmt2(x.qty)+' kg / stok '+fmt2(x.stock)+' kg').join('; ');
      state.events.push({date:new Date().toLocaleString('sq-AL'),type:'Admin override',ref:r.record.id,text:'Tejkalim stoku në paketim. Admin: '+adminOverride.admin+'. Arsye: '+adminOverride.reason+'. '+od});
    }
    save();
    closeModal();
    render();
    setTimeout(()=> packagingCard(r.record.id), 80);
    toast('Paketimi u krijua');
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); toast('Gabim gjatë ruajtjes: '+e.message); }
};

// Helper for Burime column: "3 lot (001+003+005)" — show last 3 chars of code
function burimeLabel(x){
  const lots = packSourceLots(x);
  if(!lots.length) return '—';
  const codes = lots.map(l=>{
    const c = (l.code||'').replace(/^B\d*/,'');
    // take last 3 or full code for readability
    return esc(l.code);
  });
  // spec example: "3 lot (001+003)" — show count and codes joined with +
  // we show: '3 lot (B1... + B1...)'
  const short = lots.map(l=>{
    const m = l.code.match(/(\d+)$/);
    return m ? m[1].slice(-3) : l.code;
  }).join('+');
  return `${lots.length} lot (${short})`;
}

const _origViewsPackaging = views.packaging;
views.packaging = function(){
  const a = packs();
  return head('Paketimi & Etiketat','Lotet përfundimtare dhe skedarët për BarTender — redaktim dhe fshirje direkte',`<button class="btn primary" onclick="packagingForm()">+ Paketim i ri</button>`)
    + tableCard(['Paketimi','Porosia','Produkti','Burime','Loti BioBes','Loti klientit','Thasë','Neto','Modeli','Veprime'], a.map(x=>{
      const lots = packSourceLots(x);
      const burime = lots.length ? `${lots.length} lot (${lots.map(l=> esc((l.code||'').split('-').pop()||l.code)).join('+')})` : '—';
      // For spec example "3 lot (001+003)" we keep as above
      return [
        `<a class="link" onclick="packagingCard('${x.id}')">${x.id}</a>`,
        `<a class="link" onclick="orderCard('${x.order}')">${x.order}</a>`,
        esc(by('products',x.product).name||'—'),
        burime,
        esc(x.internalLot||'—'),
        esc(x.customerLot||'—'),
        x.bags,
        n(packNet(x))+' kg',
        esc(x.template||'—'),
        `<span class="row-actions">
          <button class="btn small" onclick="packagingCard('${x.id}')">Hap</button>
          <button class="btn small" onclick="packagingEditForm('${x.id}')">Ndrysho</button>
          <button class="btn small danger" data-admin-only onclick="packagingDelete('${x.id}')">Fshi</button>
          <button class="btn small" onclick="copyPackaging('${x.id}')">Kopjo</button>
        </span>`
      ];
    }));
};

// Add Kopjo function
window.copyPackaging = function(id){
  try{
    const x = packById(id);
    if(!x) return toast('Paketimi nuk u gjet');
    const newId = nextSeqId('PK',packs(),1,new Date().getFullYear());
    const clone = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x));
    clone.id = newId;
    clone.internalLot = (x.internalLot||'') + '-KOPI';
    clone.date = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
    // need to create new stock moves? Instead we should create via corePackage with new sources same as original
    // For Kopjo we can just open form prefilled?
    // Simpler: open form with same data, let user save
    packagingForm();
    setTimeout(()=>{
      try{
        $set('pkOrder', x.order);
        fillSearchSelect('pkOrder', x.order);
        $set('pkProduct', x.product);
        fillSearchSelect('pkProduct', x.product);
        $set('pkInternal', clone.internalLot);
        $set('pkCustomer', x.customerLot||'');
        $set('pkBags', x.bags);
        $set('pkBagWeight', x.bagWeight);
        $set('pkTemplate', x.template||'BioBes standard');
        $set('pkCrop', x.cropYear);
        $set('pkBest', x.bestBefore);
        $set('pkCert', x.cert||'');
        $set('pkOrganic', x.organic||'Organik');
        $set('pkOriginType', x.originType||'Wild collected');
        $set('pkPart', x.part||'Folia');
        $set('pkOrigin', x.origin||'Albania');
        // add source lots
        const c = packSourceContainer('pk');
        const tbody = document.querySelector('[data-pack-rows="pk"]');
        if(tbody){
          tbody.innerHTML='';
          (x.sourceLots||[]).forEach(lotId=>{
            const qty = packLotQty(x, lotId);
            tbody.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', packSourceRowHtml('pk', lotId, qty, {}));
          });
          packSourceSum('pk');
        }
        toast('Formulari u kopjua — ruaje si paketim të ri');
      }catch(e){ console.error(e); }
    },120);
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); toast('Gabim: '+e.message); }
};

// Ensure coreAdvanceShipment correctly deducts from each source lot (overwrite to be precise)
const origCoreAdvanceShipment = window.coreAdvanceShipment;
window.coreAdvanceShipment = function(id, next){
  const x=by('shipments',id),cur=normalizedShipmentStatus(x);
  const expected=shipmentStages[shipmentStages.indexOf(cur)+1];
  if(!x?.id||next!==expected)return coreResult(false,'Kalimi i statusit nuk është në rend');
  const missing=shipmentRequirements(x,next);
  if(missing.length)return coreResult(false,'Mungon: '+missing.join(', '));
  if(next==='Nisur'&&!x.stockDeducted){
    const orders=new Set(shipmentOrders(x));
    const allocations=packs().filter(p=>orders.has(p.order));
    const plan=new Map();let left=+x.net||0;
    if(!(left>0))return coreResult(false,'Pesha neto duhet pozitive');
    // Plan the entire shipment before changing stock. Partial shipments must not
    // deduct every package belonging to the order, or silently clamp a shortage.
    for(const pack of allocations){
      for(const lotId of (pack.sourceLots||[])){
        const lot=by('lots',lotId),reserved=plan.get(lotId)||0;
        if(!lot.id) return coreResult(false,'Loti burimor nuk u gjet');
        const free=Math.max(0,Math.min(+lot.packedNet||0,lotAvail(lot))-reserved);
        const take=Math.min(left,packLotQty(pack,lotId),free);
        if(take>0){plan.set(lotId,reserved+take);left-=take;}
      }
    }
    if(left>0.001)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');
    for(const [lotId,take] of plan){
      const lot=by('lots',lotId),before=lotAvail(lot);
      lot.packedNet=Math.max(0,(+lot.packedNet||0)-take);
      lot.availableNet=before-take;lot.net=lot.availableNet;
      coreMove({key:'SHIP:'+id+':'+lot.id,type:'OUT',source:'shipment',sourceId:id,
        lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:-take,
        unitCost:lot.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId});
    }
    x.stockDeducted=true;x.stockDeductedAt=new Date().toISOString();
  }
  x.status=next;return coreResult(true,'',x);
};

console.log('BioBes Paketim v4 loaded');

})();

</script>

window.syncPurchaseDrafts = function(){
  try{
    let changed=false;
    (state.weighings||[]).filter(w=>w.status==='Konfirmuar').forEach(w=>{
      let f = (purchaseInvoices()||[]).find(x=>x.weighing===w.id);

        if(!(f.billable>0) && w.billable>0){ f.billable = w.billable; changed=true; }
        if(f.billable==null || f.billable==='') { f.billable = w.billable||0; changed=true; }

        });
        changed=true;
      }
    });
    if(changed) try{ save(); }catch(e){}
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); }
};

function availableLotQuantity(lotId){let lot=by('lots',lotId);if(!lot)return 0;let start=+lot.originalNet||+lot.net||0,processOut=state.processes.filter(p=>p.status!=='Anuluar'&&(p.lots||[]).includes(lotId)).reduce((a,p)=>a+(+p.input||0),0),packOut=packs().filter(p=>(p.sourceLots||[]).includes(lotId)).reduce((a,p)=>a+packLotQty(p,lotId),0);return start-processOut-packOut}
function validateProcessBusiness(v){if(!v.order||!v.product||!v.lot||!v.machine)return'Plotësoni porosinë, produktin, lotin dhe makinerinë';if(!(v.input>0)||!(v.output>0))return'Sasitë duhet të jenë më të mëdha se zero';if(v.input>availableLotQuantity(v.lot)+.001)return'Hyrja e procesit tejkalon stokun e lotit';if(v.output>v.input&&!v.authorization)return'Dalja nuk mund të jetë më e madhe se hyrja pa autorizim';return''}

function coreMove(x){if(stockMoves().some(m=>m.key===x.key))return coreResult(true,'',stockMoves().find(m=>m.key===x.key));if(x.type==='IN'&&!(+x.qty>=0))return coreResult(false,'Lëvizje IN me sasi negative');if(x.type==='OUT'&&!(+x.qty<=0))return coreResult(false,'Lëvizje OUT me sasi pozitive');let m={id:'MOV-'+String(stockMoves().length+1).padStart(6,'0')+'-'+String(x.key).replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/gi,'-'),date:x.date||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),...x};stockMoves().push(m);return coreResult(true,'',m)}
function coreCreateMaster(type,data){let map={supplier:'suppliers',customer:'customers',product:'products',warehouse:'warehouses',rack:'racks',machine:'machines',bankAccount:'bankAccounts'},k=map[type],a=state[k]||(state[k]=[]);if(!data.id||!data.code||!data.name&&type!=='bankAccount')return coreResult(false,'Kodi dhe emri janë të detyrueshëm');if(!coreUnique(a,'code',data.code))return coreResult(false,'Kodi ekziston');a.push({...data});return coreResult(true,'',data)}
function coreCreateWeighing(data){if(!data.id||!data.supplier||!data.product||!data.weighingWarehouse)return coreResult(false,'Peshimi kërkon furnitor, produkt dhe magazinë');if(state.weighings.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(false,'Peshimi ekziston');let rows=data.bagRows||[];if(!rows.length||rows.some(r=>!(+r.gross>0)||+r.tare<0||+r.gross<+r.tare))return coreResult(false,'Rresht peshimi i pavlefshëm');let gross=rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(+r.gross||0),0),tare=rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(+r.tare||0),0),net=gross-tare,discount=+data.discount||0,x={...data,gross,tare,net,physical:net,billable:net*(1-discount/100),bags:rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(+r.bags||0),0),status:data.status||'Draft'};state.weighings.push(x);return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreConfirmWeighing(id,lotData){let w=by('weighings',id);if(!w?.id)return coreResult(false,'Peshimi nuk u gjet');if(!(w.gross>0)||!((+w.net||0)>0))return coreResult(false,'Peshimi ka peshë të pavlefshme (bruto/tara) dhe nuk konfirmohet');if(w.status==='Anuluar')return coreResult(false,'Peshimi i anuluar nuk mund të konfirmohet');let existing=state.lots.find(l=>l.weighing===id);if(existing){w.status='Konfirmuar';return coreResult(true,'',existing)};let lot={...lotData,weighing:id,gross:w.gross,tare:w.tare,net:w.net,originalNet:w.net,availableNet:w.net,bags:w.bags,date:w.date,status:'Në magazinim'};state.lots.push(lot);w.status='Konfirmuar';coreMove({key:'WEIGH:'+id,type:'IN',source:'weighing',sourceId:id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:w.net,unitCost:+lot.unitCost||0,date:w.date,testRunId:w.testRunId});return coreResult(true,'',lot)}
function coreCreatePurchase(data,confirm=false){
  if(!data.id||!data.supplier||!by('suppliers',data.supplier).id)return coreResult(false,'Fatura kërkon furnitor të vlefshëm');
  if(data.weighing&&!state.weighings.some(w=>w.id===data.weighing&&w.status==='Konfirmuar'))return coreResult(false,'Peshimi duhet të jetë i konfirmuar');
  if(data.supplierInvoiceNo&& !purchaseScopeUnique(data.supplier,data.supplierInvoiceNo,data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i faturës ekziston për këtë furnitor');
  const pre=!!data.isPrepayment,qty=+data.billable,price=+data.price,vat=+data.vat||0,rate=+data.exchangeRate;
  if(!Number.isFinite(rate)||rate<=0)return coreResult(false,'Kursi duhet të jetë pozitiv');
  if(!Number.isFinite(vat)||vat<0||vat>100)return coreResult(false,'TVSH duhet të jetë midis 0 dhe 100%');
  if(pre){if(!Number.isFinite(+data.total)||!(+data.total>0))return coreResult(false,'Shuma e parapagimit duhet pozitive');}
  else if(!Number.isFinite(qty)||!(qty>0)||!Number.isFinite(price)||price<0||(confirm&&price<=0))return coreResult(false,'Plotësoni sasinë dhe çmimin pozitiv për konfirmim');
  let f=purchaseInvoices().find(x=>x.id===data.id);
  if(f?.status==='Anuluar')return coreResult(false,'Fatura e anuluar nuk konfirmohet');
  if(f?.status==='Konfirmuar')return coreResult(false,'Fatura është konfirmuar më parë — përdorni korrigjimin');
  if(!f){f={...data,status:'Draft'};purchaseInvoices().push(f)}else Object.assign(f,data);
  if(pre){f.billable=0;f.physical=0;f.price=0;f.vat=0;f.subtotal=0;f.vatAmount=0;f.total=+data.total;}
  else{f.subtotal=qty*price;f.vatAmount=f.subtotal*vat/100;f.total=f.subtotal+f.vatAmount;}
  f.status=confirm?'Konfirmuar':'Draft';return coreResult(true,'',f);
}
function coreSupplierPayment(data){if(!data.supplier||!(data.amount>0))return coreResult(false,'Furnitori dhe shuma pozitive janë të detyrueshme');if(state.payments.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i mandatit ekziston');state.payments.push({...data,status:data.status||'Konfirmuar'});return coreResult(true,'',data)}
function coreProcess(data){let lot=by('lots',data.sourceLot);if(!lot?.id)return coreResult(false,'Loti burimor mungon');if(state.processes.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(true,'',state.processes.find(x=>x.id===data.id));if(!(data.input>0)||!(data.output>0)||data.input>lotAvail(lot)+.001||data.output>data.input&&!data.authorization)return coreResult(false,'Sasi procesi e pavlefshme ose mbi stok');let inputCost=+lot.unitCost||0,totalCost=data.input*inputCost,unitCost=totalCost/data.output,p={...data,lots:[data.sourceLot],input:data.input,output:data.output,loss:data.input-data.output,yield:data.output/data.input*100,status:'Përfunduar',unitCost,totalCost};state.processes.push(p);lot.availableNet=Math.max(0,lotAvail(lot)-data.input);lot.net=lot.availableNet;let out={id:data.outputLot,code:data.outputLotCode,parentLot:lot.id,sourceLots:[lot.id],process:p.id,supplier:lot.supplier,product:data.product||lot.product,warehouse:data.warehouse||lot.warehouse,rack:data.rack||lot.rack,net:data.output,originalNet:data.output,availableNet:data.output,unpackedNet:data.output,packedNet:0,unitCost,status:'Produkt përfundimtar',date:data.date,testRunId:data.testRunId};state.lots.push(out);coreMove({key:'PROCESS-OUT:'+p.id,type:'OUT',source:'process',sourceId:p.id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:-data.input,unitCost:inputCost,date:data.date,testRunId:data.testRunId});coreMove({key:'PROCESS-IN:'+p.id,type:'IN',source:'process',sourceId:p.id,lot:out.id,product:out.product,warehouse:out.warehouse,qty:data.output,unitCost,date:data.date,testRunId:data.testRunId});return coreResult(true,'',p)}
function corePackage(data){let net=(+data.bags||0)*(+data.bagWeight||0);if(!data.order||!data.product||!data.internalLot)return coreResult(false,'Të dhënat e paketimit mungojnë');if(packs().some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(true,'',packs().find(x=>x.id===data.id));let raw=(data.sources&&data.sources.length)?data.sources:(data.sourceLot?[{lot:data.sourceLot,qty:net}]:[]);if(!raw.length)return coreResult(false,'Zgjidhni të paktën një lot burimor');let sources=[],seen=new Set();for(let z of raw){let lot=by('lots',z.lot??z.lotId??z.id),qty=+z.qty;if(!lot||!lot.id)return coreResult(false,'Loti burimor mungon');if(seen.has(lot.id))return coreResult(false,'Loti '+lot.code+' është shtuar dy herë');seen.add(lot.id);if(!(qty>0))return coreResult(false,'Sasia për lotin '+lot.code+' është e pavlefshme');if(qty>lotUnpacked(lot)+.001)return coreResult(false,'Paketimi tejkalon stokun e papaketuar të lotit '+lot.code+' (të lira '+fmt2(lotUnpacked(lot))+' kg)');if(lot.product!==data.product)return coreResult(false,'Loti '+lot.code+' është i produktit tjetër — përzieni vetëm lotë të së njëjtit produkt');sources.push({lot,qty})}if(!(net>0))return coreResult(false,'Pesha e paketimit është e pavlefshme');let sum=sources.reduce((a,z)=>a+z.qty,0);if(Math.abs(sum-net)>.01)return coreResult(false,'Sasia nga lotet ('+fmt2(sum)+' kg) duhet të jetë '+fmt2(net)+' kg');let ids=sources.map(z=>z.lot.id),qtys={};sources.forEach(z=>qtys[z.lot.id]=Math.round(z.qty*1000)/1000);let x={...data,sourceLots:ids,sourceLotQtys:qtys,net,qty:net,status:data.status||'Paketuar'};delete x.sourceLot;delete x.sources;packs().push(x);sources.forEach(z=>{z.lot.unpackedNet=lotUnpacked(z.lot)-z.qty;z.lot.packedNet=(+z.lot.packedNet||0)+z.qty;coreMove({key:(sources.length===1?'PACK:'+x.id:'PACK:'+x.id+':'+z.lot.id),type:'TRANSFER',source:'packaging',sourceId:x.id,lot:z.lot.id,product:z.lot.product,warehouse:z.lot.warehouse,qty:0,fromState:'unpacked',toState:'packed',stateQty:z.qty,unitCost:z.lot.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId})});return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreCreateSale(data,confirm=false){if(!data.id||!data.invoiceNumber||!data.customer||!(data.lines||[]).length)return coreResult(false,'Fatura e shitjes nuk është e plotë');if(!coreUnique(salesInvoices(),'invoiceNumber',data.invoiceNumber,data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i faturës ekziston');if(data.lines.some(l=>!l.product||!(l.net>0)||l.price<0))return coreResult(false,'Rresht shitjeje i pavlefshëm');let f=salesInvoices().find(x=>x.id===data.id);if(!f){f={...data,status:'Draft'};salesInvoices().push(f)}else Object.assign(f,data);f.subtotal=f.lines.reduce((a,l)=>a+l.net*l.price*(1-(+l.discount||0)/100),0);f.vatAmount=f.subtotal*(+f.vat||0)/100;f.total=f.subtotal+f.vatAmount+(+f.palletCost||0)+(+f.loadingCost||0);f.totalALL=f.total*(f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||1));f.status=confirm?'Konfirmuar':'Draft';return coreResult(true,'',f)}
function coreCustomerReceipt(data){if(!data.customer||!(data.amount>0))return coreResult(false,'Klienti dhe shuma pozitive janë të detyrueshme');if(customerPayments().some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i mandatit ekziston');customerPayments().push({...data,status:data.status||'Konfirmuar'});return coreResult(true,'',data)}
function coreCreateShipment(data){if(state.shipments.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(true,'',by('shipments',data.id));let x={...data,status:'Planifikuar',stockDeducted:false};state.shipments.push(x);return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreAdvanceShipment(id,next){let x=by('shipments',id),cur=normalizedShipmentStatus(x),expected=shipmentStages[shipmentStages.indexOf(cur)+1];if(!x?.id||next!==expected)return coreResult(false,'Kalimi i statusit nuk është në rend');let missing=shipmentRequirements(x,next);if(missing.length)return coreResult(false,'Mungon: '+missing.join(', '));if(next==='Nisur'&&!x.stockDeducted){let allocations=packs().filter(p=>p.order===x.order),left=+x.net||0,lotIds=[...new Set(allocations.flatMap(pk=>pk.sourceLots||[]))],totalPacked=lotIds.reduce((a,lid)=>a+(+by('lots',lid)?.packedNet||0),0);if(totalPacked+.001<left)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');for(let lid of lotIds){let lot=by('lots',lid),take=Math.min(left,+lot?.packedNet||0);if(take){lot.packedNet-=take;lot.availableNet=Math.max(0,lotAvail(lot)-take);lot.net=lot.availableNet;coreMove({key:'SHIP:'+id+':'+lot.id,type:'OUT',source:'shipment',sourceId:id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:-take,unitCost:lot.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId});left-=take}}if(left>.001)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');x.stockDeducted=true;x.stockDeductedAt=new Date().toISOString()}x.status=next;return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreAccountingSync(runId=''){let a=ensureAccounting(),set=a.settings,created=0,tag=x=>!runId||x.testRunId===runId;function post(key,date,journal,desc,ref,lines,testRunId){let '+f.id,f.id,[{account:set.purchasePayable||'401',debit:v,credit:0},{account:cash?(set.cash||'530'):(set.bank||'512'),debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});salesInvoices().filter(f=>tag(f)&&f.status==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let rate=f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||1),rev=((+f.subtotal||0)+(+f.palletCost||0)+(+f.loadingCost||0))*rate,vat=(+f.vatAmount||0)*rate,gross=(+f.total||0)*rate,lines=[{account:set.salesReceivable||'411',debit:gross,credit:0},{account:set.salesRevenue||'700',debit:0,credit:rev}];if(vat)lines.push({account:set.salesVat||'445',debit:0,credit:vat});post('core:sale:'+f.id,f.date,'J-SALE','Shitje '+f.id,f.id,lines,f.testRunId)});customerPayments().filter(f=>tag(f)&&(f.status||'Konfirmuar')==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let rate=f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.rate||+f.exchangeRate||1);let '+f.id,f.id,[{account:set.cogs||'603',debit:v,credit:0},{account:set.inventory||'311',debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});return created}

function coreConfirmWeighing(id,lotData){let w=by('weighings',id);if(!w?.id)return coreResult(false,'Peshimi nuk u gjet');if(!(w.gross>0)||!((+w.net||0)>0))return coreResult(false,'Peshimi ka peshë të pavlefshme (bruto/tara) dhe nuk konfirmohet');if(w.status==='Anuluar')return coreResult(false,'Peshimi i anuluar nuk mund të konfirmohet');let existing=state.lots.find(l=>l.weighing===id);if(existing){w.status='Konfirmuar';return coreResult(true,'',existing)};let lot={...lotData,weighing:id,gross:w.gross,tare:w.tare,net:w.net,originalNet:w.net,availableNet:w.net,bags:w.bags,date:w.date,status:'Në magazinim'};state.lots.push(lot);w.status='Konfirmuar';coreMove({key:'WEIGH:'+id,type:'IN',source:'weighing',sourceId:id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:w.net,unitCost:+lot.unitCost||0,date:w.date,testRunId:w.testRunId});return coreResult(true,'',lot)}
function coreCreatePurchase(data,confirm=false){
  if(!data.id||!data.supplier||!by('suppliers',data.supplier).id)return coreResult(false,'Fatura kërkon furnitor të vlefshëm');
  if(data.weighing&&!state.weighings.some(w=>w.id===data.weighing&&w.status==='Konfirmuar'))return coreResult(false,'Peshimi duhet të jetë i konfirmuar');
  if(data.supplierInvoiceNo&& !purchaseScopeUnique(data.supplier,data.supplierInvoiceNo,data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i faturës ekziston për këtë furnitor');
  const pre=!!data.isPrepayment,qty=+data.billable,price=+data.price,vat=+data.vat||0,rate=+data.exchangeRate;
  if(!Number.isFinite(rate)||rate<=0)return coreResult(false,'Kursi duhet të jetë pozitiv');
  if(!Number.isFinite(vat)||vat<0||vat>100)return coreResult(false,'TVSH duhet të jetë midis 0 dhe 100%');
  if(pre){if(!Number.isFinite(+data.total)||!(+data.total>0))return coreResult(false,'Shuma e parapagimit duhet pozitive');}
  else if(!Number.isFinite(qty)||!(qty>0)||!Number.isFinite(price)||price<0||(confirm&&price<=0))return coreResult(false,'Plotësoni sasinë dhe çmimin pozitiv për konfirmim');
  let f=purchaseInvoices().find(x=>x.id===data.id);
  if(f?.status==='Anuluar')return coreResult(false,'Fatura e anuluar nuk konfirmohet');
  if(f?.status==='Konfirmuar')return coreResult(false,'Fatura është konfirmuar më parë — përdorni korrigjimin');
  if(!f){f={...data,status:'Draft'};purchaseInvoices().push(f)}else Object.assign(f,data);
  if(pre){f.billable=0;f.physical=0;f.price=0;f.vat=0;f.subtotal=0;f.vatAmount=0;f.total=+data.total;}
  else{f.subtotal=qty*price;f.vatAmount=f.subtotal*vat/100;f.total=f.subtotal+f.vatAmount;}
  f.status=confirm?'Konfirmuar':'Draft';return coreResult(true,'',f);
}

function coreAdvanceShipment(id,next){let x=by('shipments',id),cur=normalizedShipmentStatus(x),expected=shipmentStages[shipmentStages.indexOf(cur)+1];if(!x?.id||next!==expected)return coreResult(false,'Kalimi i statusit nuk është në rend');let missing=shipmentRequirements(x,next);if(missing.length)return coreResult(false,'Mungon: '+missing.join(', '));if(next==='Nisur'&&!x.stockDeducted){let allocations=packs().filter(p=>p.order===x.order),left=+x.net||0,lotIds=[...new Set(allocations.flatMap(pk=>pk.sourceLots||[]))],totalPacked=lotIds.reduce((a,lid)=>a+(+by('lots',lid)?.packedNet||0),0);if(totalPacked+.001<left)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');for(let lid of lotIds){let lot=by('lots',lid),take=Math.min(left,+lot?.packedNet||0);if(take){lot.packedNet-=take;lot.availableNet=Math.max(0,lotAvail(lot)-take);lot.net=lot.availableNet;coreMove({key:'SHIP:'+id+':'+lot.id,type:'OUT',source:'shipment',sourceId:id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:-take,unitCost:lot.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId});left-=take}}if(left>.001)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');x.stockDeducted=true;x.stockDeductedAt=new Date().toISOString()}x.status=next;return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreAccountingSync(runId=''){let a=ensureAccounting(),set=a.settings,created=0,tag=x=>!runId||x.testRunId===runId;function post(key,date,journal,desc,ref,lines,testRunId){let bare=String(key).replace(/^core:/,'');if(a.entries.some(e=>e.sourceKey===key||e.sourceKey===bare))return;let d=lines.reduce((z,l)=>z+l.debit,0),c=lines.reduce((z,l)=>z+l.credit,0);a.entries.push({id:uid('JE'),number:'VK-'+new Date().getFullYear()+'-'+String(a.entries.length+1).padStart(5,'0'),sourceKey:key,sourceRef:ref,date,journal,description:desc,lines,status:Math.abs(d-c)<.005?'Postuar':'Gabim',testRunId});created++}purchaseInvoices().filter(f=>tag(f)&&f.status==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let v=f.total*(f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||1));post('core:purchase:'+f.id,f.invoiceDate,'J-PUR','Blerje '+f.id,f.id,[{account:set.purchaseExpense||'311',debit:v,credit:0},{account:set.purchasePayable||'401',debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});state.payments.filter(f=>tag(f)&&(f.status||'Konfirmuar')==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let rate=f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||+f.rate||1);let v=(+f.amount||0)*rate;let cash=/cash|ark/i.test(f.method||'');post('core:payment:'+f.id,f.date,cash?'J-CASH':'J-BANK','Pagesë '+f.id,f.id,[{account:set.purchasePayable||'401',debit:v,credit:0},{account:cash?(set.cash||'530'):(set.bank||'512'),debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});salesInvoices().filter(f=>tag(f)&&f.status==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let rate=f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||1),rev=((+f.subtotal||0)+(+f.palletCost||0)+(+f.loadingCost||0))*rate,vat=(+f.vatAmount||0)*rate,gross=(+f.total||0)*rate,lines=[{account:set.salesReceivable||'411',debit:gross,credit:0},{account:set.salesRevenue||'700',debit:0,credit:rev}];if(vat)lines.push({account:set.salesVat||'445',debit:0,credit:vat});post('core:sale:'+f.id,f.date,'J-SALE','Shitje '+f.id,f.id,lines,f.testRunId)});customerPayments().filter(f=>tag(f)&&(f.status||'Konfirmuar')==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let rate=f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.rate||+f.exchangeRate||1);let v=stockMoves().filter(m=>m.source==='shipment'&&m.sourceId===f.id).reduce((z,m)=>z+Math.abs(m.qty)*m.unitCost,0);post('core:cogs:'+f.id,f.date,'J-GEN','KMSH '+f.id,f.id,[{account:set.cogs||'603',debit:v,credit:0},{account:set.inventory||'311',debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});return created}

function coreConfirmWeighing(id,lotData){let w=by('weighings',id);if(!w?.id)return coreResult(false,'Peshimi nuk u gjet');if(!(w.gross>0)||!((+w.net||0)>0))return coreResult(false,'Peshimi ka peshë të pavlefshme (bruto/tara) dhe nuk konfirmohet');if(w.status==='Anuluar')return coreResult(false,'Peshimi i anuluar nuk mund të konfirmohet');let existing=state.lots.find(l=>l.weighing===id);if(existing){w.status='Konfirmuar';return coreResult(true,'',existing)};let lot={...lotData,weighing:id,gross:w.gross,tare:w.tare,net:w.net,originalNet:w.net,availableNet:w.net,bags:w.bags,date:w.date,status:'Në magazinim'};state.lots.push(lot);w.status='Konfirmuar';coreMove({key:'WEIGH:'+id,type:'IN',source:'weighing',sourceId:id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:w.net,unitCost:+lot.unitCost||0,date:w.date,testRunId:w.testRunId});return coreResult(true,'',lot)}
function coreCreatePurchase(data,confirm=false){
  if(!data.id||!data.supplier||!by('suppliers',data.supplier).id)return coreResult(false,'Fatura kërkon furnitor të vlefshëm');
  if(data.weighing&&!state.weighings.some(w=>w.id===data.weighing&&w.status==='Konfirmuar'))return coreResult(false,'Peshimi duhet të jetë i konfirmuar');
  if(data.supplierInvoiceNo&& !purchaseScopeUnique(data.supplier,data.supplierInvoiceNo,data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i faturës ekziston për këtë furnitor');
  const pre=!!data.isPrepayment,qty=+data.billable,price=+data.price,vat=+data.vat||0,rate=+data.exchangeRate;
  if(!Number.isFinite(rate)||rate<=0)return coreResult(false,'Kursi duhet të jetë pozitiv');
  if(!Number.isFinite(vat)||vat<0||vat>100)return coreResult(false,'TVSH duhet të jetë midis 0 dhe 100%');
  if(pre){if(!Number.isFinite(+data.total)||!(+data.total>0))return coreResult(false,'Shuma e parapagimit duhet pozitive');}
  else if(!Number.isFinite(qty)||!(qty>0)||!Number.isFinite(price)||price<0||(confirm&&price<=0))return coreResult(false,'Plotësoni sasinë dhe çmimin pozitiv për konfirmim');
  let f=purchaseInvoices().find(x=>x.id===data.id);
  if(f?.status==='Anuluar')return coreResult(false,'Fatura e anuluar nuk konfirmohet');
  if(f?.status==='Konfirmuar')return coreResult(false,'Fatura është konfirmuar më parë — përdorni korrigjimin');
  if(!f){f={...data,status:'Draft'};purchaseInvoices().push(f)}else Object.assign(f,data);
  if(pre){f.billable=0;f.physical=0;f.price=0;f.vat=0;f.subtotal=0;f.vatAmount=0;f.total=+data.total;}
  else{f.subtotal=qty*price;f.vatAmount=f.subtotal*vat/100;f.total=f.subtotal+f.vatAmount;}
  f.status=confirm?'Konfirmuar':'Draft';return coreResult(true,'',f);
}
function coreSupplierPayment(data){if(!data.supplier||!(data.amount>0))return coreResult(false,'Furnitori dhe shuma pozitive janë të detyrueshme');if(state.payments.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i mandatit ekziston');state.payments.push({...data,status:data.status||'Konfirmuar'});return coreResult(true,'',data)}
function coreProcess(data){let lot=by('lots',data.sourceLot);if(!lot?.id)return coreResult(false,'Loti burimor mungon');if(state.processes.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(true,'',state.processes.find(x=>x.id===data.id));if(!(data.input>0)||!(data.output>0)||data.input>lotAvail(lot)+.001||data.output>data.input&&!data.authorization)return coreResult(false,'Sasi procesi e pavlefshme ose mbi stok');let inputCost=+lot.unitCost||0,totalCost=data.input*inputCost,unitCost=totalCost/data.output,p={...data,lots:[data.sourceLot],input:data.input,output:data.output,loss:data.input-data.output,yield:data.output/data.input*100,status:'Përfunduar',unitCost,totalCost};state.processes.push(p);lot.availableNet=Math.max(0,lotAvail(lot)-data.input);lot.net=lot.availableNet;let out={id:data.outputLot,code:data.outputLotCode,parentLot:lot.id,sourceLots:[lot.id],process:p.id,supplier:lot.supplier,product:data.product||lot.product,warehouse:data.warehouse||lot.warehouse,rack:data.rack||lot.rack,net:data.output,originalNet:data.output,availableNet:data.output,unpackedNet:data.output,packedNet:0,unitCost,status:'Produkt përfundimtar',date:data.date,testRunId:data.testRunId};state.lots.push(out);coreMove({key:'PROCESS-OUT:'+p.id,type:'OUT',source:'process',sourceId:p.id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:-data.input,unitCost:inputCost,date:data.date,testRunId:data.testRunId});coreMove({key:'PROCESS-IN:'+p.id,type:'IN',source:'process',sourceId:p.id,lot:out.id,product:out.product,warehouse:out.warehouse,qty:data.output,unitCost,date:data.date,testRunId:data.testRunId});return coreResult(true,'',p)}

sources=[],seen=new Set();for(let z of raw){let lot=by('lots',z.lot??z.lotId??z.id),qty=+z.qty;if(!lot||!lot.id)return coreResult(false,'Loti burimor mungon');if(seen.has(lot.id))return ids=sources.map(z=>z.lot.id),qtys={};sources.forEach(z=>qtys[z.lot.id]=Math.round(z.qty*1000)/1000);let x={...data,sourceLots:ids,sourceLotQtys:qtys,net,qty:net,status:data.status||'Paketuar'};delete x.sourceLot;delete x.sources;packs().push(x);sources.forEach(z=>{z.lot.unpackedNet=lotUnpacked(z.lot)-z.qty;z.lot.packedNet=(+z.lot.packedNet||0)+z.qty;coreMove({key:(sources.length===1?'PACK:'+x.id:'PACK:'+x.id+':'+z.lot.id),type:'TRANSFER',source:'packaging',sourceId:x.id,lot:z.lot.id,product:z.lot.product,warehouse:z.lot.warehouse,qty:0,fromState:'unpacked',toState:'packed',stateQty:z.qty,unitCost:z.lot.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId})});return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreCreateSale(data,confirm=false){if(!data.id||!data.invoiceNumber||!data.customer||!(data.lines||[]).length)return coreResult(false,'Fatura e shitjes nuk është e plotë');if(!coreUnique(salesInvoices(),'invoiceNumber',data.invoiceNumber,data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i faturës ekziston');if(data.lines.some(l=>!l.product||!(l.net>0)||l.price<0))return coreResult(false,'Rresht shitjeje i pavlefshëm');let f=salesInvoices().find(x=>x.id===data.id);if(!f){f={...data,status:'Draft'};salesInvoices().push(f)}else Object.assign(f,data);f.subtotal=f.lines.reduce((a,l)=>a+l.net*l.price*(1-(+l.discount||0)/100),0);f.vatAmount=f.subtotal*(+f.vat||0)/100;f.total=f.subtotal+f.vatAmount+(+f.palletCost||0)+(+f.loadingCost||0);f.totalALL=f.total*(f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||1));f.status=confirm?'Konfirmuar':'Draft';return coreResult(true,'',f)}
function coreCustomerReceipt(data){if(!data.customer||!(data.amount>0))return coreResult(false,'Klienti dhe shuma pozitive janë të detyrueshme');if(customerPayments().some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(false,'Numri i mandatit ekziston');customerPayments().push({...data,status:data.status||'Konfirmuar'});return coreResult(true,'',data)}
function coreCreateShipment(data){if(state.shipments.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(true,'',by('shipments',data.id));let x={...data,status:'Planifikuar',stockDeducted:false};state.shipments.push(x);return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreAdvanceShipment(id,next){let x=by('shipments',id),cur=normalizedShipmentStatus(x),expected=shipmentStages[shipmentStages.indexOf(cur)+1];if(!x?.id||next!==expected)return coreResult(false,'Kalimi i statusit nuk është në rend');let missing=shipmentRequirements(x,next);if(missing.length)return coreResult(false,'Mungon: '+missing.join(', '));if(next==='Nisur'&&!x.stockDeducted){let allocations=packs().filter(p=>p.order===x.order),left=+x.net||0,lotIds=[...new Set(allocations.flatMap(pk=>pk.sourceLots||[]))],totalPacked=lotIds.reduce((a,lid)=>a+(+by('lots',lid)?.packedNet||0),0);if(totalPacked+.001<left)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');for(let lid of lotIds){let lot=by('lots',lid),take=Math.min(left,+lot?.packedNet||0);if(take){lot.packedNet-=take;lot.availableNet=Math.max(0,lotAvail(lot)-take);lot.net=lot.availableNet;coreMove({key:'SHIP:'+id+':'+lot.id,type:'OUT',source:'shipment',sourceId:id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:-take,unitCost:lot.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId});left-=take}}if(left>.001)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');x.stockDeducted=true;x.stockDeductedAt=new Date().toISOString()}x.status=next;return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function coreAccountingSync(runId=''){let a=ensureAccounting(),set=a.settings,created=0,tag=x=>!runId||x.testRunId===runId;function post(key,date,journal,desc,ref,lines,testRunId){let bare=String(key).replace(/^core:/,'');if(a.entries.some(e=>e.sourceKey===key||e.sourceKey===bare))return;let d=lines.reduce((z,l)=>z+l.debit,0),c=lines.reduce((z,l)=>z+l.credit,0);a.entries.push({id:uid('JE'),number:'VK-'+new Date().getFullYear()+'-'+String(a.entries.length+1).padStart(5,'0'),sourceKey:key,sourceRef:ref,date,journal,description:desc,lines,status:Math.abs(d-c)<.005?'Postuar':'Gabim',testRunId});created++}purchaseInvoices().filter(f=>tag(f)&&f.status==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let v=f.total*(f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||1));post('core:purchase:'+f.id,f.invoiceDate,'J-PUR','Blerje '+f.id,f.id,[{account:set.purchaseExpense||'311',debit:v,credit:0},{account:set.purchasePayable||'401',debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});state.payments.filter(f=>tag(f)&&(f.status||'Konfirmuar')==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let rate=f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||+f.rate||1);let v=(+f.amount||0)*rate;let cash=/cash|ark/i.test(f.method||'');post('core:payment:'+f.id,f.date,cash?'J-CASH':'J-BANK','Pagesë '+f.id,f.id,[{account:set.purchasePayable||'401',debit:v,credit:0},{account:cash?(set.cash||'530'):(set.bank||'512'),debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});salesInvoices().filter(f=>tag(f)&&f.status==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let rate=f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.exchangeRate||1),rev=((+f.subtotal||0)+(+f.palletCost||0)+(+f.loadingCost||0))*rate,vat=(+f.vatAmount||0)*rate,gross=(+f.total||0)*rate,lines=[{account:set.salesReceivable||'411',debit:gross,credit:0},{account:set.salesRevenue||'700',debit:0,credit:rev}];if(vat)lines.push({account:set.salesVat||'445',debit:0,credit:vat});post('core:sale:'+f.id,f.date,'J-SALE','Shitje '+f.id,f.id,lines,f.testRunId)});customerPayments().filter(f=>tag(f)&&(f.status||'Konfirmuar')==='Konfirmuar').forEach(f=>{let rate=f.currency==='ALL'?1:(+f.rate||+f.exchangeRate||1);let v=(+f.amount||0)*rate;let cash=/cash|ark/i.test(f.method||'');post('core:receipt:'+f.id,f.date,cash?'J-CASH':'J-BANK','Arkëtim '+f.id,f.id,[{account:cash?(set.cash||'530'):(set.bank||'512'),debit:v,credit:0},{account:set.salesReceivable||'411',debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});state.shipments.filter(f=>tag(f)&&f.stockDeducted).forEach(f=>{let v=stockMoves().filter(m=>m.source==='shipment'&&m.sourceId===f.id).reduce((z,m)=>z+Math.abs(m.qty)*m.unitCost,0);post('core:cogs:'+f.id,f.date,'J-GEN','KMSH '+f.id,f.id,[{account:set.cogs||'603',debit:v,credit:0},{account:set.inventory||'311',debit:0,credit:v}],f.testRunId)});return created}

(function(){
  var dashBase2=views.dashboard;
  views.dashboard=function(){
    var html=dashBase2();
    try{
      // Add Odoo-like quick stats bar if not already
      if(html.includes('odoo-dash-quick')) return html;
      var extraStats=`<div class="odoo-dash-quick" style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(180px,1fr));gap:10px;margin:14px 0">
        <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #176b49"><small>Fatura shitje</small><strong>${(state.salesInvoices||[]).filter(f=>f.status==='Konfirmuar').length} / ${(state.salesInvoices||[]).length}</strong><small style="color:var(--muted)">Konfirmuar / Total</small></div>
        <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #2e90fa"><small>Porosi aktive</small><strong>${(state.orders||[]).filter(o=>!['Anuluar','Përfunduar','E faturuar'].includes(o.status)).length}</strong><small style="color:var(--muted)">${(state.orders||[]).length} total</small></div>
        <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #f79009"><small>Mostra në proces</small><strong>${(state.samples||[]).filter(x=>['E dërguar','Në analizë','Në pritje të klientit'].includes(x.status)).length}</strong><small style="color:var(--muted)">${(state.samples||[]).length} total</small></div>
        <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #875a7b"><small>Ngarkesa</small><strong>${(state.shipments||[]).filter(x=>x.status!=='Anuluar').length}</strong><small style="color:var(--muted)">${(state.documents||[]).filter(d=>d.fileData).length} dokumente</small></div>
      </div>`;
      // Insert after kpis
      html=html.replace('</div><div class="grid2">', '</div>'+extraStats+'<div class="grid2">');
      // If grid2 not found, insert after first kpis closing
      if(!html.includes('odoo-dash-quick')){
        var kpiEnd=html.indexOf('</div>', html.indexOf('class="kpis"'));
        if(kpiEnd>0) html=html.slice(0,kpiEnd+6)+extraStats+html.slice(kpiEnd+6);
      }
    }catch(e){ console.log('dash enhance err', e); }
    return html;
  };
