<script id="biobes-packaging-edit-v1">
/* ============================================================================
   PAKETIMI — REDAKTIM DIREKT (Ndrysho / Fshi)
   ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Lista e Paketimit dhe kartela e etiketës marrin butonat "Ndrysho" dhe "Fshi".
   • Çdo ndryshim i sasisë ose i lotit burimor rregullon stokun real
     (lot.unpackedNet / lot.packedNet) dhe lëvizjen TRANSFER "PACK:<id>".
   • Fshirja është vetëm për ADMIN, kërkon arsye, kthen kilogramët te loti
     burimor dhe lë shenjë kundërveprimi (REV:PACK:<id>) në librin e lëvizjeve.
   • Paketimi i lidhur me ngarkesë të ngarkuar/nisur ose me faturë të
     konfirmuar nuk mund të ndryshohet as të fshihet.
   Të gjitha leximet e formularit bëhen me $val/$num (jo me global-e të pasigurta).
   ========================================================================== */

function packById(id){return packs().find(x=>x.id===id)||null}
function packSourceLot(x){return by('lots',(x&&x.sourceLots||[])[0])||{}}
function packSourceLots(x){return((x&&x.sourceLots)||[]).map(id=>by('lots',id)).filter(l=>l&&l.id)}
function packLotQty(x,lotId){
  if(!x||!(x.sourceLots||[]).includes(lotId))return 0;
  let q=+(x.sourceLotQtys?x.sourceLotQtys[lotId]:NaN);
  if(Number.isFinite(q)&&q>0)return q;
  if((x.sourceLots||[]).length===1)return packNet(x);
  return 0;
}
function packStockMoves(x){return stockMoves().filter(m=>m.source==='packaging'&&m.sourceId===x.id&&m.status!=='Anuluar'&&!m.reversalOf)}
function packagingCandidateLots(productId){return state.lots.filter(l=>!String(l.status||'').toLowerCase().includes('anuluar')&&(!productId||l.product===productId))}
function packSourceContainer(prefix){return document.querySelector('[data-pack-source="'+prefix+'"]')}
function packSourceRows(prefix){return Array.from(packSourceContainer(prefix)?packSourceContainer(prefix).querySelectorAll('[data-pack-source-row]'):[])}
function packSourceTarget(prefix){let c=packSourceContainer(prefix);return c?($num(c.dataset.packBags,0))*($num(c.dataset.packBw,0)):0}
function packSourceOldMap(prefix){try{return JSON.parse(packSourceContainer(prefix)?.dataset.packOld||'{}')}catch(e){return{}}}
function packSourceRowHtml(prefix,lotId,qty,oldMap){
  oldMap=oldMap||{};let lot=by('lots',lotId);if(!lot||!lot.id)return'';
  let p=by('products',lot.product)||{},free=lotUnpacked(lot)+(((+oldMap[lotId])||0));
  return '<div data-pack-source-row="'+prefix+'" data-lot="'+lotId+'" style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:6px;padding:6px 8px;border:1px solid #d4dcd6;border-radius:8px;background:#f7faf8">'
    +'<div style="flex:1"><b>'+esc(lot.code)+'</b><br><small>'+esc((p.code||'')+' — '+(p.name||''))+' · të lira '+fmt2(free)+' kg</small></div>'
    +'<input type="number" step="0.001" min="0" '+(qty===''?'':('value="'+fmt2(+qty||0)+'"'))+' data-pack-qty oninput="packSourceSum(\''+prefix+'\')" style="width:120px"> <span>kg</span>'
    +'<button type="button" class="btn small danger" onclick="packSourceRemoveRow(this,\''+prefix+'\')">✕</button></div>';
}
function packSourceAddOptions(prefix,productId,oldMap,selected){
  let chosen=new Set((selected||[]).map(z=>z.lot||z));
  let lots=packagingCandidateLots(productId).filter(l=>!chosen.has(l.id));
  return '<option value="">Shto një lot…</option>'+lots.map(l=>'<option value="'+l.id+'">'+esc(l.code+' — '+(by('products',l.product)?by('products',l.product).name:'')+' — të lira '+fmt2(lotUnpacked(l)+(((+oldMap[l.id])||0)))+' kg')+'</option>').join('');
}
function packSourcePickerHtml(prefix,selected,productId,packId){
  selected=selected||[];
  let oldMap={};if(packId){let ex=packById(packId);(ex&&ex.sourceLots||[]).forEach(id=>oldMap[id]=packLotQty(ex,id))}
  return '<div class="field full" data-pack-source="'+prefix+'" data-pack-bags="'+prefix+'Bags" data-pack-bw="'+prefix+'BagWeight" data-pack-old=\''+esc(JSON.stringify(oldMap))+'\'>'
    +'<label>Lotet burimorë nga magazina * <small>(një ose më shumë — përzierje shumë-lotëshe)</small></label>'
    +'<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-bottom:6px"><select id="'+prefix+'AddLot" style="flex:1">'+packSourceAddOptions(prefix,productId,oldMap,selected)+'</select>'
    +'<button type="button" class="btn small" onclick="packSourceAddRow(\''+prefix+'\')">+ Shto lot</button></div>'
    +'<div data-pack-rows="'+prefix+'">'+selected.map(z=>packSourceRowHtml(prefix,z.lot,z.qty,oldMap)).join('')+'</div>'
    +'<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap"><button type="button" class="btn small" onclick="packSourceDistribute(\''+prefix+'\')">Shpërnda totalin njëlloj</button><p class="hint" data-pack-sum="'+prefix+'" style="margin:0"></p></div></div>';
}
function packSourceRefreshAdd(prefix,productId){
  let sel=document.getElementById(prefix+'AddLot'),c=packSourceContainer(prefix);if(!sel||!c)return;
  sel.innerHTML=packSourceAddOptions(prefix,productId,packSourceOldMap(prefix),packSourceRows(prefix).map(r=>({lot:r.dataset.lot})));
}
function packSourceAddRow(prefix){
  let sel=document.getElementById(prefix+'AddLot');let lotId=sel?sel.value:'';if(!lotId)return;
  let rows=packSourceRows(prefix);
  if(rows.some(r=>r.dataset.lot===lotId))return toast('Ai lot është tashmë në listë');
  let c=packSourceContainer(prefix),oldMap=packSourceOldMap(prefix),total=packSourceTarget(prefix);
  if(rows.length===1){let q1=parseFloat(rows[0].querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0;if(Math.abs(q1-total)<=.01){let half=Math.round(total/2*1000)/1000;rows[0].querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value=fmt2(half)}}
  let cur=packSourceRows(prefix).reduce((a,r)=>a+(parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0),0);
  let rest=Math.round((total-cur)*1000)/1000;
  c.querySelector('[data-pack-rows="'+prefix+'"]').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',packSourceRowHtml(prefix,lotId,rest>0?rest:'',oldMap));
  sel.value='';
  let lot=by('lots',lotId),prod=document.getElementById(prefix+'Product');
  if(lot&&lot.id&&prod&&!prod.value){try{fillSearchSelect(prefix+'Product',lot.product)}catch(e){prod.value=lot.product}}
  packSourceRefreshAdd(prefix,prod?prod.value:'');
  packSourceSum(prefix);
}
function packSourceRemoveRow(btn,prefix){btn.closest('[data-pack-source-row]')?.remove();let prod=document.getElementById(prefix+'Product');packSourceRefreshAdd(prefix,prod?prod.value:'');packSourceSum(prefix)}
function packSourceDistribute(prefix){
  let rows=packSourceRows(prefix),total=packSourceTarget(prefix);if(!rows.length||!(total>0))return;
  let each=Math.floor(total/rows.length*1000)/1000;
  rows.forEach((r,i)=>{r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value=fmt2(i===rows.length-1?total-each*(rows.length-1):each)});
  packSourceSum(prefix);
}
function packSourceSum(prefix){
  let c=packSourceContainer(prefix);if(!c)return;let rows=packSourceRows(prefix);
  let total=packSourceTarget(prefix);
  if(rows.length===1&&total>0)rows[0].querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value=fmt2(total);
  let sum=rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0),0),diff=total-sum;
  let el=c.querySelector('[data-pack-sum="'+prefix+'"]');
  if(el)el.innerHTML='Nga lotet: <b>'+fmt2(sum)+' kg</b> / duhet '+fmt2(total)+' kg · '+(Math.abs(diff)<=.01?'<b style="color:#1e7e34">✓ balancuar</b>':'<b style="color:#b63c46">diferencë '+fmt2(diff)+' kg</b>')+(rows.length>1?' · <span class="pill orange">përzierje '+rows.length+' lotësh</span>':'');
}
function readPackSourceRows(prefix){return packSourceRows(prefix).map(r=>({lot:r.dataset.lot,qty:parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0}))}
function collectPackSources(prefix,totalNet,productId,oldMap){
  let rows=readPackSourceRows(prefix);
  if(!rows.length)return{error:'Zgjidhni të paktën një lot burimor'};
  let ids=[],qtys={},sum=0,seen=new Set();
  for(let r of rows){
    let lot=by('lots',r.lot);if(!lot||!lot.id)return{error:'Loti burimor nuk u gjet'};
    if(seen.has(lot.id))return{error:'Loti '+lot.code+' është dy herë në listë'};seen.add(lot.id);
    if(!(r.qty>0))return{error:'Sasia për lotin '+lot.code+' duhet të jetë pozitive'};
    let cap=lotUnpacked(lot)+(((oldMap||{})[lot.id]!=null?+(oldMap[lot.id]):0));
    if(r.qty>cap+.001)return{error:'Tejkalim stoku te loti '+lot.code+': të lira '+fmt2(cap)+' kg'};
    if(productId&&lot.product!==productId)return{error:'Loti '+lot.code+' është i produktit tjetër'};
    let q=Math.round(r.qty*1000)/1000;qtys[lot.id]=q;ids.push(lot.id);sum+=q;
  }
  if(Math.abs(sum-totalNet)>.01)return{error:'Sasia nga lotet ('+fmt2(sum)+' kg) duhet të jetë '+fmt2(totalNet)+' kg'};
  return{sources:ids.map(id=>({lot:id,qty:qtys[id]})),qtys,ids};
}
function packNet(x){let v=+(x&&x.net);if(Number.isFinite(v)&&v>0)return v;return (+x.bags||0)*(+x.bagWeight||0)}
function packLinkedShipments(x){return (state.shipments||[]).filter(s=>s&&s.order===x.order)}
function packLinkedInvoices(x){return salesInvoices().filter(f=>f.status==='Konfirmuar'&&f.order===x.order&&saleLinesOf(f).some(l=>!x.product||!l.product||l.product===x.product))}

function packBlockingReasons(x){
  if(!x)return['Paketimi nuk u gjet'];
  let out=[],shipped=['Ngarkuar','Vulosur','Dokumentuar','Nisur'];
  packLinkedShipments(x).forEach(s=>{let st=normalizedShipmentStatus(s);if(shipped.includes(st))out.push('ngarkesa '+s.id+' është "'+st+'"')});
  packLinkedInvoices(x).forEach(f=>out.push('fatura e konfirmuar '+(f.invoiceNumber||f.id)));
  return out;
}

/* ---------------------------- lista e Paketimit ---------------------------- */
views.packaging=function(){
  let a=packs();
  return head('Paketimi & Etiketat','Lotet përfundimtare dhe skedarët për BarTender — redaktim dhe fshirje direkte',
    `<button class="btn primary" onclick="packagingForm()">+ Paketim i ri</button>`)
    +tableCard(['Paketimi','Porosia','Produkti','Burime','Loti BioBes','Loti klientit','Thasë','Neto','Modeli','Veprime'],
      a.map(x=>{
        let block=packBlockingReasons(x);
        return [`<a class="link" onclick="packagingCard('${x.id}')">${x.id}</a>`,
          `<a class="link" onclick="orderCard('${x.order}')">${x.order}</a>`,
          esc(by('products',x.product).name||'—'),
          (()=>{let ls=packSourceLots(x);return ls.length?ls.length+' lot ('+ls.map(l=>l.code).join('+')+')':'—'})(),
          esc(x.internalLot||'—'),
          esc(x.customerLot||'—'),
          x.bags,
          n(packNet(x))+' kg',
          esc(x.template||'—'),
          `<span class="row-actions">`
            +`<button class="btn small" onclick="packagingCard('${x.id}')">Hap</button>`
            +`<button class="btn small" onclick="packagingEditForm('${x.id}')">Ndrysho</button>`
            +`<button class="btn small danger" data-admin-only onclick="packagingDelete('${x.id}')">Fshi</button>`
            +(block.length?`<small class="hint" title="${esc(block.join('; '))}">🔒 e lidhur</small>`:'')
          +`</span>`];
      }));
};

/* ------------------------- kartela me Ndrysho / Fshi ----------------------- */
const packagingCardReadOnly=packagingCard;
packagingCard=function(id){
  packagingCardReadOnly(id);
  let x=packById(id);
  if(!x)return;
  try{
    let foot=document.getElementById('modalFoot');
    if(foot&&!foot.querySelector('[data-pack-actions]')){
      foot.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
        `<span data-pack-actions style="display:contents">`
        +`<button class="btn" onclick="packagingEditForm('${id}')">✎ Ndrysho</button>`
        +`<button class="btn danger" data-admin-only onclick="packagingDelete('${id}')">Fshi</button>`
        +`</span>`);
    }
  }catch(e){console.error(e)}
};

/* ------------------------------ formulari i editimit ---------------------- */
function packagingEditForm(id){
  let x=packById(id);
  if(!x)return toast('Paketimi nuk u gjet');
  let block=packBlockingReasons(x);
  let labelOnly=block.length>0;
  let lot=packSourceLot(x),pick=(vals,cur)=>vals.map(v=>`<option ${String(cur)===String(v)?'selected':''}>${esc(v)}</option>`).join('');
  modal('Ndrysho paketimin — '+x.id,`<div class="form-grid">${labelOnly?`<div class="field full" style="background:#fdf7ec;border:1px solid #e8c98f;border-radius:8px;padding:10px"><b>⚠ E lidhur me dokumente të konfirmuara:</b> ${esc(block.join('; '))}<br><small>Sasia dhe lotet nuk ndryshohen — lejohet vetëm ndryshimi i fushave të etiketës.</small></div>`:''}
    <div class="field"><label>Porosia</label><select id="peOrder">${opts(state.orders,o=>o.id+' — '+by('customers',o.customer).name,x.order)}</select></div>
    ${packSourcePickerHtml('pe',x.sourceLots.map(id=>({lot:id,qty:packLotQty(x,id)})),x.product,id)}
    <div class="field"><label>Produkti</label><select id="peProduct">${opts(state.products,p=>p.code+' — '+p.name,x.product)}</select></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Loti përfundimtar BioBes</label><input id="peInternal" value="${esc(x.internalLot)}" placeholder="B2W01F/1a-II-115-26"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Loti i klientit</label><input id="peCustomer" value="${esc(x.customerLot||'')}" placeholder="NCH_LOT_R.fru_A_160"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Modeli i etiketës</label><select id="peTemplate">${pick(['BioBes standard','Etiketë klienti'],x.template)}</select></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Nr. thasëve</label><input id="peBags" type="number" value="${esc(x.bags)}"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Pesha neto/thes kg</label><input id="peBagWeight" type="number" step="0.01" value="${esc(x.bagWeight)}"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Viti i prodhimit</label><input id="peCrop" type="number" value="${esc(x.cropYear)}"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Best before</label><input id="peBest" type="number" value="${esc(x.bestBefore)}"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Certifikimi</label><input id="peCert" value="${esc(x.cert)}"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Statusi</label><select id="peOrganic">${pick(['Organik','Konvencional'],x.organic)}</select></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Origjina</label><select id="peOriginType">${pick(['Wild collected','Cultivated'],x.originType)}</select></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Pjesa e bimës</label><select id="pePart">${pick(['Folia','Herba','Flores','Radix','Fructus'],x.part)}</select></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Vendi i origjinës</label><input id="peOrigin" value="${esc(x.origin)}"></div>
    <div class="field full"><p class="hint">Neto aktuale: <b>${n(packNet(x))} kg</b> nga <b>${packSourceLots(x).length}</b> lot(ë) burimor(ë): ${packSourceLots(x).map(l=>esc(l.code)+' ('+fmt2(packLotQty(x,l.id))+' kg)').join(', ')||'—'}. Ndryshimi i sasive ose i loteve rregullon automatikisht gjendjen e magazinës për çdo lot.</p></div>
  </div>`,`<button class="btn" onclick="packagingCard('${id}')">Mbyll</button><button class="btn primary" onclick="savePackagingEdit('${id}')">Ruaj ndryshimet</button>`);
  setTimeout(()=>{
    try{
      enhanceEntitySelects();
      let prod=document.getElementById('peProduct');
      prod?.addEventListener('change',()=>packSourceRefreshAdd('pe',prod.value));
      ['peBags','peBagWeight'].forEach(z=>document.getElementById(z)?.addEventListener('input',()=>packSourceSum('pe')));
      if(labelOnly){
        let c=packSourceContainer('pe');
        if(c){c.querySelectorAll('input[data-pack-qty]').forEach(i=>i.disabled=true);c.querySelectorAll('select,button').forEach(b=>b.style.display='none')}
        ['peBags','peBagWeight'].forEach(z=>{let el=document.getElementById(z);if(el)el.disabled=true});
      }
      packSourceSum('pe');
    }catch(e){console.error(e)}
  },30);
}

/* ------------------------------- ruajtja e editimit ------------------------ */
function savePackagingEdit(id){
  try{
    let x=packById(id);
    if(!x)return toast('Paketimi nuk u gjet');
    let block=packBlockingReasons(x);
    if(block.length){
      let internalLo=$val('peInternal').trim();
      if(!internalLo)return toast('Plotësoni lotin BioBes');
      Object.assign(x,{internalLot:internalLo,customerLot:$val('peCustomer').trim(),template:$val('peTemplate','BioBes standard'),cropYear:$num('peCrop',new Date().getFullYear()),bestBefore:$num('peBest',new Date().getFullYear()+3),cert:$val('peCert'),organic:$val('peOrganic'),originType:$val('peOriginType'),part:$val('pePart'),origin:$val('peOrigin','Albania'),updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()});
      state.events.push({date:new Date().toLocaleString('sq-AL'),type:'Ndryshim etikete',ref:id,text:id+': u përditësuan vetëm fushat e etiketës (paketim i lidhur me dokumente të konfirmuara)'});
      save();closeModal();render();setTimeout(()=>packagingCard(id),80);
      toast('Fushat e etiketës u ruajtën — sasia/loti u mban siç është');
      return;
    }
    let order=$val('peOrder'),product=$val('peProduct'),internal=$val('peInternal').trim(),o=by('orders',order);
    if(!o.id||!product||!internal)return toast('Plotësoni porosinë, produktin dhe lotin BioBes');
    let bags=$num('peBags',0),bagWeight=$num('peBagWeight',0),newNet=bags*bagWeight;
    if(!(bags>0)||!(bagWeight>0))return toast('Nr. i thasëve dhe pesha neto/thes duhet të jenë më të mëdha se zero');
    let oldMap={};packSourceLots(x).forEach(l=>oldMap[l.id]=packLotQty(x,l.id));
    let coll=collectPackSources('pe',newNet,product,oldMap);
    if(coll.error)return toast(coll.error);
    let oldNet=packNet(x);
    let r=atomicTransaction('Ndryshim paketimi '+id,()=>{
      packSourceLots(x).forEach(l=>{let q=packLotQty(x,l.id);
        l.unpackedNet=lotUnpacked(l)+q;
        l.packedNet=Math.max(0,(+l.packedNet||0)-q)});
      coll.sources.forEach(z=>{let l=by('lots',z.lot);if(!l||!l.id)throw new Error('Loti burimor nuk u gjet');
        if(z.qty>lotUnpacked(l)+.001)throw new Error('Paketimi tejkalon stokun e papaketuar te loti '+l.code);
        l.unpackedNet=lotUnpacked(l)-z.qty;
        l.packedNet=(+l.packedNet||0)+z.qty});
      Object.assign(x,{
        order:order,customer:o.customer,product:product,sourceLots:coll.ids,sourceLotQtys:coll.qtys,
        internalLot:internal,customerLot:$val('peCustomer').trim(),
        template:$val('peTemplate','BioBes standard'),bags:bags,bagWeight:bagWeight,
        cropYear:$num('peCrop',new Date().getFullYear()),bestBefore:$num('peBest',new Date().getFullYear()+3),
        cert:$val('peCert'),organic:$val('peOrganic'),originType:$val('peOriginType'),part:$val('pePart'),
        origin:$val('peOrigin','Albania'),net:

### lotUnpacked(lot) @ 1731562
urn JSON.parse(packSourceContainer(prefix)?.dataset.packOld||'{}')}catch(e){return{}}}

}

}

  let rows=packSourceRows(prefix),total=packSourceTarget(prefix);if(!rows.length||!(total>0))return;
  let each=Math.floor(total/rows.length*1000)/1000;
  rows.forEach((r,i)=>{r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value=fmt2(i===rows.length-1?total-each*(rows.length-1):each)});
  packSourceSum(prefix);
}
function packSourceSum(prefix){
  let c=packSourceContainer(prefix);if(!c)return;let rows=packSourceRows(prefix);
  let total=packSourceTarget(prefix);
  if(rows.length===1&&total>0)rows[0].querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value=fmt2(total);
  let sum=rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0),0),diff=total-sum;
  let el=c.querySelector('[data-pack-sum="'+prefix+'"]');
  if(el)el.innerHTML='Nga lotet: <b>'+fmt2(sum)+' kg</b> / duhet '+fmt2(total)+' kg · '+(Math.abs(diff)<=.01?'<b style="color:#1e7e34">✓ balancuar</b>':'<b style="color:#b63c46">diferencë '+fmt2(diff)+' kg</b>')+(rows.length>1?' · <span class="pill orange">përzierje '+rows.length+' lotësh</span>':'');
}
function readPackSourceRows(prefix){return packSourceRows(prefix).map(r=>({lot:r.dataset.lot,qty:parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0}))}
function collectPackSources(prefix,totalNet,productId,oldMap){
  let rows=readPackSourceRows(prefix);
  if(!rows.length)return{error:'Zgjidhni të paktën një lot burimor'};
  let ids=[],qtys={},sum=0,seen=new Set();
  for(let r of rows){
    let lot=by('lots',r.lot);if(!lot||!lot.id)return{error:'Loti burimor nuk u gjet'};
    if(seen.has(lot.id))return{error:'Loti '+lot.code+' është dy herë në listë'};seen.add(lot.id);
    if(!(r.qty>0))return{error:'Sasia për lotin '+lot.code+' duhet të jetë pozitive'};
    let cap=lotUnpacked(lot)+(((oldMap||{})[lot.id]!=null?+(oldMap[lot.id]):0));
    if(r.qty>cap+.001)return{error:'Tejkalim stoku te loti '+lot.code+': të lira '+fmt2(cap)+' kg'};
    if(productId&&lot.product!==productId)return{error:'Loti '+lot.code+' është i produktit tjetër'};
    let q=Math.round(r.qty*1000)/1000;qtys[lot.id]=q;ids.push(lot.id);sum+=q;
  }
  if(Math.abs(sum-totalNet)>.01)return{error:'Sasia nga lotet ('+fmt2(sum)+' kg) duhet të jetë '+fmt2(totalNet)+' kg'};
  return{sources:ids.map(id=>({lot:id,qty:qtys[id]})),qtys,ids};
}
function packNet(x){let v=+(x&&x.net);if(Number.isFinite(v)&&v>0)return v;return (+x.bags||0)*(+x.bagWeight||0)}
function packLinkedShipments(x){return (state.shipments||[]).filter(s=>s&&s.order===x.order)}
function packLinkedInvoices(x){return salesInvoices().filter(f=>f.status==='Konfirmuar'&&f.order===x.order&&saleLinesOf(f).some(l=>!x.product||!l.product||l.product===x.product))}

function packBlockingReasons(x){
  if(!x)return['Paketimi nuk u gjet'];
  let out=[],shipped=['Ngarkuar','Vulosur','Dokumentuar','Nisur'];
  packLinkedShipments(x).forEach(s=>{let st=normalizedShipmentStatus(s);if(shipped.includes(st))out.push('ngarkesa '+s.id+' është "'+st+'"')});
  packLinkedInvoices(x).forEach(f=>out.push('fatura e konfirmuar '+(f.invoiceNumber||f.id)));
  return out;
}

/* ---------------------------- lista e Paketimit ---------------------------- */
views.packaging=function(){
  let a=packs();
  return head('Paketimi & Etiketat','Lotet përfundimtare dhe skedarët për BarTender — redaktim dhe fshirje direkte',
    `<button class="btn primary" onclick="packagingForm()">+ Paketim i ri</button>`)
    +tableCard(['Paketimi','Porosia','Produkti','Burime','Loti BioBes','Loti klientit','Thasë','Neto','Modeli','Veprime'],
      a.map(x=>{
        let block=packBlockingReasons(x);
        return [`<a class="link" onclick="packagingCard('${x.id}')">${x.id}</a>`,
          `<a class="link" onclick="orderCard('${x.order}')">${x.order}</a>`,
          esc(by('products',x.product).name||'—'),
          (()=>{let ls=packSourceLots(x);return ls.length?ls.length+' lot ('+ls.map(l=>l.code).join('+')+')':'—'})(),
          esc(x.internalLot||'—'),
          esc(x.customerLot||'—'),
          x.bags,
          n(packNet(x))+' kg',
          esc(x.template||'—'),
          `<span class="row-actions">`
            +`<button class="btn small" onclick="packagingCard('${x.id}')">Hap</button>`
            +`<button class="btn small" onclick="packagingEditForm('${x.id}')">Ndrysho</button>`
            +`<button class="btn small danger" data-admin-only onclick="packagingDelete('${x.id}')">Fshi</button>`
            +(block.length?`<small class="hint" title="${esc(block.join('; '))}">🔒 e lidhur</small>`:'')
          +`</span>`];
      }));
};

/* ------------------------- kartela me Ndrysho / Fshi ----------------------- */
const packagingCardReadOnly=packagingCard;
packagingCard=function(id){
  packagingCardReadOnly(id);
  let x=packById(id);
  if(!x)return;
  try{
    let foot=document.getElementById('modalFoot');
    if(foot&&!foot.querySelector('[data-pack-actions]')){
      foot.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
        `<span data-pack-actions style="display:contents">`
        +`<button class="btn" onclick="packagingEditForm('${id}')">✎ Ndrysho</button>`
        +`<button class="btn danger" data-admin-only onclick="packagingDelete('${id}')">Fshi</button>`
        +`</span>`);
    }
  }catch(e){console.error(e)}
};

/* ------------------------------ formulari i editimit ---------------------- */
function packagingEditForm(id){
  let x=packById(id);
  if(!x)return toast('Paketimi nuk u gjet');
  let block=packBlockingReasons(x);
  let labelOnly=block.length>0;
  let lot=packSourceLot(x),pick=(vals,cur)=>vals.map(v=>`<option ${String(cur)===String(v)?'selected':''}>${esc(v)}</option>`).join('');
  modal('Ndrysho paketimin — '+x.id,`<div class="form-grid">${labelOnly?`<div class="field full" style="background:#fdf7ec;border:1px solid #e8c98f;border-radius:8px;padding:10px"><b>⚠ E lidhur me dokumente të konfirmuara:</b> ${esc(block.join('; '))}<br><small>Sasia dhe lotet nuk ndryshohen — lejohet vetëm ndryshimi i fushave të etiketës.</small></div>`:''}
    <div class="field"><label>Porosia</label><select id="peOrder">${opts(state.orders,o=>o.id+' — '+by('customers',o.customer).name,x.order)}</select></div>
    ${packSourcePickerHtml('pe',x.sourceLots.map(id=>({lot:id,qty:packLotQty(x,id)})),x.product,id)}
    <div class="field"><label>Produkti</label><select id="peProduct">${opts(state.products,p=>p.code+' — '+p.name,x.product)}</select></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Loti përfundimtar BioBes</label><input id="peInternal" value="${esc(x.internalLot)}" placeholder="B2W01F/1a-II-115-26"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Loti i klientit</label><input id="peCustomer" value="${esc(x.customerLot||'')}" placeholder="NCH_LOT_R.fru_A_160"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Modeli i etiketës</label><select id="peTemplate">${pick(['BioBes standard','Etiketë klienti'],x.template)}</select></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Nr. thasëve</label><input id="peBags" type="number" value="${esc(x.bags)}"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Pesha neto/thes kg</label><input id="peBagWeight" type="number" step="0.01" value="${esc(x.bagWeight)}"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Viti i prodhimit</label><input id="peCrop" type="number" value="${esc(x.cropYear)}"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Best before</label><input id="peBest" type="number" value="${esc(x.bestBefore)}"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Certifikimi</label><input id="peCert" value="${esc(x.cert)}"></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Statusi</label><select id="peOrganic">${pick(['Organik','Konvencional'],x.organic)}</select></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Origjina</label><select id="peOriginType">${pick(['Wild collected','Cultivated'],x.originType)}</select></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Pjesa e bimës</label><select id="pePart">${pick(['Folia','Herba','Flores','Radix','Fructus'],x.part)}</select></div>
    <div class="field"><label>Vendi i origjinës</label><input id="peOrigin" value="${esc(x.origin)}"></div>
    <div class="field full"><p class="hint">Neto aktuale: <b>${n(packNet(x))} kg</b> nga <b>${packSourceLots(x).length}</b> lot(ë) burimor(ë): ${packSourceLots(x).map(l=>esc(l.code)+' ('+fmt2(packLotQty(x,l.id))+' kg)').join(', ')||'—'}. Ndryshimi i sasive ose i loteve rregullon automatikisht gjendjen e magazinës për çdo lot.</p></div>
  </div>`,`<button class="btn" onclick="packagingCard('${id}')">Mbyll</button><button class="btn primary" onclick="savePackagingEdit('${id}')">Ruaj ndryshimet</button>`);
  setTimeout(()=>{
    try{
      enhanceEntitySelects();
      let prod=document.getElementById('peProduct');
      prod?.addEventListener('change',()=>packSourceRefreshAdd('pe',prod.value));
      ['peBags','peBagWeight'].forEach(z=>document.getElementById(z)?.addEventListener('input',()=>packSourceSum('pe')));
      if(labelOnly){
        let c=packSourceContainer('pe');
        if(c){c.querySelectorAll('input[data-pack-qty]').forEach(i=>i.disabled=true);c.querySelectorAll('select,button').forEach(b=>b.style.display='none')}
        ['peBags','peBagWeight'].forEach(z=>{let el=document.getElementById(z);if(el)el.disabled=true});
      }
      packSourceSum('pe');
    }catch(e){console.error(e)}
  },30);
}

/* ------------------------------- ruajtja e editimit ------------------------ */
function savePackagingEdit(id){
  try{
    let x=packById(id);
    if(!x)return toast('Paketimi nuk u gjet');
    let block=packBlockingReasons(x);
    if(block.length){
      let internalLo=$val('peInternal').trim();
      if(!internalLo)return toast('Plotësoni lotin BioBes');
      Object.assign(x,{internalLot:internalLo,customerLot:$val('peCustomer').trim(),template:$val('peTemplate','BioBes standard'),cropYear:$num('peCrop',new Date().getFullYear()),bestBefore:$num('peBest',new Date().getFullYear()+3),cert:$val('peCert'),organic:$val('peOrganic'),originType:$val('peOriginType'),part:$val('pePart'),origin:$val('peOrigin','Albania'),updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()});
      state.events.push({date:new Date().toLocaleString('sq-AL'),type:'Ndryshim etikete',ref:id,text:id+': u përditësuan vetëm fushat e etiketës (paketim i lidhur me dokumente të konfirmuara)'});
      save();closeModal();render();setTimeout(()=>packagingCard(id),80);
      toast('Fushat e etiketës u ruajtën — sasia/loti u mban siç është');
      return;
    }
    let order=$val('peOrder'),product=$val('peProduct'),internal=$val('peInternal').trim(),o=by('orders',order);
    if(!o.id||!product||!internal)return toast('Plotësoni porosinë, produktin dhe lotin BioBes');
    let bags=$num('peBags',0),bagWeight=$num('peBagWeight',0),newNet=bags*bagWeight;
    if(!(bags>0)||!(bagWeight>0))return toast('Nr. i thasëve dhe pesha neto/thes duhet të jenë më të mëdha se zero');
    let oldMap={};packSourceLots(x).forEach(l=>oldMap[l.id]=packLotQty(x,l.id));
    let coll=collectPackSources('pe',newNet,product,oldMap);
    if(coll.error)return toast(coll.error);
    let oldNet=packNet(x);
    let r=atomicTransaction('Ndryshim paketimi '+id,()=>{
      packSourceLots(x).forEach(l=>{let q=packLotQty(x,l.id);
        l.unpackedNet=lotUnpacked(l)+q;
        l.packedNet=Math.max(0,(+l.packedNet||0)-q)});
      coll.sources.forEach(z=>{let l=by('lots',z.lot);if(!l||!l.id)throw new Error('Loti burimor nuk u gjet');
        if(z.qty>lotUnpacked(l)+.001)throw new Error('Paketimi tejkalon stokun e papaketuar te loti '+l.code);
        l.unpackedNet=lotUnpacked(l)-z.qty;
        l.packedNet=(+l.packedNet||0)+z.qty});
      Object.assign(x,{
        order:order,customer:o.customer,product:product,sourceLots:coll.ids,sourceLotQtys:coll.qtys,
        internalLot:internal,customerLot:$val('peCustomer').trim(),
        template:$val('peTemplate','BioBes standard'),bags:bags,bagWeight:bagWeight,
        cropYear:$num('peCrop',new Date().getFullYear()),bestBefore:$num('peBest',new Date().getFullYear()+3),
        cert:$val('peCert'),organic:$val('peOrganic'),originType:$val('peOriginType'),part:$val('pePart'),
        origin:$val('peOrigin','Albania'),net:newNet,qty:newNet,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()
      });
      let oldMoves=packStockMoves(x),used=new Set();
      coll.sources.forEach(z=>{
        let l=by('lots',z.lot),key=(coll.sources.length===1?'PACK:'+id:'PACK:'+id+':'+l.id),
            mv=oldMoves.find(m=>!used.has(m.id)&&m.lot===l.id)||oldMoves.find(m=>!used.has(m.id));
        if(mv){used.add(mv.id);mv.key=key;mv.lot=l.id;mv.product=l.product;mv.warehouse=l.warehouse;
          mv.stateQty=z.qty;mv.unitCost=l.unitCost;mv.date=x.date}
        else coreMove({key,type:'TRANSFER',source:'packaging',sourceId:id,lot:l.id,product:l.product,
          warehouse:l.warehouse,qty:0,fromState:'unpacked',toState:'packed',stateQty:z.qty,
          unitCost:l.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId})});
      oldMoves.filter(m=>!used.has(m.id)).forEach(mv=>{mv.status='Anuluar';
        coreMove({key:'REV:'+mv.key+':'+stockMoves().length,type:'TRANSFER',source:'reversal',sourceId:id,
          lot:mv.lot,product:mv.product,warehouse:mv.warehouse,qty:0,fromState:'packed',toState:'unpacked',
          stateQty:+(mv.stateQty||0),unitCost:mv.unitCost,reversalOf:mv.id,
          date:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),testRunId:x.testRunId})});
      state.events.push({date:new Date().toLocaleString('sq-AL'),type:'Ndryshim paketimi',ref:id,
        text:`${id}: ${fmt2(oldNet)} kg → ${fmt2(newNet)} kg; lotët: ${coll.sources.map(z=>(by('lots',z.lot)?.code||'?')+' '+fmt2(z.qty)+' kg').join(', ')}`});
      return coreResult(true,'',x);
    });
    if(!r||!r.ok)return toast((r&&r.error)||'Ndryshimi dështoi');
    save();closeModal();render();
    setTimeout(()=>packagingCard(id),80);
    toast('Paketimi u përditësua');
  }catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Gabim gjatë ruajtjes: '+e.message)}
}

/* --------------------------------- fshirja -------------------------------- */
function packagingDelete(id){
  if(!requireAdmin('fshijë paketimin'))return;
  let x=packById(id);
  if(!x)return toast('Paketimi nuk u gjet');
  let block=packBlockingReasons(x);
  if(block.length)return toast('Paketimi nuk fshihet sepse është i lidhur me: '+block.join('; '));
  let reason=prompt('ADMIN — arsyeja e fshirjes së paketimit '+id+':');
  if(!reason||!String(reason).trim())return toast('Arsyeja e fshirjes është e detyrueshme');
  if(!confirm('Ta fshini paketimin '+id+'? '+fmt2(packNet(x))+' kg do të kthehen te lotet burimorë ('+packSourceLots(x).map(l=>l.code).join(', ')+').'))return;
  let net=packNet(x);
  let r=atomicTransaction('Fshirje paketimi '+id,()=>{
    packSourceLots(x).forEach(lot=>{let q=packLotQty(x,lot.id);if(!q)return;
      lot.unpackedNet=lotUnpacked(lot)+q;
      lot.packedNet=Math.max(0,(+lot.packedNet||0)-q)});
    packStockMoves(x).forEach(mv=>{
      mv.status='Anuluar';
      coreMove({key:'REV:'+mv.key+':'+stockMoves().length,type:'TRANSFER',source:'reversal',sourceId:id,lot:mv.lot,product:mv.product,
        warehouse:mv.warehouse,qty:0,fromState:'packed',toState:'unpacked',stateQty:+(mv.stateQty||net),unitCost:mv.unitCost,
        reversalOf:mv.id,date:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),testRunId:x.testRunId})});
    state.packagings=(state.packagings||[]).filter(p=>p.id!==id);
    state.events.push({date:new Date().toLocaleString('sq-AL'),type:'Fshirje paketimi',ref:id,text:String(reason).trim()});
    try{securityAudit('PACKAGING_DELETED',id+': '+String(reason).trim())}catch(e){}
    return coreResult(true,'',x);
  });
  if(!r||!r.ok)return toast((r&&r.error)||'Fshirja dështoi');
  save();closeModal();render();
  toast('Paketimi '+id+' u fshi — '+fmt2(net)+' kg u kthyen në stok');
}
</script>
<script id="biobes-multilot-v2">
/* ============ Shumë-lotësh v2 — Paketim / Ngarkesë / Përpunim / Eksport (2026-09-11) ============
   - Paketim: fushë e gjerë "KG nga ky lot", gjithmonë e editueshme, placeholder 0.00, buton X në
     kolonë më vete, shpërndarje njëlloj me saktësi miligrami dhe rrillogaritje live e totalit/diferencës.
   - Ngarkesa: 2-3 porosi të të njëjtit klient në një kontejner (përzgjedhës shto/hiq), klient auto,
     neto/thasë auto nga paketimet, zbritje stoku për të gjitha lotet; "Krijo Ngarkesë" nga porositë
     e shton porosinë automatikisht.
   - Përpunim: disa lote lënde të parë me kg për lot (hyrje auto si shumë, readonly, e shpërndarë
     njëlloj); coreProcess bën një lëvizje OUT për lot dhe një IN; loti dalës me kosto mesatare të
     ponderuar dhe sourceLots; refuzohen tejkalimi i stokut, produkti tjetër dhe loti i përsëritur.
   - Eksport: dosja bashkon porositë/faturat e kontejnerit dhe tregon manifestin e ngarkimit
     (produkt, lot BioBes, lote burimore, kg, thasë, neto).
   - Prapëpërputhshmëri e plotë me të dhënat e vjetra një-porosi/një-lot; të gjitha tekstet në shqip. */

/* ---------- saktësia miligram & shpërndarja njëlloj ---------- */
function mgRound(v){let r=Math.round((+v||0)*1e6)/1e6;return Object.is(r,-0)?0:r}
function mgStr(v){let s=mgRound(v).toFixed(6).replace(/\.?0+$/,'');return s===''||s==='-'?'0':s}
function distributeEvenMg(total,count){total=mgRound(total);count=Math.max(1,Math.floor(count)||1);if(count===1)return[total];let each=Math.floor(total/count*1e6)/1e6,out=[];for(let i=0;i<count;i++)out.push(i===count-1?mgRound(total-each*(count-1)):each);return out}

/* ---------- Paketim: rreshtat e loteve burimore (KG e gjerë, gjithmonë e editueshme) ---------- */
function packSourceRowHtml(prefix,lotId,qty,oldMap){
  oldMap=oldMap||{};let lot=by('lots',lotId);if(!lot||!lot.id)return'';
  let p=by('products',lot.product)||{},free=lotUnpacked(lot)+(((+oldMap[lotId])||0));
  return '<div data-pack-source-row="'+prefix+'" data-lot="'+lotId+'" style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:6px;padding:6px 8px;border:1px sol

### lotUnpacked(lot) @ 1744451
'pe',prod.value));
      ['peBags','peBagWeight'].forEach(z=>document.getElementById(z)?.addEventListener('input',()=>packSourceSum('pe')));
      if(labelOnly){
        let c=packSourceContainer('pe');
        if(c){c.querySelectorAll('input[data-pack-qty]').forEach(i=>i.disabled=true);c.querySelectorAll('select,button').forEach(b=>b.style.display='none')}
        ['peBags','peBagWeight'].forEach(z=>{let el=document.getElementById(z);if(el)el.disabled=true});
      }
      packSourceSum('pe');
    }catch(e){console.error(e)}
  },30);
}

/* ------------------------------- ruajtja e editimit ------------------------ */
function savePackagingEdit(id){
  try{
    let x=packById(id);
    if(!x)return toast('Paketimi nuk u gjet');
    let block=packBlockingReasons(x);
    if(block.length){
      let internalLo=$val('peInternal').trim();
      if(!internalLo)return toast('Plotësoni lotin BioBes');
      Object.assign(x,{internalLot:internalLo,customerLot:$val('peCustomer').trim(),template:$val('peTemplate','BioBes standard'),cropYear:$num('peCrop',new Date().getFullYear()),bestBefore:$num('peBest',new Date().getFullYear()+3),cert:$val('peCert'),organic:$val('peOrganic'),originType:$val('peOriginType'),part:$val('pePart'),origin:$val('peOrigin','Albania'),updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()});
      state.events.push({date:new Date().toLocaleString('sq-AL'),type:'Ndryshim etikete',ref:id,text:id+': u përditësuan vetëm fushat e etiketës (paketim i lidhur me dokumente të konfirmuara)'});
      save();closeModal();render();setTimeout(()=>packagingCard(id),80);
      toast('Fushat e etiketës u ruajtën — sasia/loti u mban siç është');
      return;
    }
    let order=$val('peOrder'),product=$val('peProduct'),internal=$val('peInternal').trim(),o=by('orders',order);
    if(!o.id||!product||!internal)return toast('Plotësoni porosinë, produktin dhe lotin BioBes');
    let bags=$num('peBags',0),bagWeight=$num('peBagWeight',0),newNet=bags*bagWeight;
    if(!(bags>0)||!(bagWeight>0))return toast('Nr. i thasëve dhe pesha neto/thes duhet të jenë më të mëdha se zero');
    let oldMap={};packSourceLots(x).forEach(l=>oldMap[l.id]=packLotQty(x,l.id));
    let coll=collectPackSources('pe',newNet,product,oldMap);
    if(coll.error)return toast(coll.error);
    let oldNet=packNet(x);
    let r=atomicTransaction('Ndryshim paketimi '+id,()=>{
      packSourceLots(x).forEach(l=>{let q=packLotQty(x,l.id);
        l.unpackedNet=lotUnpacked(l)+q;
        l.packedNet=Math.max(0,(+l.packedNet||0)-q)});
      coll.sources.forEach(z=>{let l=by('lots',z.lot);if(!l||!l.id)throw new Error('Loti burimor nuk u gjet');
        if(z.qty>lotUnpacked(l)+.001)throw new Error('Paketimi tejkalon stokun e papaketuar te loti '+l.code);
        l.unpackedNet=lotUnpacked(l)-z.qty;
        l.packedNet=(+l.packedNet||0)+z.qty});
      Object.assign(x,{
        order:order,customer:o.customer,product:product,sourceLots:coll.ids,sourceLotQtys:coll.qtys,
        internalLot:internal,customerLot:$val('peCustomer').trim(),
        template:$val('peTemplate','BioBes standard'),bags:bags,bagWeight:bagWeight,
        cropYear:$num('peCrop',new Date().getFullYear()),bestBefore:$num('peBest',new Date().getFullYear()+3),
        cert:$val('peCert'),organic:$val('peOrganic'),originType:$val('peOriginType'),part:$val('pePart'),
        origin:$val('peOrigin','Albania'),net:newNet,qty:newNet,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()
      });
      let oldMoves=packStockMoves(x),used=new Set();
      coll.sources.forEach(z=>{
        let l=by('lots',z.lot),key=(coll.sources.length===1?'PACK:'+id:'PACK:'+id+':'+l.id),
            mv=oldMoves.find(m=>!used.has(m.id)&&m.lot===l.id)||oldMoves.find(m=>!used.has(m.id));
        if(mv){used.add(mv.id);mv.key=key;mv.lot=l.id;mv.product=l.product;mv.warehouse=l.warehouse;
          mv.stateQty=z.qty;mv.unitCost=l.unitCost;mv.date=x.date}
        else coreMove({key,type:'TRANSFER',source:'packaging',sourceId:id,lot:l.id,product:l.product,
          warehouse:l.warehouse,qty:0,fromState:'unpacked',toState:'packed',stateQty:z.qty,
          unitCost:l.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId})});
      oldMoves.filter(m=>!used.has(m.id)).forEach(mv=>{mv.status='Anuluar';
        coreMove({key:'REV:'+mv.key+':'+stockMoves().length,type:'TRANSFER',source:'reversal',sourceId:id,
          lot:mv.lot,product:mv.product,warehouse:mv.warehouse,qty:0,fromState:'packed',toState:'unpacked',
          stateQty:+(mv.stateQty||0),unitCost:mv.unitCost,reversalOf:mv.id,
          date:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),testRunId:x.testRunId})});
      state.events.push({date:new Date().toLocaleString('sq-AL'),type:'Ndryshim paketimi',ref:id,
        text:`${id}: ${fmt2(oldNet)} kg → ${fmt2(newNet)} kg; lotët: ${coll.sources.map(z=>(by('lots',z.lot)?.code||'?')+' '+fmt2(z.qty)+' kg').join(', ')}`});
      return coreResult(true,'',x);
    });
    if(!r||!r.ok)return toast((r&&r.error)||'Ndryshimi dështoi');
    save();closeModal();render();
    setTimeout(()=>packagingCard(id),80);
    toast('Paketimi u përditësua');
  }catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Gabim gjatë ruajtjes: '+e.message)}
}

/* --------------------------------- fshirja -------------------------------- */
function packagingDelete(id){
  if(!requireAdmin('fshijë paketimin'))return;
  let x=packById(id);
  if(!x)return toast('Paketimi nuk u gjet');
  let block=packBlockingReasons(x);
  if(block.length)return toast('Paketimi nuk fshihet sepse është i lidhur me: '+block.join('; '));
  let reason=prompt('ADMIN — arsyeja e fshirjes së paketimit '+id+':');
  if(!reason||!String(reason).trim())return toast('Arsyeja e fshirjes është e detyrueshme');
  if(!confirm('Ta fshini paketimin '+id+'? '+fmt2(packNet(x))+' kg do të kthehen te lotet burimorë ('+packSourceLots(x).map(l=>l.code).join(', ')+').'))return;
  let net=packNet(x);
  let r=atomicTransaction('Fshirje paketimi '+id,()=>{
    packSourceLots(x).forEach(lot=>{let q=packLotQty(x,lot.id);if(!q)return;
      lot.unpackedNet=lotUnpacked(lot)+q;
      lot.packedNet=Math.max(0,(+lot.packedNet||0)-q)});
    packStockMoves(x).forEach(mv=>{
      mv.status='Anuluar';
      coreMove({key:'REV:'+mv.key+':'+stockMoves().length,type:'TRANSFER',source:'reversal',sourceId:id,lot:mv.lot,product:mv.product,
        warehouse:mv.warehouse,qty:0,fromState:'packed',toState:'unpacked',stateQty:+(mv.stateQty||net),unitCost:mv.unitCost,
        reversalOf:mv.id,date:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),testRunId:x.testRunId})});
    state.packagings=(state.packagings||[]).filter(p=>p.id!==id);
    state.events.push({date:new Date().toLocaleString('sq-AL'),type:'Fshirje paketimi',ref:id,text:String(reason).trim()});
    try{securityAudit('PACKAGING_DELETED',id+': '+String(reason).trim())}catch(e){}
    return coreResult(true,'',x);
  });
  if(!r||!r.ok)return toast((r&&r.error)||'Fshirja dështoi');
  save();closeModal();render();
  toast('Paketimi '+id+' u fshi — '+fmt2(net)+' kg u kthyen në stok');
}
</script>
<script id="biobes-multilot-v2">
/* ============ Shumë-lotësh v2 — Paketim / Ngarkesë / Përpunim / Eksport (2026-09-11) ============
   - Paketim: fushë e gjerë "KG nga ky lot", gjithmonë e editueshme, placeholder 0.00, buton X në
     kolonë më vete, shpërndarje njëlloj me saktësi miligrami dhe rrillogaritje live e totalit/diferencës.
   - Ngarkesa: 2-3 porosi të të njëjtit klient në një kontejner (përzgjedhës shto/hiq), klient auto,
     neto/thasë auto nga paketimet, zbritje stoku për të gjitha lotet; "Krijo Ngarkesë" nga porositë
     e shton porosinë automatikisht.
   - Përpunim: disa lote lënde të parë me kg për lot (hyrje auto si shumë, readonly, e shpërndarë
     njëlloj); coreProcess bën një lëvizje OUT për lot dhe një IN; loti dalës me kosto mesatare të
     ponderuar dhe sourceLots; refuzohen tejkalimi i stokut, produkti tjetër dhe loti i përsëritur.
   - Eksport: dosja bashkon porositë/faturat e kontejnerit dhe tregon manifestin e ngarkimit
     (produkt, lot BioBes, lote burimore, kg, thasë, neto).
   - Prapëpërputhshmëri e plotë me të dhënat e vjetra një-porosi/një-lot; të gjitha tekstet në shqip. */

/* ---------- saktësia miligram & shpërndarja njëlloj ---------- */
function mgRound(v){let r=Math.round((+v||0)*1e6)/1e6;return Object.is(r,-0)?0:r}
function mgStr(v){let s=mgRound(v).toFixed(6).replace(/\.?0+$/,'');return s===''||s==='-'?'0':s}
function distributeEvenMg(total,count){total=mgRound(total);count=Math.max(1,Math.floor(count)||1);if(count===1)return[total];let each=Math.floor(total/count*1e6)/1e6,out=[];for(let i=0;i<count;i++)out.push(i===count-1?mgRound(total-each*(count-1)):each);return out}

/* ---------- Paketim: rreshtat e loteve burimore (KG e gjerë, gjithmonë e editueshme) ---------- */
function packSourceRowHtml(prefix,lotId,qty,oldMap){
  oldMap=oldMap||{};let lot=by('lots',lotId);if(!lot||!lot.id)return'';
  let p=by('products',lot.product)||{},free=lotUnpacked(lot)+(((+oldMap[lotId])||0));
  return '<div data-pack-source-row="'+prefix+'" data-lot="'+lotId+'" style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:6px;padding:6px 8px;border:1px solid #d4dcd6;border-radius:8px;background:#f7faf8">'
    +'<div style="flex:1;min-width:150px"><b>'+esc(lot.code)+'</b> <small>FR '+esc(((by('suppliers',lot.supplier)||{}).code)||'—')+'</small><br><small>'+esc((p.code||'')+' — '+(p.name||''))+' · Stok i papaketuar: '+fmt2(free)+' kg</small></div>'
    +'<div style="flex:0 0 210px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px"><input type="number" step="0.000001" min="0" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0.00" title="KG nga ky lot" '+(qty===''||qty==null?'':('value="'+mgStr(+qty||0)+'"'))+' data-pack-qty oninput="packSourceSum(\''+prefix+'\')" style="width:150px;font-size:15px;padding:9px 10px"> <span>kg</span></div>'
    +'<div style="flex:0 0 42px;text-align:right"><button type="button" class="btn small danger" title="Hiq lotin" onclick="packSourceRemoveRow(this,\''+prefix+'\')">✕</button></div></div>';
}
function packSourceSum(prefix){
  let c=packSourceContainer(prefix);if(!c)return;let rows=packSourceRows(prefix);
  let total=packSourceTarget(prefix);
  let sum=mgRound(rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0),0)),diff=mgRound(total-sum);
  let el=c.querySelector('[data-pack-sum="'+prefix+'"]');
  if(el)el.innerHTML='Nga lotet: <b>'+fmt2(sum)+' kg</b> / duhet '+fmt2(total)+' kg · '+(Math.abs(diff)<=.001?'<b style="color:#1e7e34">✓ balancuar</b>':(Math.abs(diff)<=1?'<b style="color:#d28b13">diferencë '+fmt2(diff)+' kg (afershmë)</b>':'<b style="color:#b63c46">diferencë '+fmt2(diff)+' kg</b>'))+(rows.length>1?' · <span class="pill orange">përzierje '+rows.length+' lotësh</span>':'');
}
function packSourceDistribute(prefix){
  let rows=packSourceRows(prefix),total=packSourceTarget(prefix);if(!rows.length||!(total>0))return;
  let parts=distributeEvenMg(total,rows.length);
  rows.forEach((r,i)=>{r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value=mgStr(parts[i])});
  packSourceSum(prefix);
}
function packSourceAddRow(prefix){
  let sel=document.getElementById(prefix+'AddLot');let lotId=sel?sel.value:'';if(!lotId)return;
  let rows=packSourceRows(prefix);
  if(rows.some(r=>r.dataset.lot===lotId))return toast('Ai lot është tashmë në listë');
  let c=packSourceContainer(prefix),oldMap=packSourceOldMap(prefix),total=packSourceTarget(prefix);
  if(rows.length===1){let q1=parseFloat(rows[0].querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0;if(Math.abs(q1-total)<=.001){let half=mgRound(total/2);rows[0].querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value=mgStr(half)}}
  let cur=mgRound(packSourceRows(prefix).reduce((a,r)=>a+(parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0),0));
  let rest=mgRound(total-cur);
  c.querySelector('[data-pack-rows="'+prefix+'"]').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',packSourceRowHtml(prefix,lotId,rest>0?rest:'',oldMap));
  sel.value='';
  let lot=by('lots',lotId),prod=document.getElementById(prefix+'Product');
  if(lot&&lot.id&&prod&&!prod.value){try{fillSearchSelect(prefix+'Product',lot.product)}catch(e){prod.value=lot.product}}
  packSourceRefreshAdd(prefix,prod?prod.value:'');
  packSourceSum(prefix);
}
function collectPackSources(prefix,totalNet,productId,oldMap){
  let rows=readPackSourceRows(prefix);
  if(!rows.length)return{error:'Zgjidhni të paktën një lot burimor'};
  let ids=[],qtys={},sum=0,seen=new Set();
  for(let r of rows){
    let lot=by('lots',r.lot);if(!lot||!lot.id)return{error:'Loti burimor nuk u gjet'};
    if(seen.has(lot.id))return{error:'Loti '+lot.code+' është dy herë në listë'};seen.add(lot.id);
    if(!(r.qty>0))return{error:'Sasia për lotin '+lot.code+' duhet të jetë pozitive'};
    let cap=lotUnpacked(lot)+(((oldMap||{})[lot.id]!=null?+(oldMap[lot.id]):0));
    if(r.qty>cap+.001)return{error:'Tejkalim stoku te loti '+lot.code+': të lira '+fmt2(cap)+' kg'};
    if(productId&&lot.product!==productId)return{error:'Loti '+lot.code+' është i produktit tjetër'};
    let q=mgRound(r.qty);qtys[lot.id]=q;ids.push(lot.id);sum=mgRound(sum+q);
  }
  if(Math.abs(sum-totalNet)>.001)return{error:'Sasia nga lotet ('+fmt2(sum)+' kg) duhet të jetë '+fmt2(totalNet)+' kg'};
  return{sources:ids.map(id=>({lot:id,qty:qtys[id]})),qtys,ids};
}

</script>
<script id="biobes-multilot-v2b">
/* ---------- Ngarkesa: 2-3 porosi të së njëjtit klient në një kontejner ---------- */
function shipmentOrders(x){if(!x)return[];if(Array.isArray(x.orders)&&x.orders.length)return x.orders.slice();return x.order?[x.order]:[]}
function shipmentHasOrder(x,id){return shipmentOrders(x).includes(id)}
function containerPackTotals(orderIds){let net=0,bags=0;(orderIds||[]).forEach(oid=>{packs().filter(p=>p.order===oid).forEach(p=>{net=mgRound(net+packNet(p));bags+=(+p.bags||0)})});return{net,bags}}
function coreCreateShipment(data){
  if(state.shipments.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(true,'',by('shipments',data.id));
  let orders=Array.isArray(data.orders)&&data.orders.length?data.orders.slice():(data.order?[data.order]:[]);
  if(!orders.length)return coreResult(false,'Porosia mungon');
  if(orders.length>3)return coreResult(false,'Më së shumti 3 porosi në një kontejner');
  let cust=data.customer||null;
  for(let oid of orders){let o=by('orders',oid);if(!o||!o.id)return coreResult(false,'Porosia '+oid+' nuk u gjet');if(!cust)cust=o.customer;else if(o.customer&&cust&&o.customer!==cust)return coreResult(false,'Të gjitha porositë e kontejnerit duhet të jenë të së njëjtit klient')}
  let x={...data,orders,order:orders[0],customer:cust||data.customer,status:'Planifikuar',stockDeducted:false};
  state.shipments.push(x);return coreResult(true,'',x);
}
function shipmentRequirements(x,next){let missing=[];if(next==='Në ngarkim'){if(!shipmentOrders(x).length)missing.push('porosia');if(!x.plate)missing.push('targa');if(!x.carrier)missing.push('transportuesi')}if(next==='Ngarkuar'){if(!(x.net>0))missing.push('pesha neto');if(!(x.gross>0))missing.push('pesha bruto');if(!(x.bags>0))missing.push('numri i thasëve')}if(next==='Vulosur'){if(!x.seal)missing.push('numri i plumbçes');if(!x.sealPhotoData)missing.push('fotografia e plumbçes')}if(next==='Dokumentuar'){let docs=state.documents.filter(d=>d.shipment===x.id),required=['Commercial Invoice','Packing List','CMR'];required.forEach(t=>{if(!docs.find(d=>d.type===t&&d.fileData))missing.push(t)})}if(next==='Ngarkuar'){
  let orders=new Set(shipmentOrders(x));
  let packed=packs().filter(p=>orders.has(p.order)).reduce((sum,p)=>sum+packNet(p),0);
  let shipped=state.shipments.filter(sh=>sh.id!==x.id&&sh.stockDeducted&&shipmentOrders(sh).some(o=>orders.has(o))).reduce((sum,sh)=>sum+(+sh.net||0),0);
  if((+x.net||0)>packed-shipped+0.001)missing.push('stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');
  if(+x.gross<+x.net)missing.push('bruto më e vogël se neto');
}
if(next==='Nisur'){
  let dossier=exportDossiers().find(d=>d.shipment===x.id);
  if(!dossier||dossier.status!=='Mbyllur')missing.push('dosja e eksportit e mbyllur');
  if(dossier&&dossierConsistencyChecks(dossier).some(c=>c.status!=='pass'))missing.push('mospërputhjet e dosjes');
}
return [...new Set(missing)]}
function coreAdvanceShipment(id,next){let x=by('shipments',id),cur=normalizedShipmentStatus(x),expected=shipmentStages[shipmentStages.indexOf(cur)+1];if(!x?.id||next!==expected)return coreResult(false,'Kalimi i statusit nuk është në rend');let missing=shipmentRequirements(x,next);if(missing.length)return coreResult(false,'Mungon: '+missing.join(', '));if(next==='Nisur'&&!x.stockDeducted){let os=new Set(shipmentOrders(x)),allocations=packs().filter(p=>os.has(p.order)),left=+x.net||0,lotIds=[...new Set(allocations.flatMap(pk=>pk.sourceLots||[]))],totalPacked=lotIds.reduce((a,lid)=>a+(+by('lots',lid)?.packedNet||0),0);if(totalPacked+.001<left)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');for(let lid of lotIds){let lot=by('lots',lid),take=Math.min(left,+lot?.packedNet||0);if(take){lot.packedNet-=take;lot.availableNet=Math.max(0,lotAvail(lot)-take);lot.net=lot.availableNet;coreMove({key:'SHIP:'+id+':'+lot.id,type:'OUT',source:'shipment',sourceId:id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:-take,unitCost:lot.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId});left-=take}}if(left>.001)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');x.stockDeducted=true;x.stockDeductedAt=new Date().toISOString()}x.status=next;return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function shipOrderOptionsHtml(chosen){let set=new Set(chosen||[]);return '<option value="">Shto porosi…</option>'+(state.orders||[]).filter(o=>!set.has(o.id)).map(o=>'<option value="'+o.id+'">'+esc(o.id+' — '+(by('customers',o.customer).name||''))+'</option>').join('')}
function shipOrderRowHtml(orderId){let o=by('orders',orderId),c=by('customers',o?o.customer:'');return '<div data-ship-order-row data-order="'+orderId+'" style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:6px;padding:6px 8px;border:1px solid #d4dcd6;border-radius:8px;background:#f7faf8"><div style="flex:1;min-width:150px"><b>'+esc(orderId)+'</b><br><small>'+esc(c?.name||'')+' · '+esc(c?.country||'')+'</small></div><div style="flex:0 0 42px;text-align:right"><button type="button" class="btn small danger" title="Hiq porosinë" onclick="shipOrderRemoveRow(this)">✕</button></div></div>'}
function shipOrderRows(){return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-ship-order-row]'))}
function shipOrderIds(){return shipOrderRows().map(r=>r.dataset.order)}
function shipOrderSync(){let ids=shipOrderIds(),o=by('orders',ids[0]),c=document.getElementById('shCustomer');if(c&&o&&o.customer)c.value=o.customer;let t=containerPackTotals(ids),net=document.getElementById('shNet'),bags=document.getElementById('shBags');if(net&&!net.dataset.touched)net.value=mgStr(t.net);if(bags&&!bags.dataset.touched)bags.value=t.bags;let el=document.getElementById('shOrderSum');if(el)el.innerHTML=ids.length?('Porosi: <b>'+ids.length+'</b> · nga paketimet: <b>'+fmt2(t.net)+' kg</b> dhe <b>'+t.bags+'</b> thasë (neto/thasë plotësohen auto, mund t’i ndryshoni)'):'Shto të paktën një porosi të kontejnerit'}
function shipOrderRefresh(){let sel=document.getElementById('shOrderAdd');if(sel)sel.innerHTML=shipOrderOptionsHtml(shipOrderIds());shipOrderSync()}
function shipOrderAdd(){let sel=document.getElementById('shOrderAdd'),id=sel?sel.value:'';if(!id)return;let ids=shipOrderIds();if(ids.includes(id))return toast('Kjo porosi është tashmë në kontejner');if(ids.length>=3)return toast('Më së shumti 3 porosi në një kontejner');let o=by('orders',id),first=by('orders',ids[0]);if(first&&first.id&&o&&o.customer&&first.customer&&o.customer!==first.customer)return toast('Të gjitha porositë e kontejnerit duhet të jenë të së njëjtit klient');let box=document.querySelector('[data-ship-orders]');if(!box)return;box.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',shipOrderRowHtml(id));sel.value='';shipOrderRefresh()}
function shipOrderRemoveRow(btn){btn.closest('[data-ship-order-row]')?.remove();shipOrderRefresh()}
function syncShipCustomer(){shipOrderSync()}
function shipmentForm(){modal('Ngarkesë e re — kontejner',`<div class="form-grid"><div class="field"><label>Data</label><input id="shDate" type="date" value="${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}"></div><div class="field full"><label>Porositë e kontejnerit * <small>(1-3 porosi të së njëjtit klient)</small></label><div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-bottom:6px"><select id="shOrderAdd" style="flex:1">${shipOrderOptionsHtml([])}</select><button type="button" class="btn small" onclick="shipOrderAdd()">+ Shto porosi</button></div><div data-ship-orders></div><p class="hint" id="shOrderSum" style="margin:0"></p></div><div class="field"><label>Klienti</label><select id="shCustomer">${opts(state.customers,x=>x.name+' — '+x.country)}</select></div><div class="field"><label>Targa</label><input id="shPlate"></div><div class="field"><label>Transportuesi</label><input id="shCarrier"></div><div class="field"><label>Numri i plumbçes</label><input id="shSeal"></div><div class="field"><label>Pesha neto kg <small>(auto nga paketimet)</small></label><input id="shNet" type="number" step="0.000001" oninput="this.dataset.touched='1'"></div><div class="field"><label>Pesha bruto kg</label><input id="shGross" type="number" step="0.000001"></div><div class="field"><label>Nr. thasëve <small>(auto)</small></label><input id="shBags" type="number" oninput="this.dataset.touched='1'"></div><div class="field"><label>Nr. paletave</label><input id="shPallets" type="number"></div><div class="field full"><label>Foto e plumbçes</label><input type="file" accept="image/*"></div></div>`,`<button class="btn" onclick="closeModal()">Anulo</button><button class="btn primary" onclick="addShipment()">Krijo ngarkesën</button>`);shipOrderSync()}
function addShipment(){try{let orders=shipOrderIds();if(!orders.length)return toast('Shto të paktën një porosi në kontejner');let data={id:nextSeqId('NG',(state.shipments||[]),1,new Date().getFullYear()),date:$val('shDate',new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)),orders,order:orders[0],custom

### lotUnpacked(lot) @ 1747355
,sourceLots:coll.ids,sourceLotQtys:coll.qtys,
        internalLot:internal,customerLot:$val('peCustomer').trim(),
        template:$val('peTemplate','BioBes standard'),bags:bags,bagWeight:bagWeight,
        cropYear:$num('peCrop',new Date().getFullYear()),bestBefore:$num('peBest',new Date().getFullYear()+3),
        cert:$val('peCert'),organic:$val('peOrganic'),originType:$val('peOriginType'),part:$val('pePart'),
        origin:$val('peOrigin','Albania'),net:newNet,qty:newNet,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()
      });
      let oldMoves=packStockMoves(x),used=new Set();
      coll.sources.forEach(z=>{
        let l=by('lots',z.lot),key=(coll.sources.length===1?'PACK:'+id:'PACK:'+id+':'+l.id),
            mv=oldMoves.find(m=>!used.has(m.id)&&m.lot===l.id)||oldMoves.find(m=>!used.has(m.id));
        if(mv){used.add(mv.id);mv.key=key;mv.lot=l.id;mv.product=l.product;mv.warehouse=l.warehouse;
          mv.stateQty=z.qty;mv.unitCost=l.unitCost;mv.date=x.date}
        else coreMove({key,type:'TRANSFER',source:'packaging',sourceId:id,lot:l.id,product:l.product,
          warehouse:l.warehouse,qty:0,fromState:'unpacked',toState:'packed',stateQty:z.qty,
          unitCost:l.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId})});
      oldMoves.filter(m=>!used.has(m.id)).forEach(mv=>{mv.status='Anuluar';
        coreMove({key:'REV:'+mv.key+':'+stockMoves().length,type:'TRANSFER',source:'reversal',sourceId:id,
          lot:mv.lot,product:mv.product,warehouse:mv.warehouse,qty:0,fromState:'packed',toState:'unpacked',
          stateQty:+(mv.stateQty||0),unitCost:mv.unitCost,reversalOf:mv.id,
          date:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),testRunId:x.testRunId})});
      state.events.push({date:new Date().toLocaleString('sq-AL'),type:'Ndryshim paketimi',ref:id,
        text:`${id}: ${fmt2(oldNet)} kg → ${fmt2(newNet)} kg; lotët: ${coll.sources.map(z=>(by('lots',z.lot)?.code||'?')+' '+fmt2(z.qty)+' kg').join(', ')}`});
      return coreResult(true,'',x);
    });
    if(!r||!r.ok)return toast((r&&r.error)||'Ndryshimi dështoi');
    save();closeModal();render();
    setTimeout(()=>packagingCard(id),80);
    toast('Paketimi u përditësua');
  }catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Gabim gjatë ruajtjes: '+e.message)}
}

/* --------------------------------- fshirja -------------------------------- */
function packagingDelete(id){
  if(!requireAdmin('fshijë paketimin'))return;
  let x=packById(id);
  if(!x)return toast('Paketimi nuk u gjet');
  let block=packBlockingReasons(x);
  if(block.length)return toast('Paketimi nuk fshihet sepse është i lidhur me: '+block.join('; '));
  let reason=prompt('ADMIN — arsyeja e fshirjes së paketimit '+id+':');
  if(!reason||!String(reason).trim())return toast('Arsyeja e fshirjes është e detyrueshme');
  if(!confirm('Ta fshini paketimin '+id+'? '+fmt2(packNet(x))+' kg do të kthehen te lotet burimorë ('+packSourceLots(x).map(l=>l.code).join(', ')+').'))return;
  let net=packNet(x);
  let r=atomicTransaction('Fshirje paketimi '+id,()=>{
    packSourceLots(x).forEach(lot=>{let q=packLotQty(x,lot.id);if(!q)return;
      lot.unpackedNet=lotUnpacked(lot)+q;
      lot.packedNet=Math.max(0,(+lot.packedNet||0)-q)});
    packStockMoves(x).forEach(mv=>{
      mv.status='Anuluar';
      coreMove({key:'REV:'+mv.key+':'+stockMoves().length,type:'TRANSFER',source:'reversal',sourceId:id,lot:mv.lot,product:mv.product,
        warehouse:mv.warehouse,qty:0,fromState:'packed',toState:'unpacked',stateQty:+(mv.stateQty||net),unitCost:mv.unitCost,
        reversalOf:mv.id,date:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),testRunId:x.testRunId})});
    state.packagings=(state.packagings||[]).filter(p=>p.id!==id);
    state.events.push({date:new Date().toLocaleString('sq-AL'),type:'Fshirje paketimi',ref:id,text:String(reason).trim()});
    try{securityAudit('PACKAGING_DELETED',id+': '+String(reason).trim())}catch(e){}
    return coreResult(true,'',x);
  });
  if(!r||!r.ok)return toast((r&&r.error)||'Fshirja dështoi');
  save();closeModal();render();
  toast('Paketimi '+id+' u fshi — '+fmt2(net)+' kg u kthyen në stok');
}
</script>
<script id="biobes-multilot-v2">
/* ============ Shumë-lotësh v2 — Paketim / Ngarkesë / Përpunim / Eksport (2026-09-11) ============
   - Paketim: fushë e gjerë "KG nga ky lot", gjithmonë e editueshme, placeholder 0.00, buton X në
     kolonë më vete, shpërndarje njëlloj me saktësi miligrami dhe rrillogaritje live e totalit/diferencës.
   - Ngarkesa: 2-3 porosi të të njëjtit klient në një kontejner (përzgjedhës shto/hiq), klient auto,
     neto/thasë auto nga paketimet, zbritje stoku për të gjitha lotet; "Krijo Ngarkesë" nga porositë
     e shton porosinë automatikisht.
   - Përpunim: disa lote lënde të parë me kg për lot (hyrje auto si shumë, readonly, e shpërndarë
     njëlloj); coreProcess bën një lëvizje OUT për lot dhe një IN; loti dalës me kosto mesatare të
     ponderuar dhe sourceLots; refuzohen tejkalimi i stokut, produkti tjetër dhe loti i përsëritur.
   - Eksport: dosja bashkon porositë/faturat e kontejnerit dhe tregon manifestin e ngarkimit
     (produkt, lot BioBes, lote burimore, kg, thasë, neto).
   - Prapëpërputhshmëri e plotë me të dhënat e vjetra një-porosi/një-lot; të gjitha tekstet në shqip. */

/* ---------- saktësia miligram & shpërndarja njëlloj ---------- */
function mgRound(v){let r=Math.round((+v||0)*1e6)/1e6;return Object.is(r,-0)?0:r}
function mgStr(v){let s=mgRound(v).toFixed(6).replace(/\.?0+$/,'');return s===''||s==='-'?'0':s}
function distributeEvenMg(total,count){total=mgRound(total);count=Math.max(1,Math.floor(count)||1);if(count===1)return[total];let each=Math.floor(total/count*1e6)/1e6,out=[];for(let i=0;i<count;i++)out.push(i===count-1?mgRound(total-each*(count-1)):each);return out}

/* ---------- Paketim: rreshtat e loteve burimore (KG e gjerë, gjithmonë e editueshme) ---------- */
function packSourceRowHtml(prefix,lotId,qty,oldMap){
  oldMap=oldMap||{};let lot=by('lots',lotId);if(!lot||!lot.id)return'';
  let p=by('products',lot.product)||{},free=lotUnpacked(lot)+(((+oldMap[lotId])||0));
  return '<div data-pack-source-row="'+prefix+'" data-lot="'+lotId+'" style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:6px;padding:6px 8px;border:1px solid #d4dcd6;border-radius:8px;background:#f7faf8">'
    +'<div style="flex:1;min-width:150px"><b>'+esc(lot.code)+'</b> <small>FR '+esc(((by('suppliers',lot.supplier)||{}).code)||'—')+'</small><br><small>'+esc((p.code||'')+' — '+(p.name||''))+' · Stok i papaketuar: '+fmt2(free)+' kg</small></div>'
    +'<div style="flex:0 0 210px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px"><input type="number" step="0.000001" min="0" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0.00" title="KG nga ky lot" '+(qty===''||qty==null?'':('value="'+mgStr(+qty||0)+'"'))+' data-pack-qty oninput="packSourceSum(\''+prefix+'\')" style="width:150px;font-size:15px;padding:9px 10px"> <span>kg</span></div>'
    +'<div style="flex:0 0 42px;text-align:right"><button type="button" class="btn small danger" title="Hiq lotin" onclick="packSourceRemoveRow(this,\''+prefix+'\')">✕</button></div></div>';
}
function packSourceSum(prefix){
  let c=packSourceContainer(prefix);if(!c)return;let rows=packSourceRows(prefix);
  let total=packSourceTarget(prefix);
  let sum=mgRound(rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0),0)),diff=mgRound(total-sum);
  let el=c.querySelector('[data-pack-sum="'+prefix+'"]');
  if(el)el.innerHTML='Nga lotet: <b>'+fmt2(sum)+' kg</b> / duhet '+fmt2(total)+' kg · '+(Math.abs(diff)<=.001?'<b style="color:#1e7e34">✓ balancuar</b>':(Math.abs(diff)<=1?'<b style="color:#d28b13">diferencë '+fmt2(diff)+' kg (afershmë)</b>':'<b style="color:#b63c46">diferencë '+fmt2(diff)+' kg</b>'))+(rows.length>1?' · <span class="pill orange">përzierje '+rows.length+' lotësh</span>':'');
}
function packSourceDistribute(prefix){
  let rows=packSourceRows(prefix),total=packSourceTarget(prefix);if(!rows.length||!(total>0))return;
  let parts=distributeEvenMg(total,rows.length);
  rows.forEach((r,i)=>{r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value=mgStr(parts[i])});
  packSourceSum(prefix);
}
function packSourceAddRow(prefix){
  let sel=document.getElementById(prefix+'AddLot');let lotId=sel?sel.value:'';if(!lotId)return;
  let rows=packSourceRows(prefix);
  if(rows.some(r=>r.dataset.lot===lotId))return toast('Ai lot është tashmë në listë');
  let c=packSourceContainer(prefix),oldMap=packSourceOldMap(prefix),total=packSourceTarget(prefix);
  if(rows.length===1){let q1=parseFloat(rows[0].querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0;if(Math.abs(q1-total)<=.001){let half=mgRound(total/2);rows[0].querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value=mgStr(half)}}
  let cur=mgRound(packSourceRows(prefix).reduce((a,r)=>a+(parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0),0));
  let rest=mgRound(total-cur);
  c.querySelector('[data-pack-rows="'+prefix+'"]').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',packSourceRowHtml(prefix,lotId,rest>0?rest:'',oldMap));
  sel.value='';
  let lot=by('lots',lotId),prod=document.getElementById(prefix+'Product');
  if(lot&&lot.id&&prod&&!prod.value){try{fillSearchSelect(prefix+'Product',lot.product)}catch(e){prod.value=lot.product}}
  packSourceRefreshAdd(prefix,prod?prod.value:'');
  packSourceSum(prefix);
}
function collectPackSources(prefix,totalNet,productId,oldMap){
  let rows=readPackSourceRows(prefix);
  if(!rows.length)return{error:'Zgjidhni të paktën një lot burimor'};
  let ids=[],qtys={},sum=0,seen=new Set();
  for(let r of rows){
    let lot=by('lots',r.lot);if(!lot||!lot.id)return{error:'Loti burimor nuk u gjet'};
    if(seen.has(lot.id))return{error:'Loti '+lot.code+' është dy herë në listë'};seen.add(lot.id);
    if(!(r.qty>0))return{error:'Sasia për lotin '+lot.code+' duhet të jetë pozitive'};
    let cap=lotUnpacked(lot)+(((oldMap||{})[lot.id]!=null?+(oldMap[lot.id]):0));
    if(r.qty>cap+.001)return{error:'Tejkalim stoku te loti '+lot.code+': të lira '+fmt2(cap)+' kg'};
    if(productId&&lot.product!==productId)return{error:'Loti '+lot.code+' është i produktit tjetër'};
    let q=mgRound(r.qty);qtys[lot.id]=q;ids.push(lot.id);sum=mgRound(sum+q);
  }
  if(Math.abs(sum-totalNet)>.001)return{error:'Sasia nga lotet ('+fmt2(sum)+' kg) duhet të jetë '+fmt2(totalNet)+' kg'};
  return{sources:ids.map(id=>({lot:id,qty:qtys[id]})),qtys,ids};
}

</script>

/* ---------- Ngarkesa: 2-3 porosi të së njëjtit klient në një kontejner ---------- */
function shipmentOrders(x){if(!x)return[];if(Array.isArray(x.orders)&&x.orders.length)return x.orders.slice();return x.order?[x.order]:[]}
function shipmentHasOrder(x,id){return shipmentOrders(x).includes(id)}
function containerPackTotals(orderIds){let net=0,bags=0;(orderIds||[]).forEach(oid=>{packs().filter(p=>p.order===oid).forEach(p=>{net=mgRound(net+packNet(p));bags+=(+p.bags||0)})});return{net,bags}}
function coreCreateShipment(data){
  if(state.shipments.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(true,'',by('shipments',data.id));
  let orders=Array.isArray(data.orders)&&data.orders.length?data.orders.slice():(data.order?[data.order]:[]);
  if(!orders.length)return coreResult(false,'Porosia mungon');
  if(orders.length>3)return coreResult(false,'Më së shumti 3 porosi në një kontejner');
  let cust=data.customer||null;
  for(let oid of orders){let o=by('orders',oid);if(!o||!o.id)return coreResult(false,'Porosia '+oid+' nuk u gjet');if(!cust)cust=o.customer;else if(o.customer&&cust&&o.customer!==cust)return coreResult(false,'Të gjitha porositë e kontejnerit duhet të jenë të së njëjtit klient')}
  let x={...data,orders,order:orders[0],customer:cust||data.customer,status:'Planifikuar',stockDeducted:false};
  state.shipments.push(x);return coreResult(true,'',x);
}
function shipmentRequirements(x,next){let missing=[];if(next==='Në ngarkim'){if(!shipmentOrders(x).length)missing.push('porosia');if(!x.plate)missing.push('targa');if(!x.carrier)missing.push('transportuesi')}if(next==='Ngarkuar'){if(!(x.net>0))missing.push('pesha neto');if(!(x.gross>0))missing.push('pesha bruto');if(!(x.bags>0))missing.push('numri i thasëve')}if(next==='Vulosur'){if(!x.seal)missing.push('numri i plumbçes');if(!x.sealPhotoData)missing.push('fotografia e plumbçes')}if(next==='Dokumentuar'){let docs=state.documents.filter(d=>d.shipment===x.id),required=['Commercial Invoice','Packing List','CMR'];required.forEach(t=>{if(!docs.find(d=>d.type===t&&d.fileData))missing.push(t)})}if(next==='Ngarkuar'){
  let orders=new Set(shipmentOrders(x));
  let packed=packs().filter(p=>orders.has(p.order)).reduce((sum,p)=>sum+packNet(p),0);
  let shipped=state.shipments.filter(sh=>sh.id!==x.id&&sh.stockDeducted&&shipmentOrders(sh).some(o=>orders.has(o))).reduce((sum,sh)=>sum+(+sh.net||0),0);
  if((+x.net||0)>packed-shipped+0.001)missing.push('stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');
  if(+x.gross<+x.net)missing.push('bruto më e vogël se neto');
}
if(next==='Nisur'){
  let dossier=exportDossiers().find(d=>d.shipment===x.id);
  if(!dossier||dossier.status!=='Mbyllur')missing.push('dosja e eksportit e mbyllur');
  if(dossier&&dossierConsistencyChecks(dossier).some(c=>c.status!=='pass'))missing.push('mospërputhjet e dosjes');
}
return [...new Set(missing)]}
function coreAdvanceShipment(id,next){let x=by('shipments',id),cur=normalizedShipmentStatus(x),expected=shipmentStages[shipmentStages.indexOf(cur)+1];if(!x?.id||next!==expected)return coreResult(false,'Kalimi i statusit nuk është në rend');let missing=shipmentRequirements(x,next);if(missing.length)return coreResult(false,'Mungon: '+missing.join(', '));if(next==='Nisur'&&!x.stockDeducted){let os=new Set(shipmentOrders(x)),allocations=packs().filter(p=>os.has(p.order)),left=+x.net||0,lotIds=[...new Set(allocations.flatMap(pk=>pk.sourceLots||[]))],totalPacked=lotIds.reduce((a,lid)=>a+(+by('lots',lid)?.packedNet||0),0);if(totalPacked+.001<left)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');for(let lid of lotIds){let lot=by('lots',lid),take=Math.min(left,+lot?.packedNet||0);if(take){lot.packedNet-=take;lot.availableNet=Math.max(0,lotAvail(lot)-take);lot.net=lot.availableNet;coreMove({key:'SHIP:'+id+':'+lot.id,type:'OUT',source:'shipment',sourceId:id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:-take,unitCost:lot.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId});left-=take}}if(left>.001)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');x.stockDeducted=true;x.stockDeductedAt=new Date().toISOString()}x.status=next;return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function shipOrderOptionsHtml(chosen){let set=new Set(chosen||[]);return '<option value="">Shto porosi…</option>'+(state.orders||[]).filter(o=>!set.has(o.id)).map(o=>'<option value="'+o.id+'">'+esc(o.id+' — '+(by('customers',o.customer).name||''))+'</option>').join('')}
function shipOrderRowHtml(orderId){let o=by('orders',orderId),c=by('customers',o?o.customer:'');return '<div data-ship-order-row data-order="'+orderId+'" style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:6px;padding:6px 8px;border:1px solid #d4dcd6;border-radius:8px;background:#f7faf8"><div style="flex:1;min-width:150px"><b>'+esc(orderId)+'</b><br><small>'+esc(c?.name||'')+' · '+esc(c?.country||'')+'</small></div><div style="flex:0 0 42px;text-align:right"><button type="button" class="btn small danger" title="Hiq porosinë" onclick="shipOrderRemoveRow(this)">✕</button></div></div>'}
