<script id="biobes-multilot-v2">
/* ============ Shumë-lotësh v2 — Paketim / Ngarkesë / Përpunim / Eksport (2026-09-11) ============
   - Paketim: fushë e gjerë "KG nga ky lot", gjithmonë e editueshme, placeholder 0.00, buton X në
     kolonë më vete, shpërndarje njëlloj me saktësi miligrami dhe rrillogaritje live e totalit/diferencës.
   - Ngarkesa: 2-3 porosi të të njëjtit klient në një kontejner (përzgjedhës shto/hiq), klient auto,
     neto/thasë auto nga paketimet, zbritje stoku për të gjitha lotet; "Krijo Ngarkesë" nga porositë
     e shton porosinë automatikisht.
   - Përpunim: disa lote lënde të parë me kg për lot (hyrje auto si shumë, readonly, e shpërndarë
     njëlloj); coreProcess bën një lëvizje OUT për lot dhe një IN; loti dalës me kosto mesatare të
     ponderuar dhe sourceLots; refuzohen tejkalimi i stokut, produkti tjetër dhe loti i përsëritur.
   - Eksport: dosja bashkon porositë/faturat e kontejnerit dhe tregon manifestin e ngarkimit
     (produkt, lot BioBes, lote burimore, kg, thasë, neto).
   - Prapëpërputhshmëri e plotë me të dhënat e vjetra një-porosi/një-lot; të gjitha tekstet në shqip. */

/* ---------- saktësia miligram & shpërndarja njëlloj ---------- */
function mgRound(v){let r=Math.round((+v||0)*1e6)/1e6;return Object.is(r,-0)?0:r}
function mgStr(v){let s=mgRound(v).toFixed(6).replace(/\.?0+$/,'');return s===''||s==='-'?'0':s}
function distributeEvenMg(total,count){total=mgRound(total);count=Math.max(1,Math.floor(count)||1);if(count===1)return[total];let each=Math.floor(total/count*1e6)/1e6,out=[];for(let i=0;i<count;i++)out.push(i===count-1?mgRound(total-each*(count-1)):each);return out}

/* ---------- Paketim: rreshtat e loteve burimore (KG e gjerë, gjithmonë e editueshme) ---------- */
function packSourceRowHtml(prefix,lotId,qty,oldMap){
  oldMap=oldMap||{};let lot=by('lots',lotId);if(!lot||!lot.id)return'';
  let p=by('products',lot.product)||{},free=lotUnpacked(lot)+(((+oldMap[lotId])||0));
  return '<div data-pack-source-row="'+prefix+'" data-lot="'+lotId+'" style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:6px;padding:6px 8px;border:1px solid #d4dcd6;border-radius:8px;background:#f7faf8">'
    +'<div style="flex:1;min-width:150px"><b>'+esc(lot.code)+'</b> <small>FR '+esc(((by('suppliers',lot.supplier)||{}).code)||'—')+'</small><br><small>'+esc((p.code||'')+' — '+(p.name||''))+' · Stok i papaketuar: '+fmt2(free)+' kg</small></div>'
    +'<div style="flex:0 0 210px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px"><input type="number" step="0.000001" min="0" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0.00" title="KG nga ky lot" '+(qty===''||qty==null?'':('value="'+mgStr(+qty||0)+'"'))+' data-pack-qty oninput="packSourceSum(\''+prefix+'\')" style="width:150px;font-size:15px;padding:9px 10px"> <span>kg</span></div>'
    +'<div style="flex:0 0 42px;text-align:right"><button type="button" class="btn small danger" title="Hiq lotin" onclick="packSourceRemoveRow(this,\''+prefix+'\')">✕</button></div></div>';
}
function packSourceSum(prefix){
  let c=packSourceContainer(prefix);if(!c)return;let rows=packSourceRows(prefix);
  let total=packSourceTarget(prefix);
  let sum=mgRound(rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+(parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0),0)),diff=mgRound(total-sum);
  let el=c.querySelector('[data-pack-sum="'+prefix+'"]');
  if(el)el.innerHTML='Nga lotet: <b>'+fmt2(sum)+' kg</b> / duhet '+fmt2(total)+' kg · '+(Math.abs(diff)<=.001?'<b style="color:#1e7e34">✓ balancuar</b>':(Math.abs(diff)<=1?'<b style="color:#d28b13">diferencë '+fmt2(diff)+' kg (afershmë)</b>':'<b style="color:#b63c46">diferencë '+fmt2(diff)+' kg</b>'))+(rows.length>1?' · <span class="pill orange">përzierje '+rows.length+' lotësh</span>':'');
}
function packSourceDistribute(prefix){
  let rows=packSourceRows(prefix),total=packSourceTarget(prefix);if(!rows.length||!(total>0))return;
  let parts=distributeEvenMg(total,rows.length);
  rows.forEach((r,i)=>{r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value=mgStr(parts[i])});
  packSourceSum(prefix);
}
function packSourceAddRow(prefix){
  let sel=document.getElementById(prefix+'AddLot');let lotId=sel?sel.value:'';if(!lotId)return;
  let rows=packSourceRows(prefix);
  if(rows.some(r=>r.dataset.lot===lotId))return toast('Ai lot është tashmë në listë');
  let c=packSourceContainer(prefix),oldMap=packSourceOldMap(prefix),total=packSourceTarget(prefix);
  if(rows.length===1){let q1=parseFloat(rows[0].querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0;if(Math.abs(q1-total)<=.001){let half=mgRound(total/2);rows[0].querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value=mgStr(half)}}
  let cur=mgRound(packSourceRows(prefix).reduce((a,r)=>a+(parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0),0));
  let rest=mgRound(total-cur);
  c.querySelector('[data-pack-rows="'+prefix+'"]').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',packSourceRowHtml(prefix,lotId,rest>0?rest:'',oldMap));
  sel.value='';
  let lot=by('lots',lotId),prod=document.getElementById(prefix+'Product');
  if(lot&&lot.id&&prod&&!prod.value){try{fillSearchSelect(prefix+'Product',lot.product)}catch(e){prod.value=lot.product}}
  packSourceRefreshAdd(prefix,prod?prod.value:'');
  packSourceSum(prefix);
}
function collectPackSources(prefix,totalNet,productId,oldMap){
  let rows=readPackSourceRows(prefix);
  if(!rows.length)return{error:'Zgjidhni të paktën një lot burimor'};
  let ids=[],qtys={},sum=0,seen=new Set();
  for(let r of rows){
    let lot=by('lots',r.lot);if(!lot||!lot.id)return{error:'Loti burimor nuk u gjet'};
    if(seen.has(lot.id))return{error:'Loti '+lot.code+' është dy herë në listë'};seen.add(lot.id);
    if(!(r.qty>0))return{error:'Sasia për lotin '+lot.code+' duhet të jetë pozitive'};
    let cap=lotUnpacked(lot)+(((oldMap||{})[lot.id]!=null?+(oldMap[lot.id]):0));
    if(r.qty>cap+.001)return{error:'Tejkalim stoku te loti '+lot.code+': të lira '+fmt2(cap)+' kg'};
    if(productId&&lot.product!==productId)return{error:'Loti '+lot.code+' është i produktit tjetër'};
    let q=mgRound(r.qty);qtys[lot.id]=q;ids.push(lot.id);sum=mgRound(sum+q);
  }
  if(Math.abs(sum-totalNet)>.001)return{error:'Sasia nga lotet ('+fmt2(sum)+' kg) duhet të jetë '+fmt2(totalNet)+' kg'};
  return{sources:ids.map(id=>({lot:id,qty:qtys[id]})),qtys,ids};
}

</script>
<script id="biobes-multilot-v2b">
/* ---------- Ngarkesa: 2-3 porosi të së njëjtit klient në një kontejner ---------- */
function shipmentOrders(x){if(!x)return[];if(Array.isArray(x.orders)&&x.orders.length)return x.orders.slice();return x.order?[x.order]:[]}
function shipmentHasOrder(x,id){return shipmentOrders(x).includes(id)}
function containerPackTotals(orderIds){let net=0,bags=0;(orderIds||[]).forEach(oid=>{packs().filter(p=>p.order===oid).forEach(p=>{net=mgRound(net+packNet(p));bags+=(+p.bags||0)})});return{net,bags}}
function coreCreateShipment(data){
  if(state.shipments.some(x=>x.id===data.id))return coreResult(true,'',by('shipments',data.id));
  let orders=Array.isArray(data.orders)&&data.orders.length?data.orders.slice():(data.order?[data.order]:[]);
  if(!orders.length)return coreResult(false,'Porosia mungon');
  if(orders.length>3)return coreResult(false,'Më së shumti 3 porosi në një kontejner');
  let cust=data.customer||null;
  for(let oid of orders){let o=by('orders',oid);if(!o||!o.id)return coreResult(false,'Porosia '+oid+' nuk u gjet');if(!cust)cust=o.customer;else if(o.customer&&cust&&o.customer!==cust)return coreResult(false,'Të gjitha porositë e kontejnerit duhet të jenë të së njëjtit klient')}
  let x={...data,orders,order:orders[0],customer:cust||data.customer,status:'Planifikuar',stockDeducted:false};
  state.shipments.push(x);return coreResult(true,'',x);
}
function shipmentRequirements(x,next){let missing=[];if(next==='Në ngarkim'){if(!shipmentOrders(x).length)missing.push('porosia');if(!x.plate)missing.push('targa');if(!x.carrier)missing.push('transportuesi')}if(next==='Ngarkuar'){if(!(x.net>0))missing.push('pesha neto');if(!(x.gross>0))missing.push('pesha bruto');if(!(x.bags>0))missing.push('numri i thasëve')}if(next==='Vulosur'){if(!x.seal)missing.push('numri i plumbçes');if(!x.sealPhotoData)missing.push('fotografia e plumbçes')}if(next==='Dokumentuar'){let docs=state.documents.filter(d=>d.shipment===x.id),required=['Commercial Invoice','Packing List','CMR'];required.forEach(t=>{if(!docs.find(d=>d.type===t&&d.fileData))missing.push(t)})}if(next==='Ngarkuar'){
  let orders=new Set(shipmentOrders(x));
  let packed=packs().filter(p=>orders.has(p.order)).reduce((sum,p)=>sum+packNet(p),0);
  let shipped=state.shipments.filter(sh=>sh.id!==x.id&&sh.stockDeducted&&shipmentOrders(sh).some(o=>orders.has(o))).reduce((sum,sh)=>sum+(+sh.net||0),0);
  if((+x.net||0)>packed-shipped+0.001)missing.push('stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');
  if(+x.gross<+x.net)missing.push('bruto më e vogël se neto');
}
if(next==='Nisur'){
  let dossier=exportDossiers().find(d=>d.shipment===x.id);
  if(!dossier||dossier.status!=='Mbyllur')missing.push('dosja e eksportit e mbyllur');
  if(dossier&&dossierConsistencyChecks(dossier).some(c=>c.status!=='pass'))missing.push('mospërputhjet e dosjes');
}
return [...new Set(missing)]}
function coreAdvanceShipment(id,next){let x=by('shipments',id),cur=normalizedShipmentStatus(x),expected=shipmentStages[shipmentStages.indexOf(cur)+1];if(!x?.id||next!==expected)return coreResult(false,'Kalimi i statusit nuk është në rend');let missing=shipmentRequirements(x,next);if(missing.length)return coreResult(false,'Mungon: '+missing.join(', '));if(next==='Nisur'&&!x.stockDeducted){let os=new Set(shipmentOrders(x)),allocations=packs().filter(p=>os.has(p.order)),left=+x.net||0,lotIds=[...new Set(allocations.flatMap(pk=>pk.sourceLots||[]))],totalPacked=lotIds.reduce((a,lid)=>a+(+by('lots',lid)?.packedNet||0),0);if(totalPacked+.001<left)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');for(let lid of lotIds){let lot=by('lots',lid),take=Math.min(left,+lot?.packedNet||0);if(take){lot.packedNet-=take;lot.availableNet=Math.max(0,lotAvail(lot)-take);lot.net=lot.availableNet;coreMove({key:'SHIP:'+id+':'+lot.id,type:'OUT',source:'shipment',sourceId:id,lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:-take,unitCost:lot.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId});left-=take}}if(left>.001)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');x.stockDeducted=true;x.stockDeductedAt=new Date().toISOString()}x.status=next;return coreResult(true,'',x)}
function shipOrderOptionsHtml(chosen){let set=new Set(chosen||[]);return '<option value="">Shto porosi…</option>'+(state.orders||[]).filter(o=>!set.has(o.id)).map(o=>'<option value="'+o.id+'">'+esc(o.id+' — '+(by('customers',o.customer).name||''))+'</option>').join('')}
function shipOrderRowHtml(orderId){let o=by('orders',orderId),c=by('customers',o?o.customer:'');return '<div data-ship-order-row data-order="'+orderId+'" style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:6px;padding:6px 8px;border:1px solid #d4dcd6;border-radius:8px;background:#f7faf8"><div style="flex:1;min-width:150px"><b>'+esc(orderId)+'</b><br><small>'+esc(c?.name||'')+' · '+esc(c?.country||'')+'</small></div><div style="flex:0 0 42px;text-align:right"><button type="button" class="btn small danger" title="Hiq porosinë" onclick="shipOrderRemoveRow(this)">✕</button></div></div>'}
function shipOrderRows(){return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-ship-order-row]'))}
function shipOrderIds(){return shipOrderRows().map(r=>r.dataset.order)}
function shipOrderSync(){let ids=shipOrderIds(),o=by('orders',ids[0]),c=document.getElementById('shCustomer');if(c&&o&&o.customer)c.value=o.customer;let t=containerPackTotals(ids),net=document.getElementById('shNet'),bags=document.getElementById('shBags');if(net&&!net.dataset.touched)net.value=mgStr(t.net);if(bags&&!bags.dataset.touched)bags.value=t.bags;let el=document.getElementById('shOrderSum');if(el)el.innerHTML=ids.length?('Porosi: <b>'+ids.length+'</b> · nga paketimet: <b>'+fmt2(t.net)+' kg</b> dhe <b>'+t.bags+'</b> thasë (neto/thasë plotësohen auto, mund t’i ndryshoni)'):'Shto të paktën një porosi të kontejnerit'}

function shipOrderAdd(){let sel=document.getElementById('shOrderAdd'),id=sel?sel.value:'';if(!id)return;let ids=shipOrderIds();if(ids.includes(id))return toast('Kjo porosi është tashmë në kontejner');if(ids.length>=3)return toast('Më së shumti 3 porosi në një kontejner');let o=by('orders',id),first=by('orders',ids[0]);if(first&&first.id&&o&&o.customer&&first.customer&&o.customer!==first.customer)return toast('Të gjitha porositë e kontejnerit duhet të jenë të së njëjtit klient');let box=document.querySelector('[data-ship-orders]');if(!box)return;box.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',shipOrderRowHtml(id));sel.value='';shipOrderRefresh()}

/* ---------- Përpunim: disa lote hyrëse me kg për lot ---------- */
function processCandidateLots(productId){return (state.lots||[]).filter(l=>!String(l.status||'').toLowerCase().includes('anuluar')&&(!productId||l.product===productId)&&lotAvail(l)>0.0000005)}
function processSourceRowHtml(lotId,qty){let lot=by('lots',lotId);if(!lot||!lot.id)return'';let p=by('products',lot.product)||{};return '<div data-pr-source-row data-lot="'+lotId+'" style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:6px;padding:6px 8px;border:1px solid #d4dcd6;border-radius:8px;background:#f7faf8"><div style="flex:1;min-width:150px"><b>'+esc(lot.code)+'</b><br><small>'+esc((p.code||'')+' — '+(p.name||''))+' · i lirë '+fmt2(lotAvail(lot))+' kg</small></div><div style="flex:0 0 200px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px"><input type="number" step="0.000001" min="0" readonly value="'+mgStr(+qty||0)+'" data-pr-qty title="KG nga ky lot (shpërndahet auto)" style="width:140px;background:#eef2ef"> <span>kg</span></div><div style="flex:0 0 42px;text-align:right"><button type="button" class="btn small danger" title="Hiq lotin" onclick="processSourceRemoveRow(this)">✕</button></div></div>'}

window.downloadTracePhotos = function(lotId){
  const lot = by('lots', lotId);
  const photos = collectLotPhotos(lot);
  if(!photos.length) return toast('Nuk ka foto');
  photos.forEach((p,i)=>{
    setTimeout(()=>{
      const a=document.createElement('a');
      a.href=p.data; a.download=p.name|| (p.title + '-' + (i+1));
      a.click();
    }, i*300);
  });
  toast('Shkarkimi i '+photos.length+' fotove filloi');
};

const _origViewsTrace = views.trace;
views.trace = function(){
  const base = _origViewsTrace();
  // add global gallery shortcut below
  const all = collectAllPhotos();
  const preview = all.slice(0,8);
  const previewHtml = preview.length
    ? `<div class="card"><h3>🖼 Fotot e fundit — ${all.length} skedarë në sistem</h3><div class="photo-grid">${preview.map((p,i)=> photoCardHtml(p,i)).join('')}</div><div class="actions" style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn" onclick="go('photos')">Shiko të gjitha fotot →</button></div></div>`
    : `<div class="card"><h3>🖼 Galeria e fotove</h3><div class="photo-empty">Nuk ka foto të ngarkuara ende.<br><small>Fotot e peshimit, mostrës, porosisë, plumbçes dhe dokumenteve shfaqen këtu.</small></div></div>`;
  return base + previewHtml;
};

views.photos = function(){
  const all = collectAllPhotos();
  const groups = [...new Set(all.map(p=>p.group))];
  const activeFilter = window._photosFilter || 'Të gjitha';
  const filtered = activeFilter==='Të gjitha' ? all : all.filter(p=> p.group===activeFilter);
  window._lastGalleryPhotos = filtered;
  const filterBtns = ['Të gjitha', ...groups].map(g=> `<button class="${g===activeFilter?'active':''}" onclick="window._photosFilter='${g.replace(/'/g,"\\'")}'; render()">${esc(g)}</button>`).join('');
  return head('Fotot','Galeria e plotë — ndiq fotot nga peshimi te eksporti', `<button class="btn" onclick="go('trace')">⌘ Gjurmueshmëria</button> <span class="pill green">${all.length} skedarë</span>`)
    + `<div class="card"><div class="toolbar"><input placeholder="Kërko foto me titull, grup ose lot..." oninput="filterPhotosList(this.value)"><div class="photo-filter" id="photosFilterBar">${filterBtns}</div></div>`
    + (filtered.length ? `<div class="photo-grid" id="allPhotosGrid">${filtered.map((p,i)=> photoCardHtml(p,i)).join('')}</div>` : `<div class="photo-empty">Nuk ka foto për filtrin "${esc(activeFilter)}"</div>`)
    + `</div>`
    + `<div class="card"><h3>Si funksionon "Ndiq fotot"</h3><p class="hint">Çdo foto e ngarkuar lidhet automatikisht me lotin dhe rrjedhën dokumentare:</p><ul style="line-height:1.7"><li><b>Peshimi:</b> kuponi elektronik i peshores — shfaqet te loti dhe te gjurmueshmëria</li><li><b>Mostra:</b> analiza / CoA — shihet te loti burimor</li><li><b>Porosia:</b> dokumenti i klientit (PO)</li><li><b>Plumbçe & Dokumente eksporti:</b> foto e plumbçes + Invoice, Packing List, CMR etj. — ndiqen deri te ngarkesa</li></ul><p><b>Këshillë:</b> kërkoni lotin te <a class="link" onclick="go('trace')">Gjurmueshmëria</a> dhe hapni <b>📸 Ndiq fotot</b> për të parë gjithë galerinë e lidhur.</p></div>`;
};
window.filterPhotosList = function(q){
  q=(q||'').toLowerCase();
  const grid=document.getElementById('allPhotosGrid');
  if(!grid) return;
  grid.querySelectorAll('.photo-card').forEach(card=>{
    const txt = card.textContent.toLowerCase();
    card.style.display = txt.includes(q) ? '' : 'none';
  });
};

// Helper: free stock
function pkFree(lot, oldMap){
  let extra = (oldMap && oldMap[lot.id] != null) ? +oldMap[lot.id] : 0;
  return lotUnpacked(lot) + extra;
}

// Override packSourceRowHtml to return <tr>
window.packSourceRowHtml = function(prefix, lotId, qty, oldMap){
  oldMap = oldMap || {};
  const lot = by('lots', lotId);
  if(!lot || !lot.id) return '';
  const sup = by('suppliers', lot.supplier);
  const free = pkFree(lot, oldMap);
  const qtyVal = (qty===''||qty==null) ? mgStr(free) : mgStr(+qty||0);
  return `<tr data-pack-source-row="${prefix}" data-lot="${lotId}">
    <td><b>${esc(lot.code)}</b><br><small style="color:#6b7d75">${esc(lot.code)}</small></td>
    <td>${esc(sup.code||'—')}<br><small>${esc(sup.name||'')}</small></td>
    <td>${fmt2(free)} kg</td>
    <td><input type="number" step="0.001" min="0" value="${qtyVal}" data-pack-qty oninput="packSourceSum('${prefix}')" style="width:110px"> kg</td>
    <td><button type="button" class="btn small danger" onclick="packSourceRemoveRow(this,'${prefix}')" title="Fshi rreshtin">×</button></td>
  </tr>`;
};

// Override packSourcePickerHtml to use table + KPIs + modal button
window.packSourcePickerHtml = function(prefix, selected, productId, packId){
  selected = selected || [];
  let oldMap={};
  if(packId){
    const ex = packById(packId);
    (ex && ex.sourceLots||[]).forEach(id=> oldMap[id]=packLotQty(ex,id));
  }
  // build initial rows
  const rows = selected.map(z=> packSourceRowHtml(prefix, z.lot, z.qty, oldMap)).join('');
  const totalNeeded = 0; // will be computed in packSourceSum
  return `<div class="field full pk-card" data-pack-source="${prefix}" data-pack-bags="${prefix}Bags" data-pack-bw="${prefix}BagWeight" data-pack-old='${esc(JSON.stringify(oldMap))}'>
    <h4>Lotet burimore (përzierja) * <small style="font-weight:400;color:#6b7d75">— përzierje shumë-lotëshe</small></h4>
    <div class="table-wrap" style="border:1px solid #dfe6e2;border-radius:8px;overflow:auto">
      <table class="pk-table">
        <thead><tr><th>Loti burimor</th><th>Furnitori</th><th>Stok i papaketuar</th><th>KG të marra</th><th></th></tr></thead>
        <tbody data-pack-rows="${prefix}">${rows || ''}</tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    <div id="${prefix}Empty" class="pk-empty" style="${selected.length?'display:none':''}">Nuk ka lot të shtuar — shto nga magazina.</div>
    <div style="margin-top:8px;display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap">
      <button type="button" class="btn small primary" onclick="showPackLotModal('${prefix}')">+ Shto lot</button>
      <button type="button" class="btn small" onclick="packSourceDistribute('${prefix}')">Shpërnda njëlloj</button>
      <span class="hint" style="align-self:center">Vetëm lote me stok të papaketuar &gt;0 dhe të së njëjtit produkt.</span>
    </div>
    <div class="pk-kpis">
      <div class="pk-kpi" id="${prefix}KpiTotal"><small>Totali nga lotet</small><b>0.00 kg</b></div>
      <div class="pk-kpi" id="${prefix}KpiNeed"><small>Duhet</small><b>0.00 kg</b></div>
      <div class="pk-kpi" id="${prefix}KpiDiff"><small>Diferenca</small><b>0.00 kg</b></div>
    </div>
    <p class="hint" data-pack-sum="${prefix}" style="margin:6px 0 0;display:none"></p>
  </div>`;
};

// Show modal with available lots
window.showPackLotModal = function(prefix){
  try{
    const prodId = $val(prefix+'Product');
    // if no product selected, show all with stock
    let lots = packagingCandidateLots(prodId).filter(l=> lotUnpacked(l) > 0.001);
    // filter out already selected
    const chosen = new Set(packSourceRows(prefix).map(r=> r.dataset.lot));
    lots = lots.filter(l=> !chosen.has(l.id));
    if(!lots.length){
      return toast('Nuk ka lote të disponueshme për këtë produkt/magazinë');
    }
    const rows = lots.map(l=>{
      const sup = by('suppliers', l.supplier);
      const p = by('products', l.product);
      return `<tr>
        <td><b>${esc(l.code)}</b><br><small>${esc(p.code+' — '+p.name)}</small></td>
        <td>${esc(sup.code||'—')}<br><small>${esc(sup.name||'')}</small></td>
        <td>${fmt2(lotUnpacked(l))} kg<br><small>${esc(by('warehouses', l.warehouse).name||'')}</small></td>
        <td><button class="btn small primary" onclick="packLotModalAdd('${prefix}','${l.id}')">Shto</button></td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join('');
    modal('Zgjedh lotet burimore', `<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Loti</th><th>Furnitori</th><th>Stok i papaketuar</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div><p class="hint">Vetëm lote të papaketuara &gt;0. Pasi zgjedh, vendos KG të marra te tabela kryesore.</p>`, `<button class="btn" onclick="closeModal(); setTimeout(()=>{if(typeof enhanceEntitySelects==='function') enhanceEntitySelects();},50)">Mbyll</button>`);
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); toast('Gabim: '+e.message); }
};

    closeModal();

      // need to reopen packaging form? Actually we closed lot modal, but packaging modal was closed? We used modal() which replaces packaging modal. Need to restore packaging form.
      // Instead we should not close packaging modal but show lot selector as nested modal? For now we close selector and re-add to packaging form, then reopen packaging form is lost.
      // Workaround: we stored packaging form state before opening modal? Simpler: close selector modal and then directly add row to the packaging form which is no longer visible because modal was overwritten.
      // So we need to keep packaging form data and reopen it.
      // Instead we will not use modal() for selector but use a custom overlay.
      // For now, fallback: add to a temp store and reopen packaging form? This is complex.
      // Alternative approach: directly add row without closing packaging modal by using a custom div overlay instead of modal().
      toast('Loti u shtua — rihape formularin dhe shto përsëri');
    }, 100);
  }catch(e){}
};

// Better implementation: custom overlay for lot selector that does not close packaging modal
// Override showPackLotModal to use overlay div
window.showPackLotModal = function(prefix){
  try{
    // remove existing overlay
    let old = document.getElementById('pkLotOverlay');
    if(old) old.remove();
    const prodId = $val(prefix+'Product');
    let lots = packagingCandidateLots(prodId).filter(l=> lotUnpacked(l) > 0.001);
    const wareSel = document.getElementById(prefix+'Warehouse');
    const wareId = wareSel ? wareSel.value : '';
    if(wareId) lots = lots.filter(l=> l.warehouse===wareId);
    const chosen = new Set(packSourceRows(prefix).map(r=> r.dataset.lot));
    lots = lots.filter(l=> !chosen.has(l.id));
    if(!lots.length){
      return toast('Nuk ka lote të disponueshme për magazinën/produktin');
    }
    const overlay = document.createElement('div');
    overlay.id='pkLotOverlay';
    overlay.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;background:#13251d88;z-index:90;display:flex;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:center;padding:35px 14px;overflow:auto';
    const box = document.createElement('div');
    box.style.cssText='background:#fff;width:min(700px,100%);border-radius:11px;box-shadow:0 20px 70px #0004;overflow:hidden';
    const rows = lots.map(l=>{
      const sup = by('suppliers', l.supplier);
      const p = by('products', l.product);
      return `<tr>
        <td><b>${esc(l.code)}</b><br><small>${esc(p.code||'')} — ${esc(p.name)}</small></td>
        <td>${esc(sup.code||'—')}<br><small>${esc(sup.name||'')}</small></td>
        <td>${fmt2(lotUnpacked(l))} kg</td>
        <td><button class="btn small primary" data-add-lot="${l.id}">Shto</button></td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join('');
    box.innerHTML = `<div style="padding:16px 19px;border-bottom:1px solid #dfe6e2;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center"><h3 style="margin:0">Zgjedh lotet — ${esc(by('products', prodId).name||'Të gjitha')}</h3><button class="btn small" id="pkLotClose">✕</button></div><div style="padding:19px;max-height:60vh;overflow:auto"><div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Loti</th><th>Furnitori</th><th>Stok i papaketuar</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div></div><div style="padding:14px 19px;border-top:1px solid #dfe6e2;display:flex;justify-content:flex-end"><button class="btn" id="pkLotClose2">Mbyll</button></div>`;
    overlay.appendChild(box);
    document.body.appendChild(overlay);
    const close = ()=> overlay.remove();
    box.querySelectorAll('#pkLotClose, #pkLotClose2').forEach(b=> b.onclick=close);
    overlay.addEventListener('click', e=>{ if(e.target===overlay) close(); });
    box.querySelectorAll('[data-add-lot]').forEach(btn=>{
      btn.onclick = ()=>{
        const lotId = btn.dataset.addLot;
        // add row to packaging form
        const c = packSourceContainer(prefix);
        const oldMap = packSourceOldMap(prefix);
        const total = packSourceTarget(prefix);
        const cur = packSourceRows(prefix).reduce((a,r)=> a + (parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0), 0);
        // if single row and balanced, split?
        const rowsEl = packSourceRows(prefix);
        if(rowsEl.length===1){
          let q1 = parseFloat(rowsEl[0].querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0;
          if(Math.abs(q1 - total) <= 0.01){
            let half = Math.round(total/2*1000)/1000;
            rowsEl[0].querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value = fmt2(half);
          }
        }
        let rest = Math.round((total - packSourceRows(prefix).reduce((a,r)=> a + (parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0),0))*1000)/1000;
        const tbody = document.querySelector(`[data-pack-rows="${prefix}"]`);
        if(tbody){
          const lotForQty = by('lots', lotId);
          const freeForQty = lotForQty && lotForQty.id ? pkFree(lotForQty, oldMap) : 0;
          const suggestedQty = rest>0 ? Math.min(freeForQty, rest) : freeForQty;
          tbody.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', packSourceRowHtml(prefix, lotId, suggestedQty, oldMap));
          const lot = by('lots', lotId);
          const prodSel = document.getElementById(prefix+'Product');
          if(lot && lot.id && prodSel && !prodSel.value){
            try{ fillSearchSelect(prefix+'Product', lot.product); }catch(e){ prodSel.value = lot.product; }
          }
          packSourceSum(prefix);
          // remove this lot from overlay table
          btn.closest('tr').remove();
          if(!box.querySelectorAll('[data-add-lot]').length){
            close();
            toast('Loti u shtua');
          } else {
            btn.textContent='✓';
            btn.disabled=true;
          }
        }
      };
    });
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); toast('Gabim: '+e.message); }
};

// Update KPI logic
const origPackSourceSum = window.packSourceSum;
window.packSourceSum = function(prefix){
  try{
    const c = packSourceContainer(prefix);
    if(!c) return;
    const rows = packSourceRows(prefix);
    const totalNeeded = ($num(prefix+'Bags',0))*($num(prefix+'BagWeight',0));
    // if single row and needed >0, auto-fill
    if(rows.length===1 && totalNeeded>0){
      const inp = rows[0].querySelector('[data-pack-qty]');
      if(inp && !String(inp.value||'').trim()){
        const lot = by('lots', rows[0].dataset.lot);
        const free = lot && lot.id ? pkFree(lot, packSourceOldMap(prefix)) : 0;
        inp.value = mgStr(Math.min(free, totalNeeded));
      }
    }
    let sum = 0;
    rows.forEach(r=>{
      const v = parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]')?.value)||0;
      sum += v;
    });
    sum = Math.round(sum*1000)/1000;
    const diff = Math.round((totalNeeded - sum)*1000)/1000;
    // update empty placeholder
    const empty = document.getElementById(prefix+'Empty');
    if(empty) empty.style.display = rows.length ? 'none' : '';
    // update KPIs
    const kTotal = document.getElementById(prefix+'KpiTotal');
    const kNeed = document.getElementById(prefix+'KpiNeed');
    const kDiff = document.getElementById(prefix+'KpiDiff');
    if(kTotal) kTotal.querySelector('b').textContent = fmt2(sum)+' kg';
    if(kNeed) kNeed.querySelector('b').textContent = fmt2(totalNeeded)+' kg';
    if(kDiff){
      kDiff.querySelector('b').textContent = fmt2(diff)+' kg';
      kDiff.className = 'pk-kpi ' + (Math.abs(diff)<=0.01 ? 'good' : (Math.abs(diff) < 1 ? 'warn' : 'bad'));
    }
    // also update hidden sum hint
    const el = c.querySelector('[data-pack-sum="'+prefix+'"]');
    if(el){
      el.textContent = `Totali ${fmt2(sum)} / Duhet ${fmt2(totalNeeded)} / Diferenca ${fmt2(diff)}`;
    }
    // call original for compatibility but we already did
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); }
};

window.packRequireAdminOverride = async function(overruns){
  const base = (typeof serverBaseUrl!=='undefined' ? String(serverBaseUrl||'') : '').replace(/\/+$/,'');
  if(!base){ toast('Override kërkon lidhje me serverin dhe verifikim administratori'); return null; }
  return await new Promise(resolve=>{
    const old=document.getElementById('pkAdminOverrideOverlay'); if(old) old.remove();
    const ov=document.createElement('div'); ov.id='pkAdminOverrideOverlay';
    ov.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;background:#13251dcc;z-index:120;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:16px';
    const box=document.createElement('div'); box.style.cssText='background:#fff;width:min(520px,100%);border-radius:12px;padding:18px;box-shadow:0 20px 70px #0005';
    const detail=overruns.map(x=>`${esc(x.code)}: ${fmt2(x.qty)} kg > stok ${fmt2(x.cap)} kg`).join('<br>');
    box.innerHTML=`<h3 style="margin-top:0">Autorizim administratori</h3><p class="hint">Sasia tejkalon stokun e papaketuar. Vazhdimi kërkon administrator dhe arsye.</p><div class="card" style="margin-bottom:12px">${detail}</div><div class="field"><label>Admin username</label><input id="pkAdminUser" autocomplete="username"></div><div class="field"><label>Admin password</label><input id="pkAdminPass" type="password" autocomplete="current-password"></div><div class="field"><label>Arsyeja *</label><textarea id="pkAdminReason" rows="3" placeholder="Shpjego pse lejohet tejkalimi"></textarea></div><div id="pkAdminErr" style="color:#a32934;margin:8px 0"></div><div style="display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;gap:8px"><button class="btn" id="pkAdminCancel">Anulo</button><button class="btn primary" id="pkAdminApprove">Autorizo</button></div>`;
    ov.appendChild(box); document.body.appendChild(ov);
    const done=v=>{ const pw=box.querySelector('#pkAdminPass'); if(pw) pw.value=''; ov.remove(); resolve(v); };
    box.querySelector('#pkAdminCancel').onclick=()=>done(null);
    box.querySelector('#pkAdminApprove').onclick=async()=>{
      const username=box.querySelector('#pkAdminUser').value.trim();
      const password=box.querySelector('#pkAdminPass').value;
      const reason=box.querySelector('#pkAdminReason').value.trim();
      const err=box.querySelector('#pkAdminErr');
      if(!username||!password||!reason){ err.textContent='Plotëso administratorin, password-in dhe arsyen.'; return; }
      try{
        const resp=await fetch(base+'/api/auth/login',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({username,password})});
        const data=await resp.json().catch(()=>({}));
        if(!resp.ok || !data.ok){ err.textContent=data.error||'Autorizimi dështoi'; return; }
        if(!data.user || data.user.role!=='ROLE-ADMIN'){
          if(data.token) fetch(base+'/api/auth/logout',{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+data.token}}).catch(()=>{});
          err.textContent='Kredencialet nuk i përkasin një administratori.'; return;
        }
        if(data.token) fetch(base+'/api/auth/logout',{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+data.token}}).catch(()=>{});
        done({admin:data.user.username||username,reason,time:new Date().toISOString(),overruns:overruns.map(x=>({lot:x.lotId,code:x.code,qty:x.qty,stock:x.cap}))});
      }catch(e){ err.textContent='Serveri nuk përgjigjet — override nuk lejohet offline.'; }
    };
  });
};

// Override savePackaging to handle 

### lotUnpacked(lot) @ 1866743
= mgStr(Math.min(free, totalNeeded));
      }
    }
    let sum = 0;
    rows.forEach(r=>{
      const v = parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]')?.value)||0;
      sum += v;
    });
    sum = Math.round(sum*1000)/1000;
    const diff = Math.round((totalNeeded - sum)*1000)/1000;
    // update empty placeholder
    const empty = document.getElementById(prefix+'Empty');
    if(empty) empty.style.display = rows.length ? 'none' : '';
    // update KPIs
    const kTotal = document.getElementById(prefix+'KpiTotal');
    const kNeed = document.getElementById(prefix+'KpiNeed');
    const kDiff = document.getElementById(prefix+'KpiDiff');
    if(kTotal) kTotal.querySelector('b').textContent = fmt2(sum)+' kg';
    if(kNeed) kNeed.querySelector('b').textContent = fmt2(totalNeeded)+' kg';
    if(kDiff){
      kDiff.querySelector('b').textContent = fmt2(diff)+' kg';
      kDiff.className = 'pk-kpi ' + (Math.abs(diff)<=0.01 ? 'good' : (Math.abs(diff) < 1 ? 'warn' : 'bad'));
    }
    // also update hidden sum hint
    const el = c.querySelector('[data-pack-sum="'+prefix+'"]');
    if(el){
      el.textContent = `Totali ${fmt2(sum)} / Duhet ${fmt2(totalNeeded)} / Diferenca ${fmt2(diff)}`;
    }
    // call original for compatibility but we already did
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); }
};

window.packRequireAdminOverride = async function(overruns){
  const base = (typeof serverBaseUrl!=='undefined' ? String(serverBaseUrl||'') : '').replace(/\/+$/,'');
  if(!base){ toast('Override kërkon lidhje me serverin dhe verifikim administratori'); return null; }
  return await new Promise(resolve=>{
    const old=document.getElementById('pkAdminOverrideOverlay'); if(old) old.remove();
    const ov=document.createElement('div'); ov.id='pkAdminOverrideOverlay';
    ov.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;background:#13251dcc;z-index:120;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:16px';
    const box=document.createElement('div'); box.style.cssText='background:#fff;width:min(520px,100%);border-radius:12px;padding:18px;box-shadow:0 20px 70px #0005';
    const detail=overruns.map(x=>`${esc(x.code)}: ${fmt2(x.qty)} kg > stok ${fmt2(x.cap)} kg`).join('<br>');
    box.innerHTML=`<h3 style="margin-top:0">Autorizim administratori</h3><p class="hint">Sasia tejkalon stokun e papaketuar. Vazhdimi kërkon administrator dhe arsye.</p><div class="card" style="margin-bottom:12px">${detail}</div><div class="field"><label>Admin username</label><input id="pkAdminUser" autocomplete="username"></div><div class="field"><label>Admin password</label><input id="pkAdminPass" type="password" autocomplete="current-password"></div><div class="field"><label>Arsyeja *</label><textarea id="pkAdminReason" rows="3" placeholder="Shpjego pse lejohet tejkalimi"></textarea></div><div id="pkAdminErr" style="color:#a32934;margin:8px 0"></div><div style="display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;gap:8px"><button class="btn" id="pkAdminCancel">Anulo</button><button class="btn primary" id="pkAdminApprove">Autorizo</button></div>`;
    ov.appendChild(box); document.body.appendChild(ov);
    const done=v=>{ const pw=box.querySelector('#pkAdminPass'); if(pw) pw.value=''; ov.remove(); resolve(v); };
    box.querySelector('#pkAdminCancel').onclick=()=>done(null);
    box.querySelector('#pkAdminApprove').onclick=async()=>{
      const username=box.querySelector('#pkAdminUser').value.trim();
      const password=box.querySelector('#pkAdminPass').value;
      const reason=box.querySelector('#pkAdminReason').value.trim();
      const err=box.querySelector('#pkAdminErr');
      if(!username||!password||!reason){ err.textContent='Plotëso administratorin, password-in dhe arsyen.'; return; }
      try{
        const resp=await fetch(base+'/api/auth/login',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({username,password})});
        const data=await resp.json().catch(()=>({}));
        if(!resp.ok || !data.ok){ err.textContent=data.error||'Autorizimi dështoi'; return; }
        if(!data.user || data.user.role!=='ROLE-ADMIN'){
          if(data.token) fetch(base+'/api/auth/logout',{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+data.token}}).catch(()=>{});
          err.textContent='Kredencialet nuk i përkasin një administratori.'; return;
        }
        if(data.token) fetch(base+'/api/auth/logout',{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+data.token}}).catch(()=>{});
        done({admin:data.user.username||username,reason,time:new Date().toISOString(),overruns:overruns.map(x=>({lot:x.lotId,code:x.code,qty:x.qty,stock:x.cap}))});
      }catch(e){ err.textContent='Serveri nuk përgjigjet — override nuk lejohet offline.'; }
    };
  });
};

// Override savePackaging to handle multi-lot with validation and stockMove
window.savePackaging = async function(){
  try{
    const order = $val('pkOrder');
    const product = $val('pkProduct');
    const internal = $val('pkInternal').trim();
    const o = by('orders', order);
    if(!order || !product || !internal) return toast('Plotësoni porosinë, produktin dhe lotin BioBes');
    const bags = $num('pkBags',0);
    const bagWeight = $num('pkBagWeight',0);
    if(!(bags>0) || !(bagWeight>0)) return toast('Thasët dhe kg/thes duhet të jenë më të mëdha se zero');
    const total = Math.round(bags*bagWeight*1000)/1000;
    const rows = packSourceRows('pk');
    if(!rows.length) return toast('Zgjidhni të paktën një lot burimor');
    // collect sources
    const seen=new Set();
    const sources=[];
    const overruns=[];
    let sum=0;
    for(let r of rows){
      const lotId = r.dataset.lot;
      const lot = by('lots', lotId);
      if(!lot || !lot.id) return toast('Loti burimor nuk u gjet');
      if(seen.has(lotId)) return toast('Loti '+lot.code+' është shtuar dy herë');
      seen.add(lotId);
      const qty = parseFloat(r.querySelector('[data-pack-qty]').value)||0;
      if(!(qty>0)) return toast('Sasia për lotin '+lot.code+' duhet të jetë pozitive');
      const cap = lotUnpacked(lot);
      if(qty > cap + 0.001) overruns.push({lotId:lotId,code:lot.code,qty:qty,cap:cap});
      if(lot.product !== product) return toast('Loti '+lot.code+' është i produktit tjetër — përzieni vetëm lotë të së njëjtit produkt');
      const q = Math.round(qty*1000)/1000;
      sources.push({lot: lotId, qty: q});
      sum = Math.round((sum+q)*1000)/1000;
    }
    let adminOverride = null;
    if(overruns.length){
      adminOverride = await packRequireAdminOverride(overruns);
      if(!adminOverride) return;
    }
    if(Math.abs(sum - total) > 0.01) return toast('Sasia nga lotet ('+fmt2(sum)+' kg) duhet të jetë '+fmt2(total)+' kg (diferenca '+fmt2(total-sum)+' kg)');
    const data = {
      id:nextSeqId('PK',packs(),1,new Date().getFullYear()),
      order: order,
      customer: o.customer,
      product: product,
      internalLot: internal,
      customerLot: $val('pkCustomer'),
      template: $val('pkTemplate'),
      bags: bags,
      bagWeight: bagWeight,
      cropYear: $num('pkCrop', new Date().getFullYear()),
      bestBefore: $num('pkBest', new Date().getFullYear()+3),
      cert: $val('pkCert'),
      organic: $val('pkOrganic'),
      originType: $val('pkOriginType'),
      part: $val('pkPart'),
      origin: $val('pkOrigin'),
      date: new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),
      sources: sources,
      adminOverride: adminOverride
    };
    const r = atomicTransaction('Paketim', ()=> corePackage(data));
    if(!r || !r.ok) return toast((r&&r.error)||'Paketimi dështoi');
    if(adminOverride){
      state.events = state.events || [];
      const od = adminOverride.overruns.map(x=>x.code+': '+fmt2(x.qty)+' kg / stok '+fmt2(x.stock)+' kg').join('; ');
      state.events.push({date:new Date().toLocaleString('sq-AL'),type:'Admin override',ref:r.record.id,text:'Tejkalim stoku në paketim. Admin: '+adminOverride.admin+'. Arsye: '+adminOverride.reason+'. '+od});
    }
    save();
    closeModal();
    render();
    setTimeout(()=> packagingCard(r.record.id), 80);
    toast('Paketimi u krijua');
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); toast('Gabim gjatë ruajtjes: '+e.message); }
};

    <div class="field"><label>Produkti</label><select id="pkProduct" onchange="packSourceRefreshForProduct('pk',this.value)">${opts(state.products,x=>x.code+' — '+x.name)}</select></div>

// Helper for Burime column: "3 lot (001+003+005)" — show last 3 chars of code
function burimeLabel(x){
  const lots = packSourceLots(x);
  if(!lots.length) return '—';
  const codes = lots.map(l=>{
    const c = (l.code||'').replace(/^B\d*/,'');
    // take last 3 or full code for readability
    return esc(l.code);
  });
  // spec example: "3 lot (001+003)" — show count and codes joined with +
  // we show: '3 lot (B1... + B1...)'
  const short = lots.map(l=>{
    const m = l.code.match(/(\d+)$/);
    return m ? m[1].slice(-3) : l.code;
  }).join('+');
  return `${lots.length} lot (${short})`;
}

const _origViewsPackaging = views.packaging;
views.packaging = function(){
  const a = packs();
  return head('Paketimi & Etiketat','Lotet përfundimtare dhe skedarët për BarTender — redaktim dhe fshirje direkte',`<button class="btn primary" onclick="packagingForm()">+ Paketim i ri</button>`)
    + tableCard(['Paketimi','Porosia','Produkti','Burime','Loti BioBes','Loti klientit','Thasë','Neto','Modeli','Veprime'], a.map(x=>{
      const lots = packSourceLots(x);
      const burime = lots.length ? `${lots.length} lot (${lots.map(l=> esc((l.code||'').split('-').pop()||l.code)).join('+')})` : '—';
      // For spec example "3 lot (001+003)" we keep as above
      return [
        `<a class="link" onclick="packagingCard('${x.id}')">${x.id}</a>`,

        esc(x.internalLot||'—'),
        esc(x.customerLot||'—'),
        x.bags,
        n(packNet(x))+' kg',
        esc(x.template||'—'),
        `<span class="row-actions">
          <button class="btn small" onclick="packagingCard('${x.id}')">Hap</button>
          <button class="btn small" onclick="packagingEditForm('${x.id}')">Ndrysho</button>
          <button class="btn small danger" data-admin-only onclick="packagingDelete('${x.id}')">Fshi</button>
          <button class="btn small" onclick="copyPackaging('${x.id}')">Kopjo</button>
        </span>`
      ];
    }));
};

// Add Kopjo function
window.copyPackaging = function(id){
  try{
    const x = packById(id);
    if(!x) return toast('Paketimi nuk u gjet');
    const newId = nextSeqId('PK',packs(),1,new Date().getFullYear());
    const clone = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x));
    clone.id = newId;
    clone.internalLot = (x.internalLot||'') + '-KOPI';
    clone.date = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
    // need to create new stock moves? Instead we should create via corePackage with new sources same as original
    // For Kopjo we can just open form prefilled?
    // Simpler: open form with same data, let user save
    packagingForm();
    setTimeout(()=>{
      try{
        $set('pkOrder', x.order);
        fillSearchSelect('pkOrder', x.order);
        $set('pkProduct', x.product);
        fillSearchSelect('pkProduct', x.product);
        $set('pkInternal', clone.internalLot);
        $set('pkCustomer', x.customerLot||'');
        $set('pkBags', x.bags);
        $set('pkBagWeight', x.bagWeight);
        $set('pkTemplate', x.template||'BioBes standard');
        $set('pkCrop', x.cropYear);
        $set('pkBest', x.bestBefore);
        $set('pkCert', x.cert||'');
        $set('pkOrganic', x.organic||'Organik');
        $set('pkOriginType', x.originType||'Wild collected');
        $set('pkPart', x.part||'Folia');
        $set('pkOrigin', x.origin||'Albania');
        // add source lots
        const c = packSourceContainer('pk');
        const tbody = document.querySelector('[data-pack-rows="pk"]');
        if(tbody){
          tbody.innerHTML='';
          (x.sourceLots||[]).forEach(lotId=>{
            const qty = packLotQty(x, lotId);
            tbody.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', packSourceRowHtml('pk', lotId, qty, {}));
          });
          packSourceSum('pk');
        }
        toast('Formulari u kopjua — ruaje si paketim të ri');
      }catch(e){ console.error(e); }
    },120);
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); toast('Gabim: '+e.message); }
};

// Also ensure edit form uses same table logic (already via packSourcePickerHtml, so it will)
// Patch packSourceSum for edit prefix 'pe' as well — our new version already handles any prefix

// Ensure coreAdvanceShipment correctly deducts from each source lot (overwrite to be precise)
const origCoreAdvanceShipment = window.coreAdvanceShipment;
window.coreAdvanceShipment = function(id, next){
  const x=by('shipments',id),cur=normalizedShipmentStatus(x);
  const expected=shipmentStages[shipmentStages.indexOf(cur)+1];
  if(!x?.id||next!==expected)return coreResult(false,'Kalimi i statusit nuk është në rend');
  const missing=shipmentRequirements(x,next);
  if(missing.length)return coreResult(false,'Mungon: '+missing.join(', '));
  if(next==='Nisur'&&!x.stockDeducted){
    const orders=new Set(shipmentOrders(x));
    const allocations=packs().filter(p=>orders.has(p.order));
    const plan=new Map();let left=+x.net||0;
    if(!(left>0))return coreResult(false,'Pesha neto duhet pozitive');
    // Plan the entire shipment before changing stock. Partial shipments must not
    // deduct every package belonging to the order, or silently clamp a shortage.
    for(const pack of allocations){
      for(const lotId of (pack.sourceLots||[])){
        const lot=by('lots',lotId),reserved=plan.get(lotId)||0;
        if(!lot.id) return coreResult(false,'Loti burimor nuk u gjet');
        const free=Math.max(0,Math.min(+lot.packedNet||0,lotAvail(lot))-reserved);
        const take=Math.min(left,packLotQty(pack,lotId),free);
        if(take>0){plan.set(lotId,reserved+take);left-=take;}
      }
    }
    if(left>0.001)return coreResult(false,'Stok i paketuar i pamjaftueshëm');
    for(const [lotId,take] of plan){
      const lot=by('lots',lotId),before=lotAvail(lot);
      lot.packedNet=Math.max(0,(+lot.packedNet||0)-take);
      lot.availableNet=before-take;lot.net=lot.availableNet;
      coreMove({key:'SHIP:'+id+':'+lot.id,type:'OUT',source:'shipment',sourceId:id,
        lot:lot.id,product:lot.product,warehouse:lot.warehouse,qty:-take,
        unitCost:lot.unitCost,date:x.date,testRunId:x.testRunId});
    }
    x.stockDeducted=true;x.stockDeductedAt=new Date().toISOString();
  }
  x.status=next;return coreResult(true,'',x);
};

console.log('BioBes Paketim v4 loaded');

})();

(function(){

// 2) syncPurchaseDrafts: mbush billable nëse mungon
const origSync = window.syncPurchaseDrafts;
window.syncPurchaseDrafts = function(){
  try{
    let changed=false;
    (state.weighings||[]).filter(w=>w.status==='Konfirmuar').forEach(w=>{
      let f = (purchaseInvoices()||[]).find(x=>x.weighing===w.id);
      if(f){
        if(!(f.billable>0) && w.billable>0){ f.billable = w.billable; changed=true; }
        if(f.billable==null || f.billable==='') { f.billable = w.billable||0; changed=true; }
      } else {
        let nid = (typeof nextPurchaseId==='function'? nextPurchaseId() : nextSeqId('FB',purchaseInvoices(),1,new Date(w.date).getFullYear()));
        purchaseInvoices().push({
          id:nid, weighing:w.id, supplier:w.supplier, product:w.product,
          date:w.date, invoiceDate:w.date, supplierInvoiceNo:'',
          physical:w.net, discount:w.discount||0,
          billable:(+w.billable>0?+w.billable:+(((+w.net||0)*(1-((+w.discount||0)/100))).toFixed(3))),
          price:0, currency:'ALL', exchangeRate:1, vat:0, subtotal:0, vatAmount:0, total:0,
          status:'Draft', attachment:'', attachmentData:'', attachmentType:'',
          isPrepayment:false, allocatedTo:0, prepaymentLinks:[], documentType:'FB pa peshim', description:''
        });
        changed=true;
      }
    });
    if(changed) try{ save(); }catch(e){}
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); }
};
window.purchaseForWeighing = function(weighingId){
  return (purchaseInvoices()||[]).find(f=>f.weighing===weighingId) || null;
};

window.purchaseActions = function(id){
  try{
    let f = purchaseInvoices().find(x=>x.id===id||x.weighing===id);
    if(!f) return toast('Fatura nuk u gjet');
    let q=function(s){ return String(s).replace(/'/g,"\\'"); };
    let isDraft = f.status==='Draft';
    let isConf = f.status==='Konfirmuar';
    let cell=function(fn,ic,ti,sub,extra){ return '<div class="app" onclick="'+fn+'"'+(extra||'')+'><i>'+ic+'</i><b>'+ti+'</b>'+(sub?'<small>'+sub+'</small>':'')+'</div>'; };
